*ALƘALAMINMU ƳANCINMU*


BOOK 2
PAGE 1&2
            ........Idan baku manta ba mun tsaya ne a inda DOCTOR yake kwance a saman bed dinshi Yana tunanin wane Irin kallo famillyn shi zasu mishi ga Nasreen dinshi,ga kuma Mufida wace ta zakulomishi wannan alamarin.......




Yada Ya ga Rana Haka Ya ga dare wurin karfe uku n'a dare ya fito falon dan yana jin kamar hadari  hakan yasa shi fitowa falon dan rufe labulayen dakin da rufe Ko ina ,turus ya ja ya tsaya Yana karema abar kaunar tasa kalo ,duk tabi ta takure wuri Daya alamar sanyin hadarin na damuwar ta „„„


Wani Irin tausayin tane yaji yadarsu a zuciyar shi , ALLAH sarki yasan sabida shine ta kwanta anan dan yasan ko ba ita ce dashi ba Bata kwanan falo ,bargo yadoko ya luluba mata sanan ya rufe KO ina yasa hakan yasa Takara gyara kwanciyar ta ,wani Irin kecewa da Ruwa akayi harda tsawa Wanda yadauka zata tashi Amma shiru kakeji murmushi kawai yayi ya sumba ceta a goshi hade dan aza hanunshi akan dan cikin ta Wanda yake cikin bargo „„„


Dubawa yayi baiga Yarinyar ba hakan yashi nufar dakinta ,wutar dakin yakuna ,yarinyar yagani lulube cikin bargo sai barccin ta takeyi hankali kwance bakin gadon yazauna Yana karema yarinyar kalo sosai yaji wani Rauni yazo mishi abubuwa da dama yashiga tunawa Wanda suka shude ,Kara kalon yarinyar yayi wace take matukar kama dashi sai dai itadin fatar ta tadan yi duhu duhu ,kiss yamata a kumatu hade dagyaramata kwanciyar ta takashe wutar dakin,Ruwan shi yadauka yakoma dakinshi ,al'wala yayi yatada nafilfili sosai yake adu''a da rokon Allah Daya dafamasa akan al'amuran shi ,yakamasa komi cikin sauki ,yajima Yana adu''a sai dayaji Kiran sallah ya fito zai wuce massalaci yadan bubuga kafarta cikin barcci tace “Haba DOCTOR kafadamin mana miye yakedamunka nifah Matar kace”tafada tana jimke hanunshi ,wani Irin tausayin ta yasakeji yasan takwana da damuwar shi ne„„„


Kara dadabata yayi yace “Ke tashi anyi sallah”dakyar ta bude idon ta tana ganinshi ta kauda kanta ko kalonshi batayi ba tamike tashige dakinta ,bayan ta yabi da kalo yasan fushi take dashi sosai Ama zai dawo daga massalaci zaifadamata duk abunda yake faruwa koda hakan zaisa ta tayayan ka naman shi ne ,Abu guda yasani shine komiye zai faru bazai rabu da Ita ba koza'ayi Yaki na uku ne aduniya””””



Duk da Yana fushi da Ita hakan bazaisa yakitada ta yin ibada ba ,har YANZU akarkashin ikon shi take ,doli zai kula da Ita ,part dinta yashiga yatarar ahargitse Wanda bayan shigar ta tayita jifah  da Kayan adakin  ahaka tasha kukanta tana nadamar abunda tayima Mijin nata Wanda Iya Zaman ta dashi Bai taba muzguna mata ba komi yimata yakeyi Yana sauke duk wani hakki da yake kanshi ,adilin Miji Wanda kamar shi sai an tona sai dataji kanta namata ciwo sosai sanan tayi shiru tana sauke ajiyar zuciya dakyar barccin wahala yayi gaba da Ita anan falon ,hakan yasa DOCTOR isko ta kwance saman kafet in falon  Wanda Itama atakure take sabida sanyin Ruwan da'akayi dayake ratsa falon ,ko dar baiji ba a tausayin ta dadaba ta yayi a firgice ta Farka  tana fadun “dan Allah kayi Hakuri bazan saké ba natuba”wata tsawa yabuga mata wada tasa ta dawowa hayacinta ,tsik tayi tana hawaye ,mikewa yayi ,gaba yayi abunsa ,hakan yasata kara fashewa da kuka tashige dakinta tana mai cigaba da rera kukan ta „„„



Nasreen kai tana shiga wanka tayi hade da alwala ta fito Kenan Yarinyar ta tashi bayin tashiga ta Doro alwala kusa da Nasreen takabarta tata sallar suna karewa sukaci gaba da nafil fili Nasreen ce tafara mikewa ganin Haka yarinyar tace “Anty ina kwana ”sosai taji yarinyar ta burgeta Cikin sakin fuska tashafa kanta tace “lafiya klw ,fatan kin tashi lafiya ”murmushi kawai yarinyar tayi ,itakuwa Nasreen kitchen ta wuce dan Dora musu girki yarinyar ma nabiye da Ita abaya ,bayan Sun shigo ne tayimusu Breakfast d'un kosai duk da tasan ba lalle bane suci ganin basa cikin mode mai dadi „„„



Suna Kamala wa tajera su a falo dakin su ka koma suna zuwa toilette tashiga da yarinyar Ita Tamata wanka sosai sanan tashafa mata mai yanzu Kayan da zata sane Babu hakan yasa ta barta da towel inyaso inshi Wanda yakawo ta yazo sai yasiyo mata wasu „„


IRFAN kamar kulum sai kusan karfe bakwai yadawo part d'un Nasreen ya wuce Yana shiga yaji kanshi yadaki hancinsa Wanda sai da ya lumshe ido dakin ta yashiga da Salama Yana ganin yarinyar ta sauko daga kan gadon ta duka har kasa tace “ina kwana uncle”tafada hannun ta Yana makar kata alamar tsoro „



Murmushi yadan Saki ya karaso wurin ta yakama hanun ta yadora ta saman cinyar shi yace“kina jin tsoro nane yaw kuma Nana ”yafada Yana shafa gashin kanta da ya kwanto har gadon bayan ta Wanda Nasreen ta wanke mata shi dama akunce yake ,,,,



Yarinyar nan Daya Kira nana Itama murmushi tayi hade da dariya tace “kayi Hakuri uncle Béa bakai bane kayi ta ma Anty Nafissa fada sanan kuma kasharara ma wanan mai kukan Mari nikuma kanrisgoni da karfi ”shiru yayi Yana sauraron ta yace “to shikenan uncle yayi laifi ayi Hakuri KO ”yafada Yana sakar mata kiss a kumatu ,,,,,


“waya Miki wanka ne ”yatanbaye ta ,“Anty Mai kirki ce tamin tana so na sosai jiyama sai da tasamin cartoon tamin adu''a dazankwanta sann......”bata karasaba ya aza yatsar shi saman bakinshi yace “shittt ya isa hakan kawai mai wayo ,ina Antyn naki ?”yafada Yana kalon fuskar ta „„



Har zatayi magana Nasreen ta shigo dakin tana sharce Ruwan kanta da karamin towel ,kalo Daya tamasa ta sauke kai hakan bakaramin Sosa masa Rai yayi ba bai yi tsamani ba yaji tace “ina kwana ”Shi kanshi yataba ta Nasreen DINSHI tadaban ce duk da tana fushi dashi hakan baisa tayi fushi da gaisheshi ba sannan ta kula da abunda ya kawo Bata Reda tasan KO Ita wacece ba wani son ta da kaunar tane yaji yakara shiga ransa „„


Murmushi kawai tasaki ganin yajuya mishi Baya tana shafa cream dinta „„„



Mikewa yayi ya fice daga d'akin ,dakin shi ya shiga Yana zuwa wanka yayi ya shirya tsaf cikin Kayan Shan iska dan yaw doli yasan akwai jan aiki a gaban shi wurin rarashin Abar kaunar shi bashida yakinin zata yafe mshi Ama zaiyi komi dan Yaga ya rarashe ta „„



Nasreen kai da gangan ta Share sa tayi alkawari ba za ta masa maganar Yarinyar ba har sai idan shine ya mata tana son jin komi tana son jin LABARIN wannan yarinyar shiryawa tayi tsaf cikin wasu Riga da Wanda Wanda suka kamata sosai kasancewar cikin ta yadan fara fitowa yasa rigar ta dage mata sama kadan jikin ta ta feshe da turare ta tumke kanta cikin wata bakar hula dama jins d'un bakin wando ta saka da farar Riga tayi kyau abunta ,kan yarinyar takama ta tumke mata shi tasa mata mayuka masu kamshi wata this_chart d'un rigarta mai dan tsawo ta saka mata aikuwa ta sauko mata har guiwa ,sosai yarinyar ta burgeta ganin tana mata godiya ,hanunta takama suka fito falon bisa kujerar daning dun ta Dora ta Ita ma ta zauna kossan ta zuba musu Bata damu ba da su zo su ci cikin sune ai Ba na ta ba ita dai ta girka suna tsakar ci ya sauko sai kanshi yake zubawa kusa da kujerar ta ya zauna „


Bata kyaleshi ba ta zuba masa abincin tura masa gabansa tayi ta ci gaba da cin nata ,ba zato ta ga ya tsoma hanunsa cikin plat d'un ta gutsuru wa yayi yakai bakinsa ,Bata kulasa ba kamar yanda abun bai mata daɗi ba Share sa tayi taci gaba da ci ,Nana ce ta katse shurun da fadun “ Anty Bana cin Irin wanan girkin Ama wanan Yamin dadi sosai koba Haka ba uncle?”ta fada tana kalon sa,murmushi yayi yace “Eh gaskiya kai yayi dadi sosai ma ta ci aourt ai Anty naki ”ya fada shima Yana sakar mata murmushi , Nasreen ce ta mike ta fara tatatra kwanoni ganin Nana m'a takare ta koma falo shi kuwa Yana ga ci „





Wanda yake cin kawai Tabari wanke wanken ta tayi sosai ta gyara kitchey d'un , DOCTOR kai yaw abun n'a Nasfan d'un nashi ya fara bashi tsoro ,bai ce komi ba shima Yana karewa ya doki plat d'un yakai kictchen ,da kanshi ya wanke plat d'un ganin ta gama wanke wanken kwanonin„„„


Falo ta dawo Nana ce tace “Anty ki kunami. Cartoon nayi kalo”murmushi tasakar mata ,ta kuna mata TV ita kuma takoma dakin ta dan tatare dakin duk da bashi da komi tana tsakar gyara bed d'un ya shigo bata kaleshi ba taci gaba da sha'anin ta „„„


DOCTOR kai ta bayan ta yazo ya rungume ta yana shafa cikin ta yace “wai nikan fushin na minene Matata ta fadamun naji ,gashi ni ban masan laifin dana miki ba yaw duk KO kalona ma bakya son yi ki fadamun laifi n'a sai na bada Hakuri mana ”ya fada Yana wasa da kanshi dai dai wuyanta ,kokarin kwacewa ta shiga yi ganin Yaki sakin ta kuma bazata Iya kwatar kanta ba yasa ta fashewa da kuka sokoko yayi yana kallon ta lalle akwai jan aiki a gaban shi ,bisa gadon ya zauna ya sata a tsakiyar shi yana mai cigaba da shafa cikin ta ,ita kuwa sai kukan ta takeyi tana kokarin kwacewa cikin kwantar da murya yace “Kiyi Haƙuri ,kiyi Haƙuri Baby Na Nine ko to kiyi Haƙuri ba zan saké ba na tuba ki yafewa Mijin ki duk kinbi kinsa n'a Rude dubi yada kike kuka sai kace wani ne ya mutu ,”ya faɗa Yana mai dan goga mata sajen shi a wuyanta „



Shiru ta danyi tana sauraranshi wasu kalamai masu daɗi yake faɗa mata Wanda sirrin su ne Nasreen d'un dake kuka sai gata tana dariya har da sauti ,shima dariya yake yi Wace bai taba Irin ta ba ,sun kai minti uku a hakan sannan yayi dan gyaran Murya kuma jikin shi yayi sanyi sosai yace “Nasreen ?”ya kira sunan ta Wanda zata Iya kirga sau nawa ya kira sunan ta ,hakan ba 'karamin mamaki ya bata saké Kiran sunanta yayi hakan yasa ta cewa “na'am ”ta fada tana Dora hannun ta saman nashi da yake wasa dashi ,hannun ta ya jimke cikin nashi taso juyowa ta kalleshi Ama bai Bata damar hakan ba.



Cikin wata irin Murya dabata taba jin yayi Magana ba yace “da Farko zan baki Hakuri ne domin ni din mai laifi ne a wurinki ,ina so kiyi wa Magana ta kyakyawar fahimta ,Nasan kin san mi ake Kira da kaddara ,Dukan musulmi ya kamata yayi Imani da kaddara wala mai kyau ce ko Mara kyau ,a rayuwa ta ba abunda na tsana kamar zina kamar naga Y'a Macé bata da kamun kai ,Iyaye na ba mazina ta bane haka ma ahalina ina musu kyayawan zato ina son ki sosai Nasreen Dina akan ki nasan minene so,ban ta'ba jin son wata Macé ba a kan ki na fara shi kema sheda ce ,Zan baki wani LABARI Wanda ban san ya zaki Doki maganar ba ban san ya zaki jiba bansan ya duk Wanda yaji maganar zai ji ba Ama kisani ina son ki koma miye zai faru ba zan rabu dake ba ina son ki ,kimin alkawari ba za ki gujeni ba duk rintsi duk wuya”ya faɗa cikin Rauni Wanda muryashi har makar kata take , Nasreen kai yanzu ta fara tsorata da al'amarin Mijin nata yanzu tsoro ne ya shige ta sosai tana jin a ranta koma minene yake damun Mijin nata ba k'aramin al'amari bane ,Amma tana fatan koma minene ba zai sa ta guje masa ba ,tana tsananin kaunar sa fiyeda da komi da ta malaka a yanzu „”




Shiru tayi hakan yasa shi cigaba da faɗin .......✍️



🖊️MADOCTOR IRFAN🥼


🖊️MAMAN FAUZAN CE🖊️

_Marubuta Masu Aiki Da Fasaha Cike Da Zallan Tsagwaron Amana,Faɗakarwa Game Da Nishaɗantarwa Tare Da Wa'azantarwa,Alƙalaminmu 'Yancinmu_


Book 2
Page 3&4
       ........✍️“kamar yanda kika sani nayi karatuna a turai ,Mata ba sa gabana kamar yanda nama Ammi na alkawari ba zan yi zina ba, kamar yanda na kyamace ta ,na hadu  da Samir kuma kamar yanda kowa ya sani mutumin kirki ne ,ya dauke ni tamkar kani ,tamkar jini daya ,a lokacin ba na son yin girki ba wai dan ban iya ba a'a kiwa nake ji sai dai Samir dun ya dafa ko da kuwa yunwa zata kasheni ba zan dafa ba ,a lokacin da ya fara exam ba shiga tashin hankli sabida ba na iya girki duk da kuwa yanda nake jin yunwar ,kasancewar turai ba lalle bane kisamu masu sai da abinci amma  Mu munyi sa'a a lokacin kasancewar akwai wata yarinyar a cikin faculté dunmu kuma ibo ce ma'ana yare ce a sunan ta Élisabeth iyayen ta yan kasar Canada ne ,Mahaifiyar ta sunan ta Emeka ,sai Mahifin ta Mr Daniel 
Y'a yan su biyu Élisabeth ce baba mai shekaru goma sha takwas ,sai kanan ta John ,Sun baro kasar suka komo France kasancewar baban ta embrasse na Niger da France, daga baya aka sauke shi sabida kamashi da laifufuka da dama ,yafadi rashin lafiya ta dalilin haka,hakan yasa duk wasu kaddarorin shi suka saida su dan yimishi magani ahaka komi yakare mafarin suka fara tallah tuwo anan bakin makarantar  Mu,sosai abinci yake da dadi ama bai kai na mata na ba ”ya fada yana sunsunar wuyan ta „„„

Shiru tayi hakan yasa shi cigaba da fadun “lokacin da Samir yake exam na fara FITA neman abinci ,ban taba lura da akwai mai abinci a wurin ,kodan ba shi nake fita nema ba oho ,a lokacin da na fita na gan ta ita da mahaifiyar ta suna zuzubama clients dinsu abinci ,zama nayi ba tare da na kallesu ba ,sabida kalo ba dabi'a ta bace ,Élisabeth ce ta tanbayeni abunda nake so tare da yimini wani dan ban zan kallo irin na yan duniya yinayi kamar ban ganta ba ,na fada mata abunda nake so ba tare da bata lokaci ba ta kawomin ,tanayi tana kwarkwasa ita à la doli zata burgeni ,kauda kaina nayi daga kalon ta ,duk abunda take ina ankare da ita ama na share ,ina karewa na bata kudin ta cemin tayi na bari ,ko kalo bata isheni ba na watsa mata kudin nayi gaba abuna, ahaka nake tafiya safe da marece ina so abinci sabida abinci yayi dadi ba karya ,kuma har inasema Samir nabiya masa dashi Wanda shima ya yaba dashi ,Élisabeth yarinya ce mai kamun kai sabida idn kaganta a faculté bata wa kowa magana ko kawa batada bare maza ,wata rana ina karatu a wurin da'aka tanada dan hutun dalibai sai ji nayi ance hai duk da naji murya mace ban dago ba naci gaba da karatu na ,cikin yaren french tacemin “please kayi hakuri ba wani Abu bane yakawoni nazo ne ka koyamin Mathématiques sabida tana bani wahala ,karatu kawai nace ”tafada tana zama kusa dani ,dagowa nayi na karemata kalo tundaga sama har kasa Élisabeth ba fara bace baka ce ama ba sosai ba ,batada muni ,sanan doguwace sanye take cikin wani gajeren siket da this-chirt mara hannu tasaka gashin doki akanta sai takalman ta masu tsini sosai ,murmushi tasakar min ,ban maida mata ba nace “shikenan ”naci gaba da duba littafin danake dubawa „„„


Kasancewar tace karatu nikuma ina shiri da duk mai son karatu ,cikin karamar murya tace min “kayi hakuri kafadamun yaushe zamu fara kuma a ina ”ban sake kalon ta ba nace “gobe da karfe hudu ,sanan anan wurin”a lokacin duk nafahimci itace mai abinci nan ama ban numa ta komi ba ,ahaka gobe mukahadu na fahimtar da ita abunda bata fahimta ba „„


Daga baya bayan ma Samir yakare exam dun ban fasa zuwa sayen abincin ba har mukanje da Samir kuma Mdm Emeka tasaba damu sosai ,tana da fada bare akn kudi batada mutunci duba dayanda takema wasu daliban Idan sunje cin bashi „„„

Élisabeth tana jikin daukar hankali hakn yasa maza suke bibiyar ta wasu da lalata wasu kuma tsakani da Allah suke son ta ,bata kulasu kasancewar tana son yin karatu sosai kuma itama médecin take karanta „„„


Ahaka take sakani magana kobana son yi nakan yi hardai mukadan saba da ita har Samir kasancewar turai ne babu wani da rayuwar wani ,yasa Har dakin mu takankai mana abinci ,takan saka matsatsun kaya Wanda shiyakejan hankalin Maza kan ta „„„


So tari takan tarar dani ina karatu alkur'ani ta kan saurare ni ,idan nakare na juyo gare ta sai naga jikin ta yayi sanyi nakan tanbayeta lafiya ama sai tace min klw ,ahaka kuma komi mukeyi idan lokacin sallah yayi mukan bari muje zuwa sallah ,nakan kwatanta mata tadaina saka matsatsun kaya hakan yasa tadaina sawa idan kuma tasa takan saka abaya daga sama kamar yanda kike naki style dun wani lokaci, takn doki magana ta serious hakan yasa nake respecting d'un ta „


Shakuwa sosai tashiga tsakanin mu ,Wanda ni dokar yar Uwa nake mata ,itakuma Ashe tamaida shakuwar soyaya ,ban fahimci so na takeyi ba atunani na shakuwa ce ,wata rana muna zaune ina koyamata assigment kawai sai ji nayi ta furta tana sona „


Atunanina wasa takeyi hakan yasa nacemata tabar wanan maganar wasan ,nan take naga tana hawaye tabani tausayi sosai sabida na yarda har cikin zuciyar ta gaskiya ne ,hakuri na bata ama ta kwatanta min ita bazata iya rayuwa da wani ba matukar bani bane ,nayi kokarin fahimtar da ita ama takasa fahimta, ta tafi tana kuka sam banji dadin hakan ba „


Kwana biyu nadaina ganin ta ,nashiga tashin hankali sabida nasaba da ita sosai hakan yasa ni binta har gidan su nabata hakuri ,akan addinin mu badaya ba sanan kuma shi baishirya soyaya yanzu ba lokaci kawai zan bata miki „„„


Haka ta nunamin tahakura ,dama ina fama da matasalar ciwon mara ,Wanda itama tasan da hakanan kuma idan yatasar min wataran har likita Samir kekaini 


Wata Rana Ranar da bazan manta da ita ba ,Ranar lalacewat komi ,Ranar da kaddara ta giftamin wace har yanzu ina nadamar ta ,kaddara ,kaddara babu mai iya kauce mata ,na rungume ta hannu bibiyu sai dai bansan ku yazuku karbeta ba ,Nayi imani da tawa kaddara duk da ta shari ce ama a ta alkairi nake kalon ta ,Ranar litinin da dadare a lokacin Samir bayanan yatafi library dan yin karatu kuma idan yaje yakan kai karfe uku ko hudu bai dawo ba a Ranar Marata ta sakani a gaba da ciwo ,sosai nashiga tashin hankali dan shine karo na biyu da tamin zafi kamar hakanan har so biyu „„„


Nikadai sai mutsu mutsu nake adaki zufah ce ke karyomun tako ina jinke kamar za'aciremin Mara ta ,a lokacin Élisabeth tazo dakinmu ganin dagani har Samir d'un babu Wanda yaje neman abinci hakan yasakata biyomu da abincin ,dama hakan takan faru wani lokaci zata dauka ko karatune yamuna yawa bamuje ba sai takawo mana ,shune yaw ma takawo kuma ta isheni cikin wanan halin ,tashiga cikin tashin hankali sosai jikin ta ta rungumo ni Wanda nayi kokarin kwacewa ama nakasa sabida ba wani karfi ne dani ba kuka take sosai tana Kiran sunana nikuwa alokacin duk ina jin ta nakasa ansa mata dagudu ta fita daga dakin bata jima ba tadawo doke da wani kwalbar magani kaina tadago ta jijiga maganin tabani sosai nasha sabida nidai kawai naji saukin abunda nakeji kawai nakeso ba tare da wani tunani ba na kafa kai nasha .....✍️

BOOK 2
PAGE 5&6
            .......✍️A maimakon idan na sha maganin naji sauki ji nayi mara ta ta 'kara hargitsewa ji nake idan har banyi sex ba zan iya mutuwa „

Na 'kara shiga cikin tashin hankali Nasreen ban ma san me nakeyi ba ,Élisabeth naji tana fadin “kayi hakuri ina son ka, ina son jin dadin da yake tare da kai Na-san zaka bada pawer bisa gado ,na so ka Aure ni amma taurin kan ka ya hanaka hakan kai ga ustaz ko ,hmm kayi hakuri ,Nasreen duk ina jin abunda take fada nakasa fahimtar ina zancen ta ya dosa a lokacin „


Ina gani tayi tsirara a gabana ita ce ta shiga sarafa ni yanda take so ,duk ina jin abunda take min amma nakasa hanata ,ban san ya'akyi na farmata ba sai tashi nayi na ganta kwance saman gado na tayi kuka sosai dan har hawaye sun bushe a fuskar ta ,wani irin zabura nayi ganina atube nan take abubuwan da suka faru suka dawomin cikin kaina,wani irin sarawa kaina yayi ,wani irin zabura nayi ina girgiza kaina sabida ba 'karamin tashin hankali na shiga ba „



Ruwa na doko mai sanyi na bulbulamata aikuwa ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sabida ga dukan almu suma tayi ,kuka ta fashe dashi tana fadun na yafe mata ta cutar dani kuma ta cuci kanta laifinta ne dan Allah nayi hakuri „


A lokacin Nama kasa fahimtar zancan ta cikin tsanani bacin Rai da zuciya Wanda ban san ina dashi ba na fisgota ta fado daga kan saman gadon ,ban tsaya wata wata ba na saka kaya na belt dun jikina na shiga tsula mata duk da kuwa Na-san da ciwo a jikin ta kuma ina da yakini a budurce na sameta Ganin yanda jini ya bata bed din sosai na shiga tsula mata shi „


Samir ne ya shigo ya rike belt din hade da hankadani saman gadon yana min wani irin wulkantacen kallo ,wani irin kallo yake min na kyama ,hade da ashe haka kake ban sani ba ,na shiga tashin hankali ganin irin kallon da Samir d'in yake min Wanda na gwamaci mutuwa ta a lokacin idan har shi Samir din da nake doka dan uwa ya min haka waye ba zai min ba ,ba na son irin kallon da yakemin ,ba na son ganin abunda nake karanta a cikin idonun shi ,shi din ya wuce amini sai dai dan Uwa,ina tsananin kaunar shi ya dokeni tamkar jinin sa „


Cikin wata irin murya da ban san shi da ita ba yace “kan na kirga goma kin bar dakin nan ”ya fada yana mai juya baya dan bata damar saka kayanta „


Ba ita kadai ba nikaran kaina na tsorata da ganin yanayinshi da Sauri ta saka kayanta tana dingirisa ta fuce daga dakin ,kasancewar karfe uku ne zuwa hudu daidai lokacin dama Samir din yake dawowa shine ya tarar da abunda yake faruwa „


Dama yau tun karfe biyu ya dawo yana shiga ya tarar da su cikin halin jima'i sosai ya shiga cikin tashin hankali Wanda saura kadan ya zube ,kasancewar bama hayacinmu bamu masan ya shigo ba ashe komawa baya yayi yanata duk addu'ar da ta shigo bakinshi ,bakin kofar dakin ya zauna dabass ya shiga rere kuka kamar k'aramin yaro ,yayi kuka mara adadi da taimakon addu'ar ya daina kuka a haka yaji ina zabga mata belt shine ya shigo „


Da gudu tabar dakin shi kuwa ko kallona baiyi ba sai kokarin shiga toilet da yake yi Samir dina yana fushi dani ina zan saka kaina ji nayi na tsani duniya gabadaya kowa da komai da yake cikin ta „„


Ganin zai shige yasa ni binshi da gudu na rungume shi ta baya ina kuka kamar Rai na zai fita “Kayi hakuri karkayi fushi dani ,kar ka tsaneni Samir kai kadai nake kallo naji dadi a matsayin Uwa da uba bani da kowa sai kai kaine majin ginata kar ka kasa yarda da kaddara dan uwanka Abokin ka kamin Alkawari zaka kasance tare da ni a kowane hali ,ka rungumi kaddarata kamar yanda mu'umini yake imani da kaddara sa ta alkairi ce ko ta sharri ,wallahi ni ba Mazina ci ba ne ka yarda da ni dan Uwa na ,ban taba aikata Zina ba sabida Na-san bashi ce ,na yi ma Ammi na Alkawarin ba zan yi ba ,amma kasani babu Wanda yake wuce kaddara sa ”na fada ina kara rungume shi sosai ina kuka ,shima kukan ya fashe dashi ya juyo ya rungumeni yana fadun “Nasani ,Nasani dan Uwa na ba mazina ci ba ne baya shiga gonar da bata shi ba mai kare hakkin Mata ne ,na Rungumi kaddara ka Kanina ina fatan Allah ya yafe maka, yasa wannan ya zama Izina a gareka ”ya fada yana bubuga bayana hade da rungemi gam kamar wani ne zai kwace masa ni „


Hakika duk yanda zan gaya miki ina kaunar Samir Abun ya wuce nan bana hada kowa dashi na yarda soyayar da yake min ta gaskiya ce shi din masoyin asali ne „


Bayan mun sha kukan mu da kanshi ya gyara dakin sanan ya hadamin Ruwan wanka sabida komi nakasa yi ,bana iya yin komi Wata irin kunyar shi nakeji ya kamani turmi da tabarya Samir dina ,wanka nayi sosai hade da alwala na saka jalabiya tare mukayi nafilfili hade da Rokon Allah ya gafarta mini kura kurai na ,sosai abun ya shigeni a Ranar ba muje massalci ba a gida mukayi sallah da gani har shi „„


Safiya nayi wurin karfe takwas yacemin muje banyi musu ba namishi abaya bamu zarce ko inaba sai gidan su Élisabeth Wace takoma gida tana kuka wiwi sosai mahaifiyar ta tashiga tashin hankali bare mahaifin ta dayeke kwance babu lafiya ,ta fahimci abunda yafaru da yar ta hakan yasa ta sheke wuyan ta tana fadun “kafadamun wane shege ne yaketama haddin ka Élisabeth kabani kunya ,ashe duk kamun kanka na banza ne wo kaji kunya zaka fadamun wanene ko sai nakasheka ”tafada tana kokarin doko yuka ,da gudu taje wuru  mahaifin ta tabuya wanda baya um bare um um„

Shakota tayi kasancewar dama kinsan yare da karfi kuma ga Mdm Emeka wata katuwa ce ,dukan ta ta shiga yi ganin ba sarki sai Allah yasa tafadi gaskiya ama bata ce tabani wani Abu nasha ba ,“eh Jésus oh Jesus aide moi”tafada tana dora hanu akai hade da kai dakawo wa ,kuka Mdm Emeka tashiga yi sosai kanin Élisabeth jhon shine mai rarashin ta Mdm Emeka ta matsu Safiya tayi taje makarantar tasu tashaida abunda yafaru Wanda idan aka kama dalibai da wanan kusu y'a yan wanene za'akoresu „


Muna zuwa muka tarara tasaka yar ta agaba zasuje makarantar, tana ganina tayi kaina Samir ne yadakatar da ita ganin tana kokarin cakumo ni cikin turanci take fadun “dan iskan yaro dai kayi nadamar abunda ka aikatamin ya ta zakayi wa fyade yaw zankai ka wurin shugabar makarantar tasu ”tafada tana zage zage dakayar Samir yasamu tasaurare shi zama mukayi Wanda ni alokacin wani irin tafarfasa zuciya ta takeyi sosai jiranake kawai takawo gareni na hanbareta ,cikin tausasa lafazi Sam yace “da Farko ina mai bamu hakuri muduka sabida abunda yafaru hakika yarinyar ki kuruciya ce take damun ta ,ke kikeda mace antaka mata budurcin ta Wanda bazata saké samun wani original kamar saba sabida shi wanan original ne ma'ana na ubangiji ne ,kuyi hakuri abunda yafaru kaddara ce mu rungume ta hannu bibiyu ,kuyi hakuri zanbaku Million goma akan fansar budurcin ta duk da nasan dayafi haka ama sabida ragemuku radadi zanyi haka IRFAN kanina ne zanyi komi sabida shi sabida haka zanbaku kudin akashe maganar daganan ”yafada kai tsaye yana kalon Mdm Emeka daketa zabgamin harara nikuma hankali na gabadaya yana ga Samir dayace zaibasu Millions goma akan wane dalili bashida hujjar basu wanan kudin do-min yar su ita tajawa kan ta „


Cikin fada Mdm Emeka tace “karya kakeyi haussa dama ku yaran haussa macuta ne azzalumi ,baka isa ba ka ketawa y'a ta haddin ta sanan abani wani millions dun banza bana so sai nayi karara sa amakarantar ku ”
Tafada tana kunfar baki „


Wani kalo Samir ya watsa mata yace “fine idan bazaki karba ba Ruwan kine inabinki da lalama ne sabida tausayin yar ki ama ke ko ajikin ki ko ,to kije ki kai kara mugani idan baki sani ba yar taki itace tasaka masa abun kara sha'awa har ya farmata dama tana neman yanda zata cima burin shi akan ta ina da hujjoji jus dun data basa yasha nagama bincike akanshi Nagano maganin kara sha'awa ne tabasa ki tanbayi yar ta ki kuma millions goma dun nafasa ba yarwa ”yafada cikin fushi „


Mdm Emeka kuwa hankali ta tamaida kan yar tata tana tana fadun “elisabet kafadamun abunda wanan banza haussa yake céwa karya ne ”


Élisabeth kai ta rude sosai tamanta ashe ba'ita kadai bace Doctor ,cikin kuka tashiga fadama mahaifiyar ta gaskiya Wanda tafashe da kuka tana fadun “shikenan ka cuceni ka cuceni wayo ni Emaka ”tafada tana ta kururwar ta sosai suka shiga cikin tashin hankali „


Samir kai hanuna na yakama muka koma dakinmu sosai naji zuciya ta ta tsinke da lamarun nashi ashe haka yake kaunata  ni kuwa Wace irin kauna Samir yakemin ne ,rungume shi nasakeyi ina kuka hade da rokon ya yafemini ,cikin bacin Rai yace “idan kasake kuka akan wanan abun kataba bakada wani dan Uwa waishi Sam”ahaka nashare hawaye na ina rokon Allah daya karbi tubana ya yafemin kurakuraina „


Kulum ina cikin istigfari da Rokon Allah hade da tunanin halin da dangina zasu shiga idan sukaji abunda na aikata duk da kaddara ce ama ba kowa ba zai fahimci hakan ,


Wata biyu da yin haka Ashe Élisabeth tana da shigar ciki Wanda sai dayakai wata biyu sanan Mahaifiyar ta ta ankara muna zaune da Samir muna karatu tashigo cikin tashin hankali tana fadun “shikena  kamata ciki ,cikin shege ne da ita ,to bara kaji baza'a haife shi ba shege agida na karya ne ” rude kofar Samir yayi ganin mutane zasu iya jinmu sosai shima tashin haknli yabaya na akan fuskar shi ,niikuwa IRFAN mutuwar zaune nayi sabida shock da nashiga ciki cikin shege kuma nawa kai na nashiga girgizawa yanzu ne nakara shiga tashin hankali cikin shege koma miye zai faru bazan bari ahaifi wanan cikin ba „


Samir kai cikin tashin  hankali yace “please kuyi hakuri kibamu kwana biyu zamu nemo mafita ama kma minen kar kuyarda kuzubar da cikin nan idan kuma kuka zubar dashi to ina mai tabatar muku zakushiga cikin tashin hankali ”yafada yana nunamata kofa ,da Sauri tabar kofar „


Shikuwa dabass yazuna akasa yashiga nanata kalmar shahada hade de inallilahi wa'ina illaihi raji'un sosai tashin hankali yabayana afuskar shi „


Ni kuwa bansaman wane irin tunani yakamata nayi ba Nida dan shege batare da Aure ba Ammi nace tafadamin A rai bansan sanda nasfashe da kuka ba ina fadun “shikenan Ammi na duk wata yarda dakikemin Na-san takare na cuceku na cucu kaina ” da gudu nayi wurin Samir ina fadun “Samir kafadamun ya zanyi bazan bari ahaifi wanan cikin ba Ammi na zata mutu da bakin ciki na katashi muje azubar dashi ”nafada ina kama hanunun shi ,ba zato naji Mari a kuncena.....✍️

          ........✍️ Tass kake ji ya daukeni da Marin da yasa na ga hazo cak na tsaya ina kallonshi ,shima kallona yakeyi irin kallon Ashe baka da hankali ......

“ina maka kallon mai hankali ashe har yanzu da sauran ka Irfan kun yi wa ubangiji laifi duk da kaddara ce amma kake so ka'kara wani laifin ,to bari kaji na fada maka ko da wasa kada ka kuskura a zubda cikin nan ,za'a haife shi kamar ko wane ɗa a duniya ”daga haka ya banko kofar ya tafi abunshi „


Na shiga tashin hankali ganin Samir yana fushi da ni ,har wunin bai dawo ba sai cikin dare ya zo Wanda nakasa barcci ,bai min magana ba yahaye gadon a ranar ko face dina baya kallo ,bayan muna kallon juna bacci yake dokemu wataran ma har akan gadon shi nake bacci duk da kuwa gadon mutum daya ne amma a haka muke matso matso mu kwanta tare „


Juyi kawai nake tayi haka shima ina ganin ba barcci yakeyi ba ,saukowa yayi yace min muje muyi alwa'ala muyi nafil fili a haka muka kwana kowa da abunda yake sakawa a ranshi „


Da safe mukaje gidansu Élisabeth kuma munyi fahimtar juna wanda ita da Samir ne sukayi maganar su akan tayi hakuri a haife wanan yaron kuma da gargadin Idan wani Abu ya samu cikin zata gane kuranta tsoro sosai ya bata kuma ta tsoratan ,ta bangaren Élisabeth kuwa ba karamin farin cikin faruwar wannan al'amarin tayi ba sosai take son cikin bare kuma nawa ,Samir da kanshi ya doki nauyin ciyar da ita da abunda ke cikin ta har zuwa haihuwa „„


A haka muka shirya ban san da wane baki zan masa godiya ba yamin abunda ko dan uwana ba zai min ba „


Ta cigaba da Rainon cikin ta mu kuma muka ci gaba da karatunmu a haka kuma duk lokaci zuwa lokaci Samir ya kanje dubata ya mata duk wasu gwaje gwaje dan duba lafiyar su ita da jaririn ,banta zuwa dubata ba ko sau daya domin duk lokacin da na ga fuskar ta nakan ji kamar naya yanka namanta ,a haka har lokacin haihuwa tayi „


Wata rana ta tashi da nakuda dama suna da number Samir kiranshi sukayi bai ko fadamun ba ya tafi da kanshi yakai su hospital ba'a juma ba suka kai carte dinshi ya nuna na zama likita ,ai da Sauri suka karbeta ba ajima ba da shiga da ita dakin haihuwa ta cikin ikon ubangiji ta haihu sosai Samir yaji dadi har da sujada ya nuna tsananin murna shi na dota akayi aka mikomishi ita ,yana kallon ta sai da hawaye suka zubo mishi “tabarakallah ”ya fada yana kai mata kiss a goshi sak Irfan tayo sai dai daka ganta zakasan bazatayi farin shi ba duhun mahaifiyar ta ta doko sosai yayi mata addu'a hade da tauna dabino da Ruwan zam zam ya bata „


Sai motsi take da baki kyakkawar yarinya Mdm Emeka ya bawa amma ko kallon yarinyar batayi ba sai ma tanbaya da takeyi y'a lafiyar yar tata take ciki „


Sosai yayi mamaki amma bai nuna mata ba  ya mika wa  Nos d'in ita sai a lokacin ya kira ni yace nazo da gaugawa ban bata lokaci ba naje ina zuwa ya rungumeni yana fadun “ina fatan ubangiji ya yafemaka kurakuran ka kayi murna dan uwana mun samu karuwar y'a mace ”ya fada yana mai washe haƙoran shi sosai cak ya shige ni ,murmushi na kakalo hanun na yaja har zuwa inda babyn take kwance cikin towel dinta da kayan ta na sanyi dauko ta yayi ya mikomin yace “karbi jininka ce wata kila wannan ita ce farin cikinka ,ka so ta kamar yanda zaka so sauran y'ayan ka ,kayi mata hudu ba da sunan da kafi so ”ya fada yana gyara mata hanun rigar ta yakai mata kiss „

Ni kuwa tunda na dora ido na akan yarinyar naji babu wani Abu da nake so sama da ita a duniya a yanzu ,ban san lokacin da hawaye suka zubomin sunbatar ta nayi Samir din namaida ma yarinyar nace “Allah ya'albarkaci Rayuwar ta Allah yasa mai hakuri ce da juriya mai addini Samir kai ne Mahaifin Wanan yarinyar ko bana raye nasan kai mai iya kulamin da ita ne na baka ita duniya da lahira ka zaba mata sunan daya maka ”na fada ina kallo shi „


Wata irin runguma ya sake yi min cikin hawaye yace “hakika ba'a taba yimin kyautar da ta burgeni ba kamar wannan ta sakani farin ciki kamar wannan ,nagode dan Uwa daga yaw ni ne mahaifin Nana ,Na saka mata Nana firdausi Sunan hajiyata ne Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarki ”cewar Samir din yana wasa da dan yatsun hanunta „


Daga haka nakamo hanya ban sake yi mishi magana ba ina zuwa na shige toilet nayi kuka na sosai kuka ne na abubuwa da yawa Wanda ni kadai nasan abunda nake ji A rai na „


Nayi kuka sai da nakoshi na dauro al'wala na fara nafil fili ina Rokon Allah yagafartin Zunubai na tunda abun nan yaf aru ban taba barcci ba nayi kuka a fili kuma nayi a zuciya „


Da dare ina zaune da littafin fikhu na ga Samir ya shigo hanun shi rike da yarinyar ,ba karamin mamaki nasha ba amma ban masa magana ba na share sa ,sanin yanzu shine papan yarinyar ,bi sa gadon shi ya dorata cikin murya dake nuna ranshi a bace yake yace “ka dubamin ita zanje na dawo dan Allah nace ”yana mai gama hanayen sa biyu almar roko ,mamaki sosai ya bani amma kai kawai na daga mishi ,ina zaune daga gadona ina kallon yarinyar Wace sosai take kama da ni ,ganin ta fara kuka gashi har karfe goma sha daya na dare yasani daukar ta ina jijigata duk da bani da yakinin zatayi shuru sosai ta bani tausayi naji wata irin kaunar ta har nake ji kamar nadawa da ita haka „


Jijigata nakeyi aikuwa dip ta dauke kukan ta tayi lum a kirjina ,sai kusan sha biyu yazo da kaya Niki Niki yana zuwa bai min magana ba ya karbeta yana fadun “sannu Mama na nasan kin sha kuka ko ,kiyi hakuri Papa yayi laifi ba zan karaba shiiit”yafada yana jijigata dan matsalar yara da sangen da ya siyo mata ya'aza gefen gadon shi ciki ya sakata ,Wace barccin ta takeyi hankali kwance „


Madara yakada mata koda zata tashi toilet ya shiga hakan yasani Budé akwati manga biyu da ya siyo Wanda bakomi babe aciki sai kayan yara Wanda kalo daya zaka musu ka fahimci masu tsada ne ,sauran kayan kuma wani pempes dun yara ne da kayan bukatar su da Sauri na rufe kayan nakoma mazauni na „



Shikuwa yana fitowa saida yasake lekata yaga har yanzu barccin ta takeyi hankali kwance ,jalabiyar sa yasaka kawai saiji mukayi anbanko kofar anshigo Wanda bakowa bace sai Élisabeth durkeshe tayi gaban Samir tana kuka tace “na rokeka kar karaba ni da y'a ta kabarni naji dumin jikin ta taji nawa ,kubarmin y'a ta na shayar da ita ,ina son abata kubani y'a ta ”tafada tana kuka wiwi „


Cikin kalon rainin wayo Samir yace “hmm babu wanda zai rabaki da ita ,sai mahaifiyar ki tace bazaki shayar da yar musulmi ba dan haka Nana zata rayu cikin muslmi rainon musulmi tatashi cikin addinin ta da kaunar a halin ta koba ke zata rayu zata rayu insha Allah ”

Nikan sokoko nayi nakasa fahimtar inda zancen su yadosa ,sai ganin ta nayi durkushe agaba na tana fadun “Dan Allah Irfan kayimin Rai kubarni na shayar da y'a ta idan yaso daga baya ku karbeta ”karo na farko kenan data bani tausayi ama ni ba wani taimako da zan mata hankadeta nayi daga kafafuna nayi kwanciya ta ,wurin Sam takoma wanda yake ta jijiga Nana Sabida kukan Mahaifiyar tata yatashe ta ,cikin tsawa wanda yasa yarinyar kara firgita yace “kificemin daga daki Idan bahaka ba wallahi zaki gane kuranki”bansan lokacin datamike ba sai jinayi ta fice daga dakin tana kuka „


Shikuwa jijiga ta yakeyi ama taki shiru madara da yakada mata yashiga bata ama sam taki sha ,babu yanda baiyi ba ama taki karba „


Da kyar tayi shiru har nufashin ta yana dokewa nidai inajinsu sabida ba barcci nakeyi ba „„„



Sosai naji tausayin su daga shi har ita sai kusan asuba bacci yayi awan gaba damu ,kukan tane ma yatashemu da Sauri muka Mike ni nayi toilettes shikuma yashiga jijigata kasancewar mun makara har karfe takwas a gaugauce nayi al'wala nayi sallah ,shikuwa sai aikin rarashi yakeyi ganin lokaci natafiya nace yabani ita yaje yayi sallah ,baiyi musu ba yaban ina karbata tayi shiru tana cila kafafun ta da hanayen ta kamar dai yanda jarirai sukeyi „


Shima sallah yayi yana karewa takarbeta duka kayan daya hada naga yatatara wasu mutum biyu ne naga sun doki kayan shi kuma yana rike da ita yace “muje ”nsan dani yake hakan yasani binshi abaya ,bamu zarce ko inaba sai gidan wata matar abokin shi Samir dun wanda abokai ne sosai shida Sam nasan shi sabida suna yawan ziyar ta juna kuma shima doctorat yake karanta „


Ama shida Auren shi shekaru biyu kenan da Auren shi shida Nafissa itama yar damagaram ce sai dai shi Hafiz dun wato abokin Samir dan Maradi ne tare sukatashi tun yarinta har girmansu abukan kut da kut ne „



Matar shi satin ta daya da haihuwa yaw yayin da Nana Takeda kwana daya kinga kenan akwai tseraya sati daya a tsakanin su „


Muna zuwa Samir yakira shi awaya ba'ajuma ba yazo da fara'ar shi yakarbemu yana zolaya ta da yaro  shi yasan abunda bana so kenan ko Samir mukan yi fada dashi wani lokacin idan yana cemin yaro murmushi kawai nayi suka shiga suna gaba ina binsu abaya „


Wani katafaren falo mukashiga ba laifi kan gidan idan kaganshi zaka san bana talakawa bane kasancewar mahaifin Hafiz mai kudi ne kuma Hafiz dun shi yace amishi Aure ganin yana neman Rasa Nafissa wce suka kwashe shekaru biyar suna soyaya ,shine mahaifin shi yamasa Aure sabida iyaye. Nafissa sunce matukar bata fito da miji ba zasu mata Aure mafarin kenan sukayi Auren ......



Ruwa yasa mai aikin shi takawo mana da jus Nana yakarba yana fadun “Masha Allah shi is beautiful my Daughter Allah ya albarkaci Rayuwar ta yafada yana kai mata kiss „


Wata doguwar mace naga tana saukowa daga latatakalen benan Wace taci wanka cikin wata bakar gizner dogwa ce ,fara kyakyawa ce sosai akala bazata wuce shekaru ashirin da uku ba zuwa da hudu ,Nafissa kenan matar Hafiz da fara'ar ta takarbemu tana mana sannu da zuwa sosai mukaji dadin tarba da tamuna Hafiz ne yayi gyaran murya sanan yace „da farko dai ina jajanta wanan al'amarin duka bawa baya wuce kaddara sa abunda yafaru kaddara ne kuma muna fatan Allah ubangiji ya gafarta muku sanan kamar yanda nama aboki na Samir alkawarin rike Nana zan riketa tamkar yar da nahaifa Dan Samir ya wuce hakanan ,zan riketa da Amana dani da mai daki na kamar yanda tamin alkawarin kula da ita bayan namata bayanin komi ,mun karbi Nana hannu bibiyu insha Allah ko bahaka ba uwar gida ”yafada da fara'ar sa yana kalon matar tashi ,karba Nana tayi da murmushi akan fuskar ta tace “insha Allah mun muku alkawarin kula da ita kamar Yarmu Sa'adiya Allah yabamu ikon rike amana ”rungume shi Samir yayi yana godiya sosai yake musu godiya “haba Samir kadaina wallahi kasan yanzu zamu bata ko ”cewar Hafiz sabida godiyar sa Sam dun kemasa tayi yawa ,nikuwa shiru nayi ganin Samir na harara ta yasa nima nayi godiya kayan damukazo dasu yasa aka jibge nan take ya rubuta million biyu yabawa Nafissa yace “Mdm gashi duk wata zan rika turo millions biyu bawai ina nufin biyanki zanyi ba a'aa kodaya yaba kyauta tukuci ne kuma akwai bukatar wasu abubuwa nata sabida haka dan Allah kikarba ”bata so karba ba ama jin yace dan Allah yasa ta karba hade dayimasa godiya kuma ta rokeshi kar yace zai rika turo kudin zasuyima Nana komi yaji dadi sosai ama duk da haka zai rika turo musu kudin sama taje da Nana Wace tayi shiru tana sauraren mu „


Nono ta bata ama taki karba da dubara ma tasha Madara shine tafada muna kuma Samir yace bakomi a rika bata Madara munyi godiya sosai konace Samir yayi godiya sosai ,sanan mukabar gidn muka koma hostel yafadamun cewar mahaifiyar Élisabeth ce tace yar ta bazata shayar da dan Musulmi ba idan kuma ta shayar sai ta tsine mata ,itakuwa Élisabeth dun ko ajikin ta tana son yar ta ama tanaji tana gani ta rabu da ita „ 

Kwanaki na tafiya satutuka na shudewa kamar yanda shekaru ke tafiya har yau da Allah yasa su Nana sukayi shekaru biyu hudu mukuma mukaci gaba da karatun mu duk cikin wadanan shekarun Samir yakanje yaga lafiyar Nana ,Wace masha Allah ta girma tayi wayo sosai ,yarinya ce mai hankali da nutsuwa ita da sa'adiya komi nasu iri daya ne sun taso kamar tawaye ,kuma kamar yanda yayi alkawarin tura kudin haka yake turasu a lokacin da zasu shiga scool shida kanshi yaje dasu su biyu makarantar y'a yan masu kudi yasaka su dakudinshi baiyi banbanci ba ,yaran suna kaunar shi sosai dan Papa suke kiranshi ,shikuma Hafiza sukance masa Dady ne Nafissa kuma Momy sosai Nafissa take kulada Nana kamar yar ta bata nuna banbanci ,bana zuwa saida Samir yamin fada nake tafiya dubota kuma nakan jata ajiki sosai dan ina dokar su naje dasu wuraren shakatawa sosai muka shaku ama sunfi shakuwa da Samir d'à dadyn su „


Suna kirana Uncle Nana tana da kwazo a scool fiyeda sa'adiya a lokacin ida anbasu sakamako Nana ce take zawa ta daya itakuma Sa'adiya na biyu ,su shiga hutu mukuma muka kare karatu danshi Samir dama yadade da karewa ni yake jira mu tafi tare kuma yaga zaman Nana d'à walwala ta shiyasa yanemi contrat shekaru biyu, kafin nima nakare ahaka nakare wanda yayi dai dai da karewa contrat dun Samir ...✍️

PAGE 9&10
           ......Samir ya kan fadamun cewa anya zai iya barin Nana kuwa sabida shi Hafiz din saura shekaru biyu ya kare ya koma Niger din mu kuma yanzu zamu koma „


Hakuri na bashi akan ya barta kuma ai shekaru biyu d'in kamar gobe ne ,ya kan fadamun daya koma gida zai yi Aure ,zai Auri wace zata kula masa da yar sa ,sosai wani lokacin nakan yi kuka idan na tuna irin kaunar sa a gareni Sabida Nana yake so yayi Aure Allah sarki duniya ,da kyar ya yarda zai barta, da zamu tafi tare muka je dasu muka yi musu sayaya muka wuni tare dasu wanda sunji dadin haka Ranar da zamu tafi ko har aéroports suka kaimu kina ji ko ba zan Manta wannan ranar ba „


Rike rigar Samir tayi wanda ba karamin mamaki muka sha ba ganin wannan al'amarin ,hawaye ne suke sauko mata ta rungume Samir dun tana Fadun “please papa kar ka tafi ka barmu anan zamu bi ka kaji ”ta fada tana ririke masa wando „


Sosai na karanci yarinyar ban ce komi ba na haye jirgin ban san yasu ka kare ba na ga Samir ya shigo yazuna kusa na bai min magana ba nima haka sabida ina duniyar tunani „


Mun dawo Niger amma kullum Hankali na na ga Y'a ta sosai nake ganin tashin hankali a idon ta lokacin da take kuka kar muje mu barta yayin da Sa'adiya hankalin ta kwance yake, Samir yakan kirani yace min“ Irfan wani lokacin sai naji kamar Nana bata lafiya anya ba zanje dubota ba kuwa ”na kan girgiza masa kai nace “a'a kayi hakuri idan kaje yanzu zasu ga kamar kana tunanin ba zasu kula da ita bane ko kuma wani abun kasan dan Adam so please ka kyalesu kayi mata addu'a”ba dan ya so ba yake cewa to wanda nima abunda yake ji nakan ji shi a rai na a haka muka ci gaba da dauriya muna mata adu'aa „


Kuma Élisabeth takan kirani tayi ta kuka wai na bata yar ta kona hadata da ita ta waya duk da kuwa yanzu tayi Aure har da y'a y'a biyu amma bata manta yar tata ba ,haka ma Samir takan kirashi „


A kwana a tashi har Allah ya hadani da ke ,kin san Mi yake damuna kuwa kullum tunani na kar abunda ya faru da ni yafaru da y'a ta ko kuma kanaina ko iyayena ko Matar da zan Aura na ji tsoro sosai Nasreen a ranar Dana fara ganinki a ranar naji kin kwantamin a Rai ganin irin shigar ki ta tayarmin da hankali duk da ina miki kyakyawan zato amma naji tsoron kada na sameki ba budurwa ba ,a duk lokacin da batuna haka na kanji wani Abu ya tsayamin a Rai ,amma nayi alkawarin ko ban sameki a budurwa ba zan zauna da ke sabida dan Allah nake son ki kuma na san zai dubi zuciya ta ya karbi tubana „



Anyi Aure na da ke da Mufida Wace ita sam ban kawo zata zo min da budurcin ta ba kamar yanda kema banyi zato ba sai gashi kun kawomin na yi wa ubangiji godiya sosai nayi sadaqa na yi nafil fili da ustigfari „

Kafin  Auren mu aka maido  Hafiz nan Kwana biyu Samir yaje gidan bai ma fadamun yazo ba sosai yaran sukayi murna da ganin shi bare Nana kam ta rikeshi ko masallaci dakyar take bari yaje kuma har wannan lokacin Samir din ne ke biya musu kudin Makarantar tasu „


Sai dare yazo gidanmu yake fadamin tun safe yazo kuma gidansu Nana ya wuni Murmushi kawai na masa dan ban san mi zance ba bayan anan ya kwana da safe ya wuce Maradi dan can yake aiki dama duka familyn shi da muka dawo yayi neman yayi Aure amma a ganin shi har yanzu bai ga mai hankalin da zata kula masa da Nanansa ba ,yaso wata yarinya amma ta nuna ita ba za ta iya rike wata yarinya ba Wace ba yar taba yana son yarinyar sosai amma ya hakura da ita Nasreen duk yanda zan kwatanta miki irin son da Samir yake ma Nana Firdausi ba za ki gane ba „

Kwana biyu nima na je dubasu sosai sukayi murna amma na fahimci yadda Nana ta rame sannan ta dan kara duhu kamar tana cikin damuwa ,ban kawo komi a Rai na ba na tafi dasu shakatawa kinsan miyafaru kuwa  cemin tayi “Uncle meyasa Yanzu Mom Nafisa bata so na kamar Sa'adiya,tana yin banbanci a tsakininmu bayan mu duka y'a'yan tane ,Idan mukayi jarabawa a scool ni nake zuwa na daya ita kuma na biyu idan muka bawa dadyn mu murna yake sosai kuma Abu iri daya yake sai mana ,amma ita Mom ni bata bani GIF ,sannan sai dai ta rungume Sa'adiya har da Kiss ni kuma Harare nama takeyi ,ai Ni ce Auta ni ya kamata ta rungume amma batayi ba kuma bata koyamin assigment idan anbamu a school sai dai dady na yayi min”ba karamin al'ajabi maganar ta ta bani ba ba dai hakan yana nufin Nana yanzu rayuwar kunci take ba ,da yake Nana tafi Sa'adiya wayo sosai ,Sa'adiya ce tace “kuma kasan Uncle yanzu Mom bata bawa Firdausi Chocolat ko gaban Dady ne kuwa ,har da fada suke idan Mom ta hana ma Nana chocolat ” yanzu kai ya fahimce su murmushi na kakalo nace “to sorry yan mata na kina ji ko karki damu d'à abunda Mom dinki take miki duk cikin so ne zata daina yi miki banda rashin kunya ku ci gaba da karatu kunji a maida hankali sosai zan gani yanzu kowa yayi kokari ya zo na daya zan baku gift na musamman ”na fada muna tapa hannu da su dariya suka haw yi suna tsale bayan mun gama ne na maida su gida ,kin san wani Abu kuwa Nafissa Nana ta rungume tana dariya tace “y'a y'an uncle mi uncle ya siya muku na ga har kunkara kumatu” nima murmushin yake kawai na saki ina mai juya baya na tafi hade da cewa ta gaida Mai gidan tunda bayanan da to ta'amsa ni kuma na dawo gida „


A hanya ta ba'abunda nake tunani kamar maganar su Nana da Ita Nafisa ,yaro bai cika karya ba kuma Na-san Nana ba za ta yi karya ba haka kawai ,ina dawowa a ranar part din Mufida nake daki na na shiga na kira Samir dan shaida masa maganar su Nana dan gaskiya abun da cutarwa sosai ,ashe Mufida na Labé tana jin abunda nake fadi ni kuma ban kawo zata tsaya sauraren zancemu ba na kira Samir bugu biyu ya daga bayan mun gaisa ne nake shaida masa abunda Suka fadamun cikin tashin hankali yace “hakan yana nufin yanzu Nana rayuwar kunci take ina ba zai yuba zanje na daukota na kawo wa Mom Na-san zata fahimta sosai ”cewar Samir ,nima cikin tashin hankali sabida Na-san idan Mom din Samir ta sani kamar Ammi ta sani ne sabida sun zama kawaye ba sa boye ma juna komi bare ma wannan al'amarin mai girma “ka ga Samir ka yi hakuri kabarta a can har da gabaya Na-san komi zaiyi daidai idan Har Ammi ta san cewa ga abunda ya faru Na-san zata yafe ni a cikin y'ay'an ta kayi hakuri mu bar Nana ta rayu komi zai wuce” daga nan Ashe duk maganar da muke saï da taji sanan muka aje magana da Samir akan cewa mu kara mata lokaci mu gani ko zata canja „„


Ta 'bangaren Mufida kuwa Ashe wurin ta shiga rudin maganar tayi ta tunani Wanda a ranar nakasa gane kanta dan Sam banyi tunanin zata saurari Maganarmu ba „


Wata Rana na kira Hafiz akan Cewa zanzo naje dasu Nana gidan Zoo kuma kar inje in tarar Nafisa kadaice shiyasaka zan fadama ”a ranar ma part din Mufida nake Ashe ta ji komi ,ban san tana biye dani a taxi ba har lokacin da na dauko su Nana mukayi yawon mu duk tana biye damu„„„


Ban san ya'akayi ta hadu da Nafisa ba suka zama kawaye har ta kai tana bugun Cikin Nafisa da yake ita ma mai surutu ce kuma yanzu tana 'kin Nana sabida akan mi Nana zata rika zuwa na daya dayada ta fahimci ko akan ibada Nana tafi maida hankali fiye da Sa'adiya wannan dalilin yasa ta fara canjawa Nana ,ko a gaban Mufida takan nuna banbanci wannan dalilin yasa ta shiga bugun cikin ta har ta gano asalin labarin „



Ta shiga tashin hankali tayi kuka sosai da taji labarin amma har lokacin Nafissa  bata san Mufida matata ba ce„


A ranar kasa gane kanta nayi sosai take kuka am a bata fadamin Damuwarta ba hakan yasa na barta na shiga sha'anin gabana sabida ita ba yarinyar ba ce da zan tsaya ina ta rarashi bayan Wanda nayi mata daga karshe ma dakin ta ta koma ta rufe ta cigaba da sh'anin gabanta „


Kwana biyu fushi take dani sosai Wanda na rasa kona miye ama bata bari kin gane ba ,duk  kuwa abunda yake faruwa saï data kwashe ta fadawa ummi Wace mamaki ya kamata kuma daga baya takoma murna ,shine jiya sukaje har gidan Nafissa itada Ummi ,Suka saka Nana gaba da dariya suna dungure mata kai Sa'adiya cema mai kareta ,Ruwa da sanyin su Ummi ta diba ta watsa ma Nana wace saura kadan tasume sabida a bazata taji Ruwan ,Wanda yayi daidai da shigo wata Sabida ina so na musu surpris naje dasu yawo dan Na-san zasu ji dadin hakan ,kawai sai na tarara da wannan abun Banmusu magana ba na kamo hannun y'a ta na zo da ita shine ita ma Mufidan ta biyomu a baya Nasreen dina kinji kaddara da ta fadamin ban san ko zaki iya yafewa mijinki ba ,na kan ji tsoro da fargaba a duk sanda na tuna ranar da zakuji labarin ban san ya za ku ji ba ,ban san ko zaku yafemin ba„




Nasreen Ban san ya kike ji ba A Ranki ,Dan Allah ki min kowane irin hukunci amma Kar ki ce zaki rabu dani ba zan iya Rayuwa babu ke ba ke din ta daban ce ,Mar'atu saliha ,ina sonki Matata ,Ke ce zaki taimaken wajen su Abbi ina tsoro Nasreen,Ina Tsoro kada Ammi ta yafe ni a cikin y'ay'an ta ,Nana Kaddara ta ce kuma na karba hannu bibiyu na karba ina fatan ubangiji ya yafemin„




Sosai Nasreen ke kuka ,ta rasa wane irin kuka take ,ta rasa Miyake mata dadi ,kokarin kwacewa rakeyi daga jikin shi amma yaki bata dama kuka take sosai tana neman kwacewa gam ya rike ta kanshi ya cusa a wuyan ta yana fadun “Allah ya huci zuciyar ki gimbiyar Mata uwar gidan IRFAN ,Nayi laifi ki min duk hukuncin da kike so zan karba Matata”



Sosai take kuka kamar ance mata Mama ce ta rasu cikin Kuka tace “ka sake ni ,ka sakeni nace bana son ganinka anan idan baka ficemin a daki ba zan iya hade zuciya Na Mutu saï ka zauna kai daya A duniyar ”sosai take kokarin kwacewa ganin tana neman fita hayacin ta yasa shi sauka daga gadon duk yahada zufah idon nan nashi sunyi jajur dabaya dabaya yake fita yana kallon ta yace “relax Kada kijima kanki ciwo zan fita yanzun nan amma dan Allah kar fushina Ya shafi Nana ,kiyi hakuri ki kwantar da hankalin ki kada ki jima baby mu ciwo na fita ”ya fada yana barin dakin bakin kofar ya jingina har ya kai kasa yana sauraron sautin kukan ta da ke tashi, yasan zatayi fiye da haka yasan da ciwo sosai irin wannan al'amarin da bai ka mata ya boye ma iyalan shi ba yasan dama wannan Ranar zata zo ,yasan kowa zai tsane shi sabida kuskuren da ya'aika ta bayan ba laifin shi ba ne kaddara ce amma kowa ya kasa fahimtar shi „


Nana ce daketa kallon Uncle din nata ta zo ta dafashi dukawa tayi tana kallon shi tace “Ni ce ko Na-san Anty Nasreen sabida ni take kukan ,uncle idan ita ma bata so na ka kaini orphelins ka ga can gidan kowa da kowa ne babu Wanda ake hantara bare a dakeshi ”ta fada tana hawaye ,wani irin tausayin ta yaji ya kamashi bai sanda ya rungume ta ba yana fadun “bazakije Orphelins ba ke ma y'a ce kamar kowa kina da ƴanci nan ne gidan ku baki da in da ya fishi babu wani Wanda zai 'kara wulakanta ki matukar uncle dinki na raye ”ita ma duk da ba wai ta fahimci maganar ba kuka ta saka sosai ta kankame shi kam tana fadun “nagode Uncle kabawa Anty Nasreen hakuri kaji”hankalin Nana ko da yaushe burgeshi yake yi kiss ya sakar mata ya kamata yana share mata hawayen yace “haba Beauty bata kuka zo na saka miki cartoon kiyi kallo ,Anty Nasreen kuma zata sauko cikinta ne ke mata ciwo OK ”kai ta daga mishi tayi dan murmushi hade da fadun “to kayi mata allura mana tunda idan bamu da lafiya kai kake mana lokacin da muna France”mikewa yayi yana fadin “na mata ai shi yasa ma take kuka yi kallonki kyaleta ”daga haka dakin shi ya koma zama yayi hade da dafe kai yasan Nasreen dinshi zata sauko amma bai san yaushe ba tashin hankalin shi yanzu bai wuce halin da take ciki ba ....✍️

            ........✍️DOCTOR kai Yanzu tunaninshi su Abbi ne bare ma Ammi baya so ta ji labari daga bakin Ummi yasan hawan jini ma zai iya kamata shi kuwa baya son haka ,zai fada mata da kanshi „


Azahar nayi yayi sallah ,yana fitowa bai ga Nana ba dudubawa ya shiga yi Amma sam bai gan ta ba waje yaje fanfon da ke waje ya tsinkayeta tana al'wala murmushi ya saki hade da ajiyar zuciya „


Yana tsaye yana kallon ta ta kara alwala kuma tayi daidai wani dadi yaji koba komi yasan  Nana ta samu tarbiyya da addini „


Karasowa tayi wurin shi tace “Uncle na ga kana daki ne ita kuma Anty bata nan shiyasaka kawai natanbi mai gadi yabani ruwa shine ya nunamin fanfon”cikin dan murmushi yace “masha Allah tokije kiyi sallah a falo kinji kibar Antyn ki tahuta”ya fada yana shafa kanta „


Sai da yaga ta shiga part dun ya wuce masalaci ,bayan ya dawo ne yatarar bata nan zama yayi hade da dokar remote dun TV ya canja Chanel hanun shi rike da kanshi yana tunanin Rayuwa „„


Nasreen ce tafito rikeda hanun Nana Wace tunda taga anyi Sallah tafito tasan Nana zatayi sallah,Wanda yayi dai dai da zuwan ta falo taja hanun ta suka shiga dakin sukayi sallah „„


Bayan sungama ne ta kalli Farida tare da shafa kanta tace “miyasamu babyn Uncle naga kin wani marairaice ko yunwa kikeji”tafada ganin yanda ta rakube ajikin gadon tana kalon Nasreen dun 


“tunda safe naji kina kuka Anty Uncle yacemin bakida lafiya kuma dan yaliki alura ne kike kuka,kidaina kuka zakisamu lafiya bari namiki adu'ar marasa lafiya”tafada tana hada hanayen ta biyu “laba'asa tuhurun ”takaranta tashafa ma Nasreen dun ,hade da cewa “kidaina kuka kanji zaiyi ciwo haka Dady yake fadamun idan Anty Nafissa ta dungure ni nayi kuka ”cewar Nana „


Bata san ya'akayi ba itadai tanajin son yarinyar a cikin zuciyar ta sosai tun sanda tadora taganta ,tun batasan ko wacece ita ba ,Batada laifi mizaisa a tsaneta ko akyamace ta bayan ba laifin tabane ,dama haka Allah yake abunsa Duk da bata hanyar Aure aka haife taba ama Allah baisa tazama daga cikin falasasu ba ,tana fatan Allah yakare ta kodan kyawawan dabi'un ta ,jitayi ta tabata hakan yasa ta dawo hayacinta tace “yi hakuri Babyn Uncle tashi muje kici Abinci ko” kama hanun ta tayi suka fito falon Mafarin suka gamu da Shi zaune anan falon bata kalleshi Nana tajawa kujera itama ta zauna abinci tazuba musu sosai sukaci abunsu suna dariya„

Bakaramin mamaki Nasreen du dinshi kebashi ba yanzu ,ama kobakomi yaji dadi yanda takarbi Nana shikan idan yarasata baisan yazaiyi da rayuwar sa ba „


Suna karewa suka tatare kayan itada Nana ,shikuwa gogan oufan baice musu ba „


Haka tasaka Nana gaba suka kule dakin baisan misukeyi ba ama yakanji dariyar su itada Nana lokaci zuwa lokaci shikuwa Irfan sallah kawai ketadashi ahka suka wuni „...


Nasreen kai bayn la'asar kitchen suka shiga ,shikuwa yana dakinsa bayan karfe biyar ya fita baidawo ba ,sai bayan isha'i „


Ledodi uku yazo dasu yana shiga falon baigansu ba ya'aje kedodun wanka yashiga bayan yafito ne yatarar Nasreen nayima Nana kitson fulani irin Manya manyan nan sosai kitson yayi mata Kyau dayake tana da gashin ta „


Zama yayi Nasreen ce tace mishi sannu da zuwa Nana ma haka kanshi kawai yadafe dake tafaman Sara mishi ,satar kalonshi Nasreen tayi saikuma ta kauda idon ta ,bayan takare mata ne tamike zata wuce tagaban ta yace “ga shopping nan nayimuku Léda daya ta Nana ce daya kuma taki ”bai ko kaleta ba yafada ,ganin kedodin biyu da yahada wuri guda yatabar mata natane sai kawai tadoka hade da cewa “Allah yasaka da alkairi ”Nana ma godiya tamasa tana tsale „


Mikewa yayi dankai wa Mufida Nata ledan part dun ta yashiga Ama wayam kofar a rufe. Bubugawa yashigayi ama shiru kanshi yadafe wayar shi ya dauka yakirata ama shiru tana ringing ba'adauka keshewa yayi yana Jan tsaki badai gari tajeba bata fadama ba sam bazai dauki wanan sakarcin ba part dun Nasreen Yakoma dakinshi yashiga yayi wanka hade da saka jalabiya „„


Turaren shi yafeshe jikinshi dashi yahaw bed dinsa yana tunanin Rayuwa ,Nasreen dinshi tayi fushi dashi tunda gashi ko wurinshi yaw bazata kwana ba meyasa kowa yakasa fahimtar shi ne yaw bazai kwana da abar kaunar shi ba duk da kuwa yanda yake abukace da ita kwana biyu Kenan baiyi sex ba yanajin Mara shi na matukar Murda mai wani lokaci ama tashin hankalin dayake ciki yafi wanan „


Bai karasa tunanin shi ba yaji budewar kofa Nasreen ce sanye cikin kayan Barcci Riga da wando iya guiwa rigar kuwa bakin cibiyar ta tatsaya wanda cikin ta yadan fito hanun  ta rikeda da kofi ,kalo daya yamata yaji hankalin shi na neman barin jikin shi yasan Idan yanemi hakkin shi zata bashi ama bawai dan tana cikin yanayi mai dadi ba ,kauda kanshi yayi daga kalon ta itama dauke kai tayi zama tayi bakin gadon hade da fadun “ga Kofi dunka nan ”zaune yatashi miko mishi tayi ,kofin yakai bakinshi dakadan kadan yakesha yana satar kalon ta ganin itakuwa TV dake dakin kawai take kalo duk daba'a kunne take ba „


Sai dayakare sha yamika mata ansa tayi ta'aje a gefen gadon hawa bisa gadon tayi hade da kwanta wa saman kirjin shi kasancewar shima yakoma kwance ,wani irin sanyi ne yaratsa Zuciyar shi haka kadai yawadatass dashi ya yarda Nasreen itace jigon rayuwar shi itace mahadin ta „


“kana tunanin zanyi ta fushi ne dakai miji na Bayan Na-san abunda yafaru dakai Kaddara ce ,Wanda nayi imani da hakan ,babu Wanda yake kaucema kaddara sa ,taka kaddara tawa ce ,duk abunda zai shafeka yashefeni ,bazan iya yafema laifin dakamin ba sai nama hukunci da abunda kamin ,kamin laifi sabida kaboyemin wanan Baban al'amarin Wanda sam baikamata ba zanyima hukunci ne bayan su Ammi sun yankema nasu ,Ina son Nana sabida addinin ta da kyawawan dabi'unta ,ina son ta tun bansan ita wacece ba duk abunda kakawo mai Kyau ko mara kyau zan karbeshi hannu bibiyu ,Nana y'a tace nafara sabawa da ita ,Ina fatan Ubangiji kada yakamata da laifin iyeyen ta Allah yatsare ta ,Ina fatan Ubangiji yakarbi tuban ka ya yafema kura kuranka ” tafada wasu hawaye na zubo mata„

Cigaba tayi da fadun “gobe zamuje gida mugayawa su Ammi abunda yafaru ”jin yamata wata irin runguma yasata dan kara ,shikuwa Kiran sunan su Ammi datayi shi ne yake neman dagula masa lissafi yazaiyi da Ammi idan taji wanan labarin da wane ido zai kalli iyayen shi da Surukan shi tir da wanan mumunan kaddara data abka masa ,Nasreen kai tagane sarai tsoro yakeji ,karo Na farko dataga tsoro a idanun Jarumin mijin nata „


Hanun ta tajimke cikin nashi har yanzu dai hawayen take tace “zamu fadawa su Ammi koda hakan yana nufin zasuyi fushi damu Na-san Na-dan kankanin lokaci ne komi zaiyi dai dai insha Allah ina tare dakai Miji na ako wane hali katsinci kanka ”tafada tana fashewa da kuka „


IRFAN kai shikadai yasan abunda yakeji a Ran shi daya samu dama shima da kukan zaiyi zaibarta tayi kukan ta danbaiyasan abunda takeji ba,sosai tashiga yin kuka shidai shuru yayi yana sauran ta kusan minti goma ganin takidaina shurun Wanda zai iya zama barazana ga lafiyar ta itada babyn yasa shi ska harshen shi yana lasar hawayen ,haka yake lashewa wasu na gangarowa salon shi mai mantar da ita yashiga yi sosai yanzu kai tafara a jiyar zuciya saida yatabatar ta mance damuwar ta sanan yarungume abar sa Wace tafara jin barcci dan ya fahimci cikin nata maisaka ta barcci ne adu'aa yamusu yatofa yarungume ta itadai baccin ta takeyi hankali kwance shikuwa sam bacci kauracewq idon sa yayi yana tunanin makomar sa a wurin su Ammi „


Saï kusan Karfe hudu bacci yadauke sa „


Asuba nayi yatasheta tare sukayi wanka da al'wala ita kuwa dakin ta tashiga dan tada Nana taga ashema tatashi tanajin karan Ruwa a toilettes kasancewar akwai Alarmes tasan shi yatasheta ,murmushi kawai tayi ta shinfida sallaya fitowa tayi hade da dokar hijab dinta da Irfan yasoya mata jiya sababi har kala goma sallah takabarta „


DOCTOR IRFAN kuwa yana fitowa yabubuga part dun Mufida har yanzu shiru kardai Mufida ba'agida ta kwana ba sosai yaji hankalin shi yatashi ,massalaci ya wuce yau bai jima ba yadawo „


Nasreen kai bayan sunkare ne Nana tagaishe ta ta'amsa cikin kulawa tare suka fito falon dakin shi tashiga kasancewar taji wayar shi na ruri ,duba mai Kiran tayi taga ansa Ammi na hakan yatabbatar mata da Ammi ce batayi kasa aguiba ta doka hade da yin sallama ,daga bangaren Ammi kuwa ansa wa tayi batare da ta'ansa gaisuwar da Nasreen kemata ba tace “Ina IRFAN ? ” 
“yana massalaci ”Nasreen tafada tana sauke ajiyar zuciya “kifadamasa yasameni agida idan yadawo ”kitt takashe wayar 


Wani irin tsoro ne yashigeta kardai Ammi taji labari ahaka IRFAN yasameta tana jujuya wayar hade da shiga duniyar tunani ,jin an rungume ta tabaya yasaka ta dawo hayacinta Murmushi ta kakalo shikuwa wuyan ta yashiga sunsunawa hade da dora hanunshi saman dan cikin ta „ 

“miye yasamu Kyakyawa naga fuskar ta tajanja”
“Ammi ce tace kaje tana neman ka”tafada cikin sanyi ,zuciyar shi ce yaji tabuga yasan wata kila Ammi taji labari ne ,cikin dauriya dan kar takaraya yace “to miye ne kirana kawai takeyi Na-san maganar Nana ce zankarbi duk hukuncin da Ammi zatamin zan karba ”yafada tana bata kiss a baki „

Sakin ta yayi yana fadun “kitaimakamin mana baby bara nayi wanka naje ”yafada yana d'aura towel yashige bayi


Nasreen kai yanzu sosai tsoro yashige ta kayan dazaisa tafido mishi tabar dakin falon takoma tazauna tayi tagumi tana tunani Mafita zatayi komi sabida mijin ta ,zata bishi zatayi wasu Ammi bayani yakamata su fahimci ba laifin shi bane Kaddara ce tafadamai ,ko duniya zata gusjeshi itakai zata zauna da abunta a haka tajima tana wanan tunani har bata san sanda ya sumbace ta a goshi ba yace “Fadamun damuwar ki Matata zan magance miki ita kome CeCe ”yafada yana kalon cikin idanun ta dayake hango tsananin tausayin shi a idon ta ,murmushi kawai yayi ,itakuwa kanta tasada kasa sabida kwarjini daya mata hade da girgiza kai alamar ba komi „


Yagane sarai tana son binshi ne hakan yasashi mikewa yashiga dakinta baijima yafito da hijab a hanun shi mikar da ita yayi tsaye hade da dasaka mata shi „


TV dun da Nana ka kallo Wanda cartoon ne yakashe hade da kamata hanun su suka fice a part dun kulewa yayi wurin parking yaje gaba yabudema Nasreen tashiga „


Nana kuma baya yabude mata yazauna mazaunin dreba yaja motar mai gadi nadaga masa hannu ,bai zarce ko ina ba sai gidansu parking yayi yana fitowa yabudema Nana kasancewar Nasreen tabude da kanta „


Hannun Nana yarike direct yana bi hanyar da zata sadashi da part dun nasu Abbi „


Shine gaba da Nana Nasreen na bayan su „


Da salama yashiga falon ,Wanda bakaramin mamaki yasha ba yanzu da safiyar nan ganin mutanan dake falon„


Ammi ce zaune kusada Abbi ,ummi nadaga gefe sai wani Harare harere takeyi ,sai Uncle Imran da Mom mahaifiyar Mufida ,Sai Mama da Abba Wanda Irfan dun ne yakirasu dakanshi dansuna hakkin dazasu san wanan al'amarin ,sai su Al'moctar dasuke zaune kasa ,Su Abba ne suka amsa Amma banda su Abbi sai kawu Imran daya ansa kasa yaduka tareda sakin hanun Nana Wace itama Ganin abunda dadyn ta yayi yasa itama dukawa sai Nasreen da itama taduka kasa kusa da Mijin nata ,gaishesu Irfan yashiga yi ama su Ammi basu ansaba itada Abbi da Mom and ummi „


Nasreen ma gaishesu tayi ama wanan karon su Abbi sun ansa Abbi ne yayi gyaran Murya hade da fadun Al'sidiq yabude musu taro da adu'a ,adu'ar yayi sanan Abbi yafara magana kamar haka .....✍️



🖊️MAMDOCTOR IRFAN🥼

BOOK 2 
PAGE 13&14
            .........✍️“Matar ka kuma kanwar ka jiya tazo gida cikin kuka da tashin hankali ,Wanda ya firgita mu har mukayi tunanin ko mutuwa akayi ,naso kiranka tace nabari har gobe kuma takira Imran sunzo miye yake faruwa ne IRFAN ???”Abbi yafada yana kalonsa „„„

Wani irin tsoro da faduwar gaba ne suka kama Nassreen ba itakadai ba harshi Doctor dakanshi ke duke bai dago ba tunda Abbi yafara magana baisan da wane ido zai kalli iyayen nashi ba da surukan shi „„


Minti uku shuru babu Wanda yace kala Nasreen ce tadago takali Abbi da Ammi sai kuma tasada kanta kasa„


Abbi ne yace “Kuyi magana mana kunyi mana shiru”yafada cikin fada fada dan yanzu kai yaga abun nasu da Rain wayo 

Uncle Imran ne yace “Yaya kayi musu a hankali mana ”shiru kowa yayi yana jiran tsamani bazato sukaji Nasreen na fadun “Da farko dai Abbi zanfara da yin nasiha ne duk danasan ku iyaye nane ama wani lokacin kukan mata wasu abubuwa kamar yada da kaddara ,Na-san kunsan mumuni na kwarai shine mai yarda da kaddara walaw mai Kyau ko Mara Kyau ,Na-san zaku karbi kaddara datasamemu hannu bibiyu ,sanan kuyi hakuri kuyi hakuri dan Allah ku yarda da waanan kaddara da tasamemu kada kuyi fushi da hikimar ubangiji hakika duk abunda yayi dai dai ne ”tafada wasu hawaye na zubo mata bata son su Abbi sudoki abun dazafi bata so suyi fushi dashi tana so suyi tawakali kamar yanda ubangiji yafada „

Jikin kowa yayi sanyi barema Ammi kalon ta tamaida ga Nasreen tace “fadamin y'a ta miye yake faruwa ,wani abun yasameku ne ”tafada cikin dan damuwa 


“a'a Ammi ”tafada murya na rawa hannun Irfan takamo sukazo har gaban su Abbi suka duka kamar masu zaman tahiya hawayen take gogewa batasan da wane ido zata kalli surukan nata tamusu wanan bayanin hakan yasata sada kai kasa cikin sanyi tace “Hakika kowane bawa da tasa kaddara ,Muma gatamu kaddara nan ”tafada tana rungume Nana Jikin ta Wace tafara tsorata da mutanan wajen har tafara hawaye itama ganin Nasreen dun nayi „


Kowa da mamaki yake kalon ta “Ammi kuyi hakuri dan Allah kugafarta mana ko Allah muna mishi laifi ya yafemana ,gamu agabanku kumana duk hukuncin dakuke ganin yadace ”cewar  Nasreen dun 


Ummi ce tace “kinata magana cikin magana kifito fili kifadamana miye ke faruwa ”wani irin kalo Abbi y'a watsa mata hakan yasa tayi shiru 


“Abbi....”Nasreen tafada kuma sai tayi shiru sabida abun da nauyi sosai„


Cikin dauriya tafara basu labarin abunda yafaru sosai take kuka hade da Rokon kada suhukunta Mijin ta kaddara ce „


Tundaga farkon labarin har karshe tabasu takarasa tana kuka wiwi hade da rungume Nana kamar za'akwace mata tace “Dan Allah Abbi kuyafe masa kaddara ce Munkarbeta hannu bibiyu kuma ku karba please ”


Gabadaya falon shiru kakeji sai kukan Nasreen da Nana ,


IRFAN kuwa kanshi aduke yake har yanzu bai dagoba 


Ummi ce tayi wata irin shewa hade da tapi tana fadun “ahaye yaw dai gashi Allah yanuna muku illar rashin adalci ,da tura yara karatu wata kasa ga tukuiici nan kunsamu.....”bata karasa ba Abbi yace “chut up”tsik tayi kamar wuta yadauke ta „„

“inallilahi ”kowa yashiga maimaitawa ,shuru tsawon minti biyar Ammi da tun dazu tayi suman zaune wata dariya ce ta subuce mata “Haba karya kukeyi wanan ba IRFAN dina bane bashi bane a gaba na ,kun sauyamin shi yaro na ba Mazina ci bane ,yaro na mai tsoron Allah ne ,zan iya shedar IRFAN ALI ba mazina ci bane kuje kuzo min da yaro na ”tafada tana girgiza kai hade da rumtse idon ta wai ko Mafarki take ganin kamar har yanzu kukan Nasreen takeji a Kunan ta yasata Budé ido tar ta sauke su akan Nana baza to tajawo Nana hade da barke hanun rigar ta nadama da karfi Wanda yasa Nana kara firgita hade da fashewa da sabon kuka „


Hanun tashiga dudubawa wani tanbon baki tagani Wanda tasan Abbi nadashi kuma IRFAN ma yayi gado ga Nana nan ma dashi Wanda shi IRFAN dun bai masan ko yanada tabon ba „



Kai tashiga girgizawq hade da jimke hanun rigar Abbi cikikin tsoro da firgitsi tace “ko kowa zaimin karya kai bazakayimin ba Yaya ,fadamun Yaya Dagaske ne abunda nakeji kuma nake gani ayanzu gaskiya ne IRFAN dina ne da wanan yar ta shege”wani irin Abu IRFAN yaji yadaki zuciyar shi Wanda baisan y'a zai fasalta shi ba Ammin shi dakanta take furta ma yar shi wanan kalamin ,bazai bari koma wanene ba yakira Nana da yar shege itadun y'a ce mai yanci kamar kowa ,idon shi ne sukayi ja sosai hade da fuskar shi „„



Abbi ne yarike ta sosai duk da shima yana cikin choc yace “dagaske ne Zainab dagaskene ”iya abunda Abbi yafada yana mai kama Matar tashi ganin tana neman fita hayacin ta „

Abba ne yayi gyaran murya dashima tun dazu abun yadake shi yace “Alhji ku kwanatar da hankalin ku mubi abunan a sann...”bai karasa ba yaga Ammi ta hau saman benen da gudu Wanda ba karamin mamaki yabasu ba ,shuru falon yayi sai Mom ce tace “gaskiya ancuci y'a ta Mazina ci fah ta Aura ” wani banzan kalo Uncle Imran ya watsa mata hakan ya sata yin shuru tana gunguni „


Ammi ce ta sauko hannun ta dauke da dorina irin wada ake bawa dokuna kashi gaban su tazo Wanda har yanzu suna durkushen Idon ta cike da hawaye ta shiga lapta masa bulalar babu kyakyautawa sosai take dukansa Mama ce tatashi dan Hanawa Abba ya tare sabida wata kila hakan zaisa zuciyar ta tayi sanyi ,Abbi ma shuru yayi yana kalon kasa Wanda har yanzu yana jin choc din maganar „


Sosai take zabga masa bulalar IRFAN kuwa har yanzu kanshi akasa Nasreen ce ta rungumeshi ta gaba Ammi tacigaba da dukansu Wanda duk da haka Ammi bata fasa ba dukansu takeyi sosai tana hawaye Wanda dukan yamafi samun Nasreen „


IRFAN ne ya juyo ya zamana yanzu Ammi tafi samun shi sosai ya rungume Nasreen dinshi Wace take ta kuka „


La Maman ce Wace tun dazu taketa faman kuka ta ruga ta rike Abbi tana fadun “Abbi please kahana Ammi dukansu baka ganin Anty Nasreen sai kuka take rigar Yaya ma ta fara jini kace ta daina dan Allah ”sosai take kuka hade da rungume Abbi ,shima rungume ta yayi yana shafa kanta ,Kawu Imran ne yaso dakatar da ita Ama Abbi ya daga masa hannu 


Sharifa Wace tun lokacin da Ammi ta shiga dukan Yayan nasu ta rungume Asidiq tana kuka shikaran kanshi wasu hawaye ne ke zubomishi,Al'moctar kai zaune Yake har yanzu sabida choc KO motsawa baiyi ba ba wai abunda yayansu y'a aikata bane ya rudashi sai ganin irin dukan da Ammi kayiwa Irfan dun Wanda har jini ya fara kwantawa abayn farar rigarshi „„

Kuka takeyi sosai tana dukansa “ashe ban sani ba nayi kuskure yarda dakai Kaci Amana ta ,Jini nane ya'aikata wanan haramun kayimin tabo Irfan kayima zuri'ar mu tabo ,kafita ,kayi ne sa damu bama son ganinka kaje, kar nasake ganin fuskar ka akusa damu ”tafada tana yarda bulalar hade da rungume Abbi tana kuka kamar karamar yarinya,Abbi kai Rungume matar tasa yayi yana rarashi „


IRFAN kai bai sake shiga tashin hankali ba Saïda yaji kalaman Ammin nasa badai hakan na nufin ta yafe shi bane daga cikin su zuciyar shi ce yaji tana tafar fasa Ammin shi ce take yimasa wanan furucin dukan data mishi sam bai taba shi bâ kamar yanda yakejin Kukan Nasreen d'à Nana ga kalamn Ammin nashi „



Abba ne yace “asha Hajiya ayi hakuri ki daina fadun wannan kalaman akan jinin ki mu tsaya Muyi Nazari kaddara nakan kowa ,kowa baya wuce kaddara sa ”ya fada yana girgiza kansa....✍️

BOOK 2 
PAGE 15&16
            .........✍️IRFAN ne yamike rungume da Nana da Nasreen fuskar nan tashi tayi jajur hakama idon shi ,yana mikewa bai ce tak ba yafara tafiya alamar zaibar falon da gudu la maman da Sharifa suka rungume shi suna masu fashewa da kuka ,kowa tsik kakeji banda shashekar Nasreen da Nana ,sai kukan Ammi dayake tashi hade da ajiyar zuciya „


Rumtse idon shi gam yayi baya son jin kukan kanan nashi duk inda yake hankalin shi tashi yakeyi ,Ammi ce tabuga musu tsawa hade da fadun “kusake shi maza nace kada yashafa muku kashun kaji ”duk da tsawar da Ammi tamusu baisa sun sakeshi ba sai ma kara rungume shi dasukayi ,Shikuwa kalaman Ammin NASA ne suka saké gigitashi „


Hanun su yacire daga jikin shi hade da yakicesu yakama hanyar fita ,Murya Al'moctar yaji yana fadun “Haba Yaya Ina zakaje kardai kaima kayi fushi ,kada kayi nesa damu mu zamubika duk inda zakaje ,kaine gatanmu ”yafada yana rungume su sharifa dake ta kuka„

Wani irin tausayin kanan nashi ne yaji yashige shi bashida wani farin ciki bayan su ,zaiyi komi sabida su Ama wanan karon zai cika Umarnin mahaifiyar shi wata kil hakan shi zai sanya ta farin ciki zaiyi nesa dasu kamar yanda tabukata „


Tafiya yacigaba dayi har yakai kofa yaji an rungume shi tabaya ,Mufida ce Wace tàke Labé tana jin duk abunda yake faruwa sosai fuskar ta tayi ja kasancewar ta fara cikin Kuka tace “Yaya Irfan ina zakaje kabarni kasan bazan iya Rayuwa babu kai ba duk laifi nane bazan gusheba ina dorawa kaina laifi duk ni najawa ni Wace irin mace ce Wace batasan kwanciyar hankalin mijin ta ina Rokon Allah Ubangiji y'a yafemin kurakurai na hakika ni mai laifi ce zanbika Yaya duk inda zakaje ”kuka take sosai harda shasheka „


Jitayi An yakice ta ajikin shi an dauke ta da Mari Tass kakeji Ammi tazabgawa Mufida Mari “ashe bakida hankali bansani ba Mazina ci zakibi ,dalilin kema ki haifama na mazinatan wallahi idan kika bishi sai dai kema kinemi wasu famillyn badai NABAZZAGA BA ”Ammi tafada tana ture Mufida gefe „


Kofa ta saké nunawa Irfan hade da rumtse Idon ta gam batare da takaleshi ba „


IRFAN kai yanzu y'a tabata Ammin shi bata son sa ta yafeshi shine Mazinacin shi take jifah da wanan kalamin bai kara kalon taba yaja Su suka shiga mota da gudu yabar gidan Wanda saura kadan y'a take mai gadin„.

Tuki yakeyi batare da ma yana kalon gaban shi ba yarasa Miyake masa dadi yaw komi jiyake yafice masa A duniya ,Kukan Nasreen yaji tarike ciki tana fadun “Yaya Irfan ciki na wayo Mara ta ”tafada tana ririke shi,Jini yaga yana fitowa takasan ta wani irin harbawa yaji zuciyar shi tayi kardai dukan da Ammi tayimusu ne zata jawo mishi hasarar gudan jinin shi„


Hanyar dazata sadashi da asibitin shi yakaita agagauce yafito yadauketa sai office dinsa Nurse yakira suka kawo mishi kayan aiki dudubata yashiga yi sosai yajima sanan dakyar yasamu jinin yatsaya ,alokacin har tasuma Yamata alura barcci zama yayi bisa kujera office dun nashi yana sauke ajiyar zuciya ,zuciyar shi kawai yakeji tana tafarfara yanzu shikenan Ammi tamishi asara cikin nasa zai yafe komi ama banda wanan itadun Mahaifiyar shi ce KO yanzu ama wanan cikin yaji haushin barin shi sosai Ammi ce silar barar mishi da jinin shi ,itace ummul aba'isin wanan „„„


Sabida kaddara ,da Sauri yamike tunowa yabaro Nana Amota gudu gudu ya fice yana zuwa ya ganta kwance baya itadai taci kula har hawaye sunbushe mata wani irin tausayin tane yaji yaratsa shi gata dai kankanuwa ama laifin Iyayen ta yana neman shafar ta ,daukota yayi da Sauri ta farka tana kuka tana fadun “Uncle wayo Anty Nasreen ”ganin Irfan ta rungume shi tana fadun “dan Allah Uncle kamaidani wurin Dady na da Mom ni banasaon gidanku tunda suma basa sonku ”bakaramin mamaki maganar Nana tabashi ba, baya so tatsani famillyn shi ,Baya so kokadan Nana nawa take da zata fara sawanan abun A Ran ta murmushi yaka kalo yace “Haba babyn Uncle waya gayamiki basa sonmu suna sonmu mana kuma sai kince nafada miki laifi mukayi musu shiyasa suka hukun ta mu taso muje wurin Antyn ki kinga batada lafiya ama taji sauki yanzu dan haka zo maza ”yafada yana daukarta sama ,itakuwa dariya take kyakyatawa abun ta„


Dakin yakaita yadora saman kujera wayar shi yabata tashiga game shikuwa kusada Nasreen ya sauna yana karemata kalo Allah sarki dubeta yarinya karama batasan komi ba ama kulum cikin jarabawa take daga wanan sai wanan ,jin Kiran sallar la'asar yashi mikewa yace “Babyn Uncle zanje massalaci ”peak yabata akumatu yatafi zuwa massalaci hade d'à Rufe su ta waje „

Baidawo ba sai kusan karfe shida koda yashigo Nana ma tayi barcci kusa da Nasreen dun Na-san barcci ne yadauke ta ,Hanun ta ruke da waya saura kadan tafado dan fadon baya isarsu hanun ta tadora saman cikin Nasreen dun a hankali yadauke ta yadora akafada cikin Mota yakaita sanan yazo yadauki Nasreen itama Wace har yanzu barcci wahala takeyi akafada y'a dorata itama cikin mota yasakata abaya ya kwantar da ita Nana kuwa gaba yasakata koma wa yayi cikin asibitin yajima saï kusan Sallar magrib yadawo „


Yana dawowa yaja motar aguje sai gida ,yana shiga yayi parking Nasreen yafara kai wa ya aje acikin falo bisa doguwar kujera sanan yakomo yadauki Nana itakuma bisa kafet ya'ajeta dakinshi yashiga yayi wanka sanan yadauro al'wala agagauce ya wuce massalaci bai tsaya yin isha'i ba yasan koda wane lokaci Zasu iya farkawa baya kusa barema Nasreen datafi kowa bukatar shi akusa da ita „


Koda yakoma ma basu tashiba Nana yatada Ganin batayi sallah ba, dakin Nasreen dun tashiga tayi al'wala tayi sallah ,shikuwa dakinshi yashiga akwati ladaidaiciya yadauka yafara tatara duk wani Abu dayasan zai bukata har Nana ta iskoshi zama tayi bakin gadon tana kalon yanda yake hada kayan tace “Uncle tafiya zakayi ne naganka da akwati kana tatara kaya ”Murmushi yayi yace “Eh tafiya zamuyi ama tare zamuje daku ”yafada yana mai cigaba da aikin sa ,shiru yaji tayi hakan yasashi juyowa yace “lafiya kuwa Babyn Uncle ”

“Nidai bana so nayi nesa Da Sa'adiya Na-san tana kewata Uncle muje naganta kaji ”shiru yayi sai kuma can yace “ba yanzu ba zankai ki kiganta kinji ”da to ta'amsa shukuma yaci gaba da sh'anisa daga baya Jan hannun ta yayi sukaje dakin Nasreen itama cikin akwatin ta na lefe yadoki dankareriyar ya fara sakamata nata kayan bukatar yajima sosai dan baima je massalaci ba sai kusan karfe tara yakamala Falo suka fito kitchen yashiga dan yasan Nasreen doli zata bukaci abinci ga Nana ma yasan yunwa takeji ,indomie yadafa musu yasaka ma Nana cikin Plat itakuma race sam sai Antyn ta tatashi zasuci babu yanda baiyi da itaba ama taki wanka yayi yabarta tana kalon cartoon jalabiya yasaka yadawo falon kusada Nasreen dun yazauna yashiga tunanin rayuwa yayi nisa sosai atunanin shi yaji adamke hanunshi „


Nasreen kuwa tun dazu tafarka kanta ne kemata wani irin ciwo ga kuma Marata datake dan yimata zafi zafi dakyar tabude ido tausakesu akan kyakywan Mijin nata ganin yashiga duniyar tunani yasata damkar hanun sa Wanda yadawo dashi hayacinsa da Sauri ya rungumo ta jikin shi yana fadun “Sannu Kintashi ,babu inda yakemiki ciwo ko”duk yadan damu 

Nasreen kai kanta ne yayi nauyi sosai ga bakin ta batajin zata iya budeshi murmushin doli ta kakalo zaune take korrin tashi ama ya rungume ta kam„


“katashi muje nadorama Ruwan zafi kaga yanda banka yayi kuwa Na-san yanama zafi bari nadora sai nagasama ”tafada ahankali tana kokarin mikewa„


Gam yariketa sosai kamar wani ne zai kwace mishi ita „kuka yafashe dashi sosai kamar wani karamin yaro ,kuka yakeyi babu kyakyautawa Wanda yasaka Nasreen dun ma fashewa da kuka tayi tana mai kankame mijin nata kusan minti goma ganin yakidaina kukan bata taba tunanin akwai abunda zaisa Irfan kuka haka ba tasan Mijin ta jarumi ne na gaske yanzu kuma gahi yanama ta kuka Allah kadai yasan abunda yakeji aranshi Bayan shi ta shiga bubugawa tace “kayi hakuri kayi hakuri Mijina ,komi yayi zafi maganin shi Allah ,Na-san mikakeji A ranka ,kayi ad'u'a komi zai wuce ,komi zaifaru ina bayanka ba zan gujema ba ,”ta fada tana rungume shi kamar karamin yaro „


Nana ce sukaji ta fashe d'à kuka da sauri suka jiyo Nasreen ce tace “Subhannalhi baby Minene fadamun zo ”matsowa Nana tayi cikin kuka tace “wanene ya taba min Uncle ina kuma kema na ga kina kuka ku fadamin waye ya bigeku zan ramaku idan kuma yafi karfina zan hada shi da Allah idan nayi sallah, Malamin mu yace Allah yana karba adu'ar Wanda aka zalunta ko Anty ”ta fada tana goge hawayenta ,wayon Nana ba karamin mamaki yake basu ba hanun ta ta kama ta hada ita da IRFAN din ta rungume tace “Babu Wanda ya tabamu kawai Uncle dinki cikin shi yake masa ciwo nima haka shine muke kuka ”ta fada tana murmushi 


“ashe kaima Uncle in baka da lafiya kuka kake kamar anty”ta fada tana kyakyawata Murmushi ya saki yadungure mata kai yace “Naji kema haka kikeyi ai ki yi ta Kiran Momy Dady ”ya fada cikin yarinta 


Dariya suka shiga yi su duka ......✍️



🖊️MAMAN FAUDOCTOR IRFAN🥼
