BOOK 2 
PAGE 17&18
            .........✍️Dokar ta yayi cak sai dakinta ajeta yayi saman bed dun hade da bata kiss a baki yace “minti biyu yanzu zandawo OK ?”kai tadaga masa kawai sabida sabida Mara ta dataji ta murda bata so yashiga wani tashin hankalin ,cikin ta tashiga tabawa tana tunanin miye yasame ta kuma dazu harda Jini „„

Nana ce tashigo hade da zama kusan Nasreen dun tace “Anty yunwa nakeji tundazu nake jira kitashi muci abuncin mu”tafada tana kalo ta ,dariya tayi tajawo ta jikin ta tace “Matso Babyn Uncle yanzu ko zakici abinci ,shine kika ci kika tsaya jirana kinsan ba Kyau zama da yunwa” itama Nana Dariya tayi tace “matso kiji Anty ”kunan ta takai dai dai na Nasreen tana fadamata wani abun Wanda bansan komenene ba, dai dai lokacin DOCTOR IRFANyashigo yace “Miye aka kulawa bada Uncle ba gulma ba kyau”shima yafada yana ajemusu abincin kasan kafet yashunfuda Léda dankar wurin yabaci thé yahadama Nasreen mai zafi sabida cikin ta yabude sai datasha kadan sanan shukuma yasaka musu indomin a cikin plat daya suka fara ci yayi hakan ne danko zasu dan samu Farin ciki ,ama yana ci kalaman Ammin nasa namasa yawo a kunnai ,Nasreen ce ta lura dashi baya cin abincin kamar yanda itama bataci hannun shi tasaka cikin nata hade da aje cokalin ta dan kwanta a dantsen hannun shi„


Kalon ta yayi yace “yanaga kin aje cokalin oya ci abinci”yafada yana kokarin dokar cokalin„„


Kai tagirgizami shi alamar bata so ,bai kulata ba yadibo abincin yakai bakinta badan tso ba tabude baki yasa mata cokali hudu tace takoshi Ruwa masu dan dumi yabata tasha ,ganin dare tayi yasa ta san dabata yi salolin ta ba ,hakan yasata mikewa ama sai taji Marara ta rike dafe cikin tayi tace “aushhh”da Sauri yamike hade da kamata tashiga toilettes dun yaso yataimaka mata tayi wanka ama tanuna sam zata iya hakan yasashi kyaleta ya fito ya barta Nana ya iske duk tabata wurin ,bai yimata magana ba yatatara kayan yakai kicthen„


Gyaran wurin yayi tsap ,yana gyara ta fito kicibusss tayi da akwati katuwa da mamaki take kalon shi tace “Yaya Irfan kayan waye wanan ”bai bata amsaba ya shunfamata sallaya hade da doko mata doguwar Riga ta kanti mara nauyi tasaka da hijab alkibla yanuna mata bata Réda ya kaleta ba yakama hanunun Nana sukabar dakin„


Ganin haka tasaka kayan hade da tunani kala kala badai Irfan fushi yayi ba shima ,badai maganar Ammi zaibi ba yayi nesa dasu ina bazata yarda ba so yake sukara shiga cikin fushin iyayen su komi bazai yuba ,gqnin tunanin bazai gyarata ba yasa ta kabarta sallah ,duka tarama sallolin ta hade da yi musu ad'ua Allah ya huci zuciyar su Ammi su yafe musu sosai take Adu'a tana kuka sai dataji kanta yafara ciwo tabari Nana ce tashigo tace “Anty Barcci nakeji kuma tun safe banyi wanka ba Uncle baiyimin ba ”tafada cikin yarinta ,ido Nasreen ta kwalalo tace “oum gaskedai naketa jin wari Ashe kece ke warin”tafada tana dane dariyar ta ganin Yanda ta wani shagwabe fuska kamar zatayi Kuka „


Dariya tayi tac “Haba Babyn Uncle ai ko kwana ishirin tayi batayi wanka ba bata wari ”dariya Nana tayi hade da cewa “yeeee dama Na-san ni ba kazama bace ko Anty?”kai Nasreen tadaga mata hade da d'aura mata towel sukashiga toilettes wanka Tamata hade da kamota sukadawo dakin kayan barccin ta tasaka mata da turare ta doratasaman bed dun ta hade da tofamata adu'oin bacci dai dai lokacin IRFAN yashigo ,zama yayi kusa da ita hade da dora kanshi saman kafadar ta yace “Zamuyi Ne sa dasu Abbi kamar yanda suka bukata zamu barmusu garin gabadaya zamukoma inda baza suyi tunaninmu acanba ,dan Allah kada kce komi Umarni nabaki a matsayina na Mijinki karkice zaki bani hakuri kiyi abunda kawai nace ,ga kayanki nan da Na Nana na hada idan akwai abunda zaki doka to kidauka kafin asuba yaw jirgin mu zai daga karfe biyar zuwa Niamey naso mubar kasar ama bazan iyaba sabida wani dalili ”yafada yana wasa da hanun tadayake cikin nasa


Murmushin doli ta kakalo tayabama fuskar ta tunda yace umarni ne zatabi kawai cikin taushin murya tace “dan Allah kayimin alfarma kabani wayarka na kira Mama ”tafada alamar roko ,bai yimusu ba yamika mata wayar shi Mama takira ringinin bîyu aka dauka “Mama ina yini”tafada a hankali ,daga bangaren Mama ma ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tace “fatan kuna lafiya ko ”

“lafiya klw muke Ina Abba ”ta tanbaya 

Mikawa Abba wayar tayi batare da bata lokaci ba ya karba gaisheshi tayi ya'amasa yana tanbayarta Irfan Mika mishi wayar yayi karba yayi hade da gaisheda Abba „


Magana Abba kayimishi tsawon minti biyar shidai sai dai yagyada kai tamkar Abban nagabansa ,sunjima suna magana sanan yakashe wayar yamike kayan baxcin ta tasaka dokarta yayi hade da dorata saman kirjin shi ,anan dakin suka kwana bisa kafe itakuma Nana a gado ahaka bacci yadaukesu kowa da abunda yake sakawa aranshi „


Shine yafara tashi sanan yatasheta Dakinshi yaje hade da cemata tashiya yanzu ,mamaki yabata ita ba tadauka anfasa tafiyar ba batace komi ba tahada duk sauran abunda zata bukata ,Nana ta tada wacce bacci ke cikin idon ta wanka tamata tasaka mata Riga da wando yan kanti masha Allah hade da dora mata hijab dan karamin sosai tayi Kyau ,Itama wanka tayi hade da saka wata pink dun abaya tafeshe jikin ta da turaruka 


IRFAN ne yashigo cikin wani gin dun wando baki da Farar Riga sai kamshi yake zubawa Nana ce ta rungume shi da gudu tace “Ye Uncle kayi Kyau ama kasan da nafika Kyau ko ”cewar Nana cikin dariya har hakwaranta dasuke jere suka bayana ,shima yar dariya yasake yace “ina koda gami kinsan nafiki haduwa ”shagwabe fuska tayi taje wurin Nasreen datake kalonsu kamar ta hadiyesu dan bakaramin Kyau sukayimata ba leek father leek daughter tafada a ranta ,Nana ce takatse mata tunanin tace “Anty Nida Uncle wayafi wani kyau”

“kinfi shi Kyau baby babu ma gami ”Nasreen tafada tana kunshee dariyar ta ganin irin kalon da Irfan ke mata ,“yeeee kaji ko nafika kyau”Nana tafada tana masa gwalo ,dariya kawai yasaki yakaraso wurin Nasreen kiss yabata a baki yace “Kinyi Kyau sosai Matar DOCTOR ”dariya tadanyi sanan tace “Ina kwana ”amsawa yayi hade da juyawa yadauko sallaya ya shinfida musu ganin lokacin Salla nayi 


Anan sukayi sallah su ganin lokaci na tafiya sukayi adu'oin su akwatinan su yaja yasaka amota sanan yajawo nashi fita sukayi daga dakin Nasreen nakare mishi kalo dan batasan lokacin da wawowar su ba „


Kule part dun yayi mai gadi ne yazo yaduka yanamasa barka da fitowa kudi ya jimko da baisan adadin su ba yamika mishi yace “ga kudi nan Na-san zasu isheka har tsawon shekara biyu kje ka kama sana'a zamuyi tafiya ama zaka iya zama acikin gidan ka kula dashi nabar maka amana ”yafada yana kokarin shiga cikin motar 


Mai gadin ne wato habu yace “nagode Alhaji Allah yakara girma Allah yatsare ”daga haka yaje yabude masa kofar ganin yatada motar „


Aéroports sukaje Wanda ko ina haske ne ga mutane nan wasu na dawo sa wasu na tafiya ,bisa wata kujera yanunamusu suka zauna kafin a fara Kiran mutane shikuma wani lungu yabi, yabar su nan zaune Nana ce ma mai surutun itadai Nasreen hankalin ta sam ba akwance yakeba yanzu shikenan zasu bar kasar hahuwar su„


Yakima kafin yadawo jin anfara kiran sunan matafiya yasa su mkewa sukaje Nana da Nasreen kujerar su daya kowa datashi sai Irfan shi da wani abayan su Nasreen dun jirginsu ne yalula gajimare „


Nasreen kai ajiyar zuciya ta saule hade da wasu irin hawaye masu zafi shikenan tabar komi nata ,kasar ta ,dangin ta ,Karatunta,komi tabari share hawayen tayi batare da tabari Nana tagani ba da yanzu zata sata gaba da tanbaya 

Irfan kuwa duk yana lura dasu yasan dama haka zata faru kawai dai yashere ta ne„


Basu saukaba saï karfe goma ababan Birnin Niamey„

Suna sauka aka dauko musu kayan su taxi suka dauka ,mai taxi ne ya ajesu wata unguwa wacce kalo daya zakayi mata kafahimci ta talakawa ce ,gidajen gidan lakane masu dan halin ne zaka Gansu da andre dai ai wani lungu ya ajesu wada kwata ce tako ina da dauda a wurin „


Kayansu mai taxi dun nan yashiga dauka yana shi da dasu lungu saï dayakai su duka sanan Irfan yaja hanun Nana ganin haka ysa Nasreen take musu baya wace take nema tayi amai sabida daudar wurin da wari ,sundan shiga lungu suka ci karo da wani kyaure Wanda yake abude saï mutane dake kai kawo a cikin gidan kuma tun shigowar su ko ina mutane ne har wasu yara ke biye dasu kamar sunga turawa „


Suna shiga irin gidagen nan ne ajere kusan sunkai goma mutumin naga ba ,suna bayanshi Nasreen ce ta shiga gaisheda mutanan wurin ama IRFAN ko kallo basu isheshi ba dakin dayake karshe Wanda nan ya ajemusu kayansu ya shiga suma shiga sukayi irin gidan nan ne mai dan hili sai dan karamin falon sai dakuna biyu Wanda duk siminti ne kudi yalalibo yabashi godiya yashiga yi masa bai ce komi ba yaleka dakin hade da tofah aduu'oi sosai yajima yana adu'a sanan ya fito yakali Nasreen da Nana dasuka takure wuri aya saï kallon gidan suke kamar sunga kashi wani irin tausayinsu ne ya kamashi yasan Nasreen bata saba ba duk da kuwa ba wani hali ne dasu ba amma dole zataji wani iri bare ma Nana Wace tayi Rayuwa a Turai ,bata san wannan rayuwar ba sam„„„


Gambusnu yadan karo ganini Irin kalon da'akemusu .........✍️

BOOK 2 
PAGE 19&20
            .........✍️tsintsiyar dake wurin Wace ga dukan alamu sabuwa ce yadauka hade da Jan takunkumin shi sama ya rufe hancin sa „

Baice musu kala ba yashiga Falon dakin daya yashiga Kasancewar da bayi a dakin daya yadawo waje „

Inda suka isko wasu kayan sababi irin robar wanka taburma tsintsi da wasu matalu guda biyu gudar baba ce sai Dayar karama ce da sauran kayan bukata irin su tukunya da Murhu„

Bokitin yadauka yashiga dashi Nasreen kai bakaramin mamaki wanan al'amarin yake bataba ba badai yana nufin awanan unguwar zasuyi rayuwa ba ,a wanan dajin, wanan kazamin gidan Jitayi Nana na fadun “Anty mi mukeyi awanan akulkin kajin ji duk datti ne please kicewa dady yazo mu koma sabon gida ”tafada tana kokarin Jan hanun Nasreen wai subar wurin „„


Nasreen kai tama rasa abun fadi mizata ce to wani murmushin takaici ne yakasheta bata ce komi ba ta dauko yar kujerar tsugunin datagani ta nunawa Nana ta zauna itakuwa dakin tashiga jin karar ruwa adakin daya yasa ta shiga nan ganinshi tayi yana kokarin wanke bayin  kalonshi ta tsaya yi ,shikuwa ganin ta yasa yadaure fuska„


Bata kara shan Mamaki ba saidataga yana kokarin rabata ya wuce hanun shi tarike ido cikin ido take kalonshi tace “ban fahimci mikake nufi ba badai kana nufin a wanan kangon zamu zauna ba ?”tafada tana kalonshi ido cikin ido „

Murmushi yasaki sai kuma ya gimtse yace “wanan shine gida na a yanzu kinyi alkawarin zaki 
Zauna dani a kowane hali toga halinda na tsinci kaina shin zaki zauna dani ahakan ko kuwa nasakeki kitafi kiyi Rayuwa mai dadi ne ”yafada shima yana kalonta fuskar nan tashi har tayi jajur alamar baccin Rai 


Tass tass kakeji Nasreen ta dauke Doctor da Mari idon ta nazubo da hawaye ta durkushe kasa gabanshi cikin kuka tace “kaico na ,ina tausayawa Rayuwata Ashe yada nadaukeka ba haka kadaukeni ba,Ashe akwai Ranar da zakayi tunanin ni Nasreen zangujemaka ,Ashe akwai Ranar da bakinka zai furta kalmar sai kasakeni abakin ka ,banyi zato ba da bazan zauna dakai ba da banbiyoka kaba ,dabazan zauna dakaiba bazan yarda da wanan kaddara takaba Ashe nayi kuskure Wanda nadoki yarda ta nabashi Ashe shi sam bai yarda dani ba ,Allah banida kowa sai kai Allah kaga zuciya ta kakawomin dauki A rayuwata banida wani tsumi ko dubara sai taka ”sosai take kuka babu kyakutawa„


Wani irin irin tausayin tane yaji yakama shi tabaro komi da kowa nata sabida shi miyasa yafurta mata haka durkusawa yayi gabanta yana kalon ta yarasa mizai ce kalonshi tayi hade da rike gaban rigarshi cikin fushi da bacin Rai tasake cewa “basaki na zakayi ba Irfan to sakeni ,kasake ni nace kasakeni....”tafada tana dukan kirjinshi hade da rungume shi tana kuka wiwi „


Tajima tana kuka sosai harda majina akirjinshi duk tabata masa gaban Riga da hawaye da Majina “Kiyi kuka bazan hanaki ba Nasreen komi nine nake jawo miki shi,bakisan komi ba sai farin ciki ama tunda kika hadu dani kulum cikin tashin hankali kike kiyimin duk hukuncin da kike so ko zuciyar ki zatayi sanyi ”sosai takara fashewa da kuka kamar yazugata 


Shima da zaisamu yayi kukan dayaji dadi ama tayaya saida tasha kukanta ta koshi sanan ta tashida daga kirjinshi takara kai masa dan mari
kadan sanan ta goge hawayen ta tace “shikenan na huce”tafada tana kalonsa ,shima kalon ta yayi hade da sakin murmushi yace “Nagode ma Allah shikenan ko ,yanzu sai kiwankemin rigata jiyanda Kikabatamin gaban Riga da majina da hawaye”

Kamar yace tayi ta kara goge fuskar ta da rigarshi Dariya yadanyi kadan yace “zan rama wataran ,kinsan ai koni banyiba y'a y'a na zasu rama min kijira lokaci”yafada yana radamata akune shagwabe fuskar ta yi tace “Nikuma narama gareka ba”cakul culu yashigayimata yace “sai dai kirame yarinya”dariya sukeyi abinsu „.


Tsintsiyar tadoka hade da fara wanke bayin yaso hanata ganin bata ida warkewa ba ama taki doli tare sukayi aikin Wanda kafin sukare yayita raki yana banshi ciwo itakuma sai dariyar shi take hade da fadun “kadan Dana kaji dai kundauka aikin nan banza ne wallahi komu muna aikatuwa ina dalili”sosai suka wanke toilettes dun hade da gyara dakunan ,dama yasa ansayo musu ledar daki shun fudata suka yi ko ina kuma sai ta yi dai dai kayansu suka shiga ajewa daya dakin kayansu suka saka aciki na Kitchen dayan kuma matalunsu ne aciki da kayansu nasawa da turarunkasu suka jera sai karfe daya suka kare „


Nana kuwa tasamu game bata masan sunayi ba 

Kalonshi tayi agajiye tace “to yanzu ina zansamu wuta nasaka turaren wutar kaga banzo da sharbon magic ba”kalon ta yayi cikin so da kauna yace “to bari nafita naje nasamo miki”da Sauri tamike tace “a'a kabari nizanje bana so wa'inan matan da yan matan na wata takalemin kai kayimusu nisa ”tafada tana daga masa gira sama hade da dokar abun turaren wutar ta fita„


Irfan kai hankalinshi yamaida kan Nana ita yake kalo a zahiri ama abadini tunanin rayuwar da yazabar musu yakeyi ,yadibo iya adadin kudin daya keso kuma bazai kara taba accunt dinshi ba dan yasan za'a iya bibiyar shi asamo inda yake ,wayar shi ma anjuma zaikashe duk dayaga miss call dayawa ama baiduba ba„


Nasreen kuwa tana fita idon kowa yayi kanta har sai data rasa nutsuwar ta ama dataimakon adu'a tadai daita salama tamusu da Sauri wata bakar mata katuwa Wace akala zata tsofe Mama tace “Sannu yar nan ,kune bakinmu ko sannunku ,sunana asabe ,wa'inan kuma y'a y'a nane da Matayen su munan duka famille ne”tafada tana wage jajaye hakwaranta dasuka cika da goro ba kyan gani „


Murmushi Nasreen tadan yi hade da dukawa dan kasa kadan tace “Ina wuni iya Asabe dama wuta nake so kozan samu ”tafada tana karema duk mutanan wurin kalo Wanda ya wanci Wanda ke wurin surukan Asabe ne da jikokin ta kasancewar safiya yasaka y'a y'a nata maza tafiya aiki„


Washe hakora Iya Asabe tayi hade da cewa “ke hafse tashi ki eboma Bakuwa Wuta ko Girki zata d'aura ”wada aka kira da hafse ce tamike wata doguwa baka kamar kakarta baza'akirata da mumuna ba ama kuma ba kyakyawa bace „

Tashi tayi tana karai raya kamar zata karye ita a la doli yanga take Wanda saura kadan Dariya ta kubucema Nasreen „


Bata jimaba takawo wutar ansa tayi hade dayin godiya ta yi sashin su ,Yada tabarsu haka tasa mesu turaren wutar ta mai kanshi tasaka ko ina saï kanshi yakeyi „


Jin ana Kiran sallah yasashi shiga toilettes ya yi wanka yana fitowa yasaka jalabiyar sa fara kal Wace takarbi jikin shi sosai saï yafito kamar wani balarabe turare yafeshe jikin shi dashi yana fitowa yaga Nasreen na bacci kyaleta yayi yasan tagaji idan yadawo bata tashiba zai tasheta „


Shikuwa Doctor yana fita kowa ya yi ca suna gaisheshi “lafiya”kawai ya'ansa yayi gaba abunsa„„

Nasreen kuwa tajima tana bacci har kusan la'asar yadawo hannun shi dauke da ledodi sai wani mutum dake biya dashi da buhu daya „

Ajewa yayi yabiya mutum shikuwa da salama yashigo ama shiru ,Nasreen sai shara bacci take ga Nana waya akasa Wanda ga dukan alamu bacci ne yadauke ta bata shirya ba„

Zama yayi bisa matalar dake falon ,kwanciya yayi hade da kalon fuskar Nasreen dun ,bacci take hankali kwance hannun sa yadora saman cikin ta cikin ranshi kuwa fata yakeyi Allah yabashi wasu y'ayn Masu albarka ,yasan Nasreen dinshi mai hankali ce koda taji cikin yazube kuma tasan sanadin Ammi ne hankalin ta bazai tashiba ama yasan zata boye abun cikin ranta hannu yaji ta doramishi afuska hakan yasashi dawo wa hayacinsa kalonshi take ido cikin ido tana karantar Abun dake damunsa ama takasa gano minene bayan damuwa su Ammi ta bata akwai wani Abu „


Fuskar shi yakai dai dai tata yace “Miye kike kalona haka kona karamiki Kyau ne ”murmushi itama tayi tace “wane kai ai banga kyawun ba ,munin daka kara ne yasani kalon ka”kai kawai ya jinjina hade da kwantawa rigin gine ganin tashiga bayin alamar al'wala zatayi „

Yanana kwance tafito tada sallah tayi ganin ana la'asar yasashi tada Nana ya dauro alwala shima ya wuce massalaci „

Nassreen kuwa sallah sukayi itada Nana bayan sunkare ne suka zauna Jan carbi Irfan ne yashigo ya zauna yana kalonsu sai da suka kare suka shafa atare Nana ce ta matso kusan shi tace “Uncle ina missing dun su dady harda Papa ma yaushe zamuje Ganin su?”kalon juna sukayi sanan Irfan ya koma kalon Nana yace “bansani ba Nana ama nasan wataran zasu neme mu„„

Baikara magana ba ,sai jin murya ta yayi takara cewa “to Uncle kadauke mu daga wanan gidan na talakawa sam gidanmu yafi wanan kyau”tsawa yabuga mata hade dacewa “Nana bana son iskanci fah su talakawa ba mutane bane kinsan ma miye talaka miye mai kudi?”yafada yana zaro mata ido kuka ta fashe dashi hade da boyewa bayan Nasreen „


Shikuwa bai kulasu ba yamike hade da dauko daya daga cikin ledodin dayazo dasu ,kwano yadauko yazuba abincin Wanda gari rogo ne da tamaka sauran kayan yaduko ya saka mai da magi da tomate ya ya mutsa shi bai kalesu ba ko kadan Nasreen kuwa sai aikin rarashi takeyi ,itafa bata gane abun nan na Doctor ba, bayan wanan kangon yanzu kuma abincin ma ga abunda zasu komawa ci ,hmm zata samai ido tagani duk abunda yakawo zata sarashi suci tunda haka yakeso „

Yana idawa ya tura gabansu hade da fara ci Nasreen kai hannu tasaka tafara ci ama Nana sam taki ganin haka yasashi Harar ta ba shiri tasa hanunun ta tana ci tsaki yaja yacigaba da cin abincin sa Nassreen dai bata ce kalaba sai cin abunta take dan yamata dadi sosai„


Nana kuwa kadan kadan takeci daga karshe ma À mai tafara sai Nasreen ce tayi waje da'ita takarasa sai data amaye kaf abincin sanan hankalin ta ya kwanta ,Irfan kuwq KO kalo basu isheshi ba sai dayaci yakoshi yasha Ruwa hade da hamdallah„

Nasreen kuwa kasancewar tanada dan biscuits inta data kesawa yana sayo mata daga baya tazo dashi dama sabida bukatar hakan shitawa Nana taci Wace har zazabi yafara sauko mata „

Ganin Marece nayi ta gyara gidan duk da dazu sukagama gyaran Doctor kuwa yafita batasan ina yajeba baidawo ba sai karfe goma koda yake zawa Nana tayi baccin wahala Wanda dakyar tasamu naira Dari tabada akasawo mata paracetamol tasha shine bacci yadauketa ,Nasreen kuwa takasa bacci tana tunanin Rayuwar d'à ta tsinci kanta ciki taji salamar sa ,amsawa tayi hade dayimasa sannu da zuwa..✍️

PAGE 21&22
            .........✍️Zama yayi bakin Matalar dake falon hade da fadun “Alhmdllah” Nasreen dai batace tak ba sai mafificin datake hitama Nana kasancewar babu ko fanka adakin „

Kalon ta yayi hade fadun “kidaina shagwaba Nana doli itama tasaba da Canjin Rayuwa ”daga haka yamike yashige toilettes Nasreen dai batace cikanka ba sai ido databishi „


Wanka yayi hade dasaka kayan bacci falon yadawo yaduko ledar dayazo da ita Wanda tsirai ne aciki cikin palt yasaka hade da turama Nasreen ,bata kaleshi ba tace“wanan banamu bane narigada nasaba ka ajewa Nana idan tatashi ”daga haka takwanta hade da juya masa baya ,


Yasan Al'amarin shine ke Sakata cikin wanan yanayin ama yazatayi sai hakuri hakan shine dai dai ,rufe shi yayi hade da rufe kofar yadawo bayan ta yakala hanun shi yamakalo acikin ta „

Cikin nata yashiga shafawa a hankali ya rada mata “Kiyi hakuri da yanayin Mijin ki ,Na-san kina hakuri dani kikara ,Kulum nine matsalar ki kulum nine silar shiga kuncinki Nasreen nikuwa wane irin miji nazama mai son kansa, kulum sabida ni kike shiga damuwa daga wanan sai wanan ”


“Nasreen Ammi ta muna asarar cikin mu banajin Ammi tamin dai dai itace silar komi ,Itakan wacce irin Uwa ce ,idan har baki yafeba zan nemo miki hakkin ki ”yafada yanajin wani iri dagaske idan bata yafeba zai nemo mata hakkin ta ,Uwa Uwa ce ama akwai laifin da sukeyi Wanda yake kauce hanya ,itama tanada hakki da cikin itadun Uwa ce„


Shashekar kukan ta yaji sosai hankalin shi yatashi baya son jin kukan ta itace farin cikin sa ,itace rayuwar sa itace duniyar sa gabakidaya Idan babuta bayajin zai saké rayuwa adoron kasa „

Nasreen kuwa tarassa wane irin kuka zatayi Son da Doctor kemata har yakai haka tasan babu wani da aduniyar nan dabaya son Mahaifiyar sa ,tasan duk abunan daya faru yana son Mahaifiyar sa ama dan yanuna mata itama mai inci ce yafadi haka bawai dan yana so ba tasani itakan yazatayi idan baya raye sam bazata iya rayuwa babushi ba yazama ita tazama shi ,Juyowa tayi yazamana suna fuskantar juna kalon shi tayi tace “Ammi mahaifiya tace kobaka raye ,da itada Mama basuda banbanci a Wuri na ,Na yafema Ammi Na-san kuskure ne batamasan da cikin ba nayafe mata wallahi ban riketa da komi ba Allah yanufa cikin bazaizo duniya ba Allah ubangiji yabamu masu albarka yarayamana Nana,kaima ka hakura Karkasa abun cikin Ranka Ammi mahaifiyar muce Na-san zata nememu duk inda take Na-san hankalin ta yanzu atashe yake please Yaya Irfan mukoma cikin danginmu”tafada tana shafa fuskar shi hawaye naci gaba da gangaro mata „


Doctor kuwa shiru yayi yana kalon yanda abar kaunar nashi ke zubda hawaye ,bakinshi yasa hade da zura harshenshi yafara lashe hawayen ,saman ta ya haw yazamana itace a kasa yana sama kissing dinta yashiga yi ,cikin salon shi mai mantar da ita duniyar datake sosai yafara fidata a hayaccin ta ganin bai dace ba ga Nana zata iya farka wa tagansu yasa shi daukar ta hade da cirema ta Kayan ta gabadaya shima cire nashi Yayi kurya dakin suka shiga yaci gaba da Rudata Nasreen kuwa hadin kai tabashi dan anjima ba'a hadu ba, cikin kunan ta ya rada mata “yarinya kidage yau kai sai nadasa kwai na daya bare,yaw yan Hudu zansaka dan haka bana son raki duk da bansanki dashi ba kikara kaimi muji dadin mu”yafada yana mai lasar cibiyar ta ya gangaro har kasan ta ya shiga yimata salon shi Nasreen kuwa jin abunda yakemata yasata kara rudewa itama zaune tatashi hade da kama bananar sa tasaka a bakin ta Wace tamike sambal sai haniniya takeyi tana neman abincin ta „


Tsotsar ta takeyi a hankali tana kai dakawo ,Irfan kuwa yafara fita daga hankalin shi idan Nasreen na tsotsar shi jiyake kamar sec dun ne ma yakeyi da ita „


Ta iya tsotsar banana gogan kuwa gashin kanta da yabarbaje yarike hade da kara turamata bananar sai gwatso yake buga mata „


Zufah kadai ke karyo masa tako ina gashi kodan fanka babu ,Doctor fah yarasa wane irin dadi yakeji ,kwantar da ita yayi hade da Budé kafafun ta ya shiga suking dinta wani irin Abu takeji yana gangaro mata Wanda tanada yakinin Ni'imar tace shikuwa Doctor baki yakai yashiga tsotse Ruwan dake gangarowa kamar anbude fanfo„


Suduka duk sunbi sun fice ahayacin su  goho yasa tamasa yashiga a hankali ahankali yakeyi sabida jinta amatse sosai shikan Ya godewa Allah dayabashi Mata kamar Nasreen gwatso yafara bugama mata babu sauki bankareta yayi yana susukar ta kukan dadi Nasreen ta fara dan tanajin dadin wanan style d'un ama da wahala yake dan duk sai kafafun ka sunyi ciwo„


Irfan kai zufa dai yakeyi hade da aikin bugun ta yana kamo Nonuwan ta daga kasa yana matsawa sosai yakejin dadi na kwasar shi ,kusan awa daya yana aikin cacakar ta ,juyota yayi hade da saka pénis dinsa tsakankanin nonuwan ta ya hade wuri guda yashiga kai dakawo sosai yake gwatso a tsakn kanin nonuwan ta idan yanayi sai Abar sa natabo bakinta itakuwa idan takawo sai tana zura harshen ta tana lasa dan kan Wanda ruwa ke dan tstsafo musu„


Kara canja style yayi hade da kwantawa bayan ta yasakata atsakiya yashiga aikin cinta sosai sukejin dadin su Kusan awa biyu da yan mintutuka yafara wani irin gurnani Wanda bata taba jin yayi ba “yeeeh yeesh ashh wai dadi wai Dadi Nasreeen pénis dina zai tsinke”yafada yana mai kara karfin hwatson shi „

Wasu irin ruwa masu Zafi taji sun ratsa marata alamar yayi relasing sai daya tabatar da yazube mata ruwan jarabarsa gabadaya sanan yadan kwanta asaman ta kamar yanda yakeyi koda yaushe kalon fuskar ta yayi Wace itama sai nufanshin take saukewa yace “kona kara ne”yafada yana lasar gemun ta ,murmushi yayi ganin dan Harar sa datayi yasake cewa“yanzu na yan biyu ne na zuba ,saura na wasu biyun kibari nakara kinji Baby na”yafada yana mai cigaba da tsotsar kirjin ta wanka suke amike  suna kalon shi tsakan kanin Nonon ta yashiga hade da lasar wurin sama yamaidota yazamana itace a sama shi akasa yace “oya yanzu ke zakimin kece zaki motsa mu”yafada yana mai cigaba da matsar Nonuwan ta „


Nasreen kuwa mijin nata yana bata mamaki shidai baya gajiya da sex bata karasa tunanin ta ba taji yamaido kasanta dai dai bakinshi itakuma tana dai dai bananar sa tsotsar ta yashiga yi itama kuma takama bananar sa da yan marainan sa biyu tashiga tsotsar kamar tasamu halawa Yau dai sunci juna sosai sun gurji juna sai kusan karfe uku yasarara mata hade da yin al'wala yarungume abar sa suka shiga bacci mai dadi„


Da asuba tashinsu yayi shikuwa ya wuce massalaci ,sukma sallah sukayi hade da zama suna istigfari „

Irfan kuwa sai karfe bakwai yadawo tun daga shigarshi yaga layi alamar toilettes akema wanan layin„

Ya godewa Allah dayasa suna da bayinsu aciki har ta kashi ganin iya Asabe tana ta sababi wa jikokin nata yasa shi gaisheta yasan zatayi warin Ammin shi kuma ta girmeta ,washe bakitayi tana ansawa ,Hafse ce tayi zarbule tace “Yaya ina kwana”Doctor bai juyowa kuma bai ansa gaisuwar ba dan yafahimci sam yaran basada tarbiya kuma wai yaya kodaga gidan wa yazama yayan ta oho„


Nasreen kuwa suna Kare sallah ta shiga gyaran gidan duk da bawata kura yayi ba Tass ta gyara komi ,Doctor kuwa yana shigowa ya zauna bisa taburma yacigaba da Jan carbinsa„

Nana ce tafito daga dakin hade da rabewa a jikin bangon ganin kamar tsoron shi take shi kuma baya so yaran shi suna tsoron shi yana so suzama tamkar kawaye ne bawai yazame musu dodo ba ,“Zonan Nana”yafada yana nunama ta kusa dashi ,zawa tayi a dadare rungume ta yayi akirjin shi yace“Miye ne Babyn Uncle”yatanbaya 


Hawaye ne suka fara sakomata tace“Kayi hakuri Uncle bazan saké ba Anty Nasreen tace banyi dai dai ba kuma babu kyau banbancin tsakanin mai kudi da talaka ,kayi Hakuri Uncle bazan saké ba ”tafada tana kama kunan ta ,Yaji dadi sosai shibaisan mizaiyi yasakawa Nasreen ba bayada haufi akan ta yanada yakini ko mutuwa yayi y'ayan sa bazasuyi rashin tarbiya ba zata zame musu ginshikin Rayuwa„


Murmushi yayi yace “yes baby girls ba kyau karkisake kinji kuma na hakura ni bana fushi dake”


Dariya tayi hade da cewa “Uncle kabani Tuwan jiya zanci yunwa nakeji”dan kalon ta yayi sanan yace “bari Antyn ki tazo”Nasreen kuwa tana gama wanka tasaka wasu Riga da wando Wanda sukayi matukar amsar jikin ta saï kamshi take zubawa„

Tun kafin takaraso kanshi humrar datasaka yadaki hancinsa „


Tundaga shigowar ta yadaga mata hannu alamar wanka yayi murmushi tayi itama hade dakashe masa ido daya akamar godiya wani salon sai massoyan„

Zama tayi kusanshi itama hade da rungume shi gefe daya tace “Mufah yunwa mukeji”tafada tana shagwabe masa murmushi yayi yana son komi na Nasreen dinshi komi tayi kyau yake mata....✍️

PAGE 23&24
            .........✍️Nana ce tace “Uncle yunwa mukeji”dubu daya yabawa Nasreen hade da cewa “gashi kuje kusiyo naga ana tuyar waina ko kossai abakin titin nan ”karba tayi hade da mikewa tasaka hijab har kassa takama hanun Nana suka fice 

Irfan ma wanka yashiga hade da zama cikin dan falon nasu ya fudo computer dinshi yafara duba abunda zai duba“

Su Nasreen kuwa suna fita su iya Asabe suka fara kalonsu suna washe baki har kasa Nasreen ta duka tagaishe da ita ,“a'a Iya bakuwa mukayi jiya Kenan ”wani bakin mutum Wanda akala zaikai shekaru talatin da tara dan Irfan ma zai tsoheshi ,sai dai yafi Irfan dun tsufah a zahiri sabida wahala yafada yana washe baki„

“Eh Saminou ai bakinmu ne itada yar tane da mijin ta”Iya Asabe tabashi ansa „

Nasreen kuwa bayan haka bata kara cewa komi ba taja hannun Nana sukayi gaba ,shikuwa Wanda aka kira da Saminou sai washe hakora yakeyi yana leken Nasreen duk databace ma ganinsa„


“gaskiya tahadu Irin wanan cika haka”yafada yana lashe baki kamar tsohon maye ,tsaki Hafse taja tace “ai kawu sai ma kaga Mijin nata kamar bature hmm gidan nan akwai camama”tafada tana hararen kofar da Nasreen tabi

Nasreen kuwa basu masan sunayi ba sukaje suka siyo kosan su zama sukayi sukaci sosai ,Nasreen ce tamike hade da dora murahu sauran kudin dayayi saura Nera dari Biyar tace masa zataso itace da Sauran kayan miyan da babu to kawai yace mata tafita ,kamar kulum su Asabe na filin gida cikin ladabi ta tanbayesu inda ake Saïda kayan miya da itace “Mari tashi kinuna mata ”Asabe tafada tana nuna wata yar dumama dumar yarinya Wace zatayi shekaru goma sha takwas kusan taso ta tsohe Nasreen dunma„ Wace aka kira da Mari mikewa tayi hade da cewa to tadauki hijab dinta tashiga gaba Nasreen nabayanta ,kadan sukayi tafiya suka ISO wurin mai kayan miyan tumatir ne dama baisiya ba da atarugu shine tasiya sai iatce ,Mari dakanta ta daukar mata iccen duk dakuwa Nasreen tahana ama tace ita zata dauka „


Suna bisa hanyar komawa gida Mari tace “gashi nibanmasan sunan ki ba sis”tafada tana yar dariya „


Murmushi Nasreen tayi tace “Aya Sunan Nasreen yarin yata kuma Nana Firdausi sai Kuma Miji na”tafada tana mai cigaba da Murmushi„

“Masha Allah suna mai dadi ,ama gaskiya ba Wanda zai ganki yace kin haifi kamar Nana”yanzu kai dariya Nasreen tayi tace “Allah ko, kowa haka yake fada dai”daga haka tayi shiru sabida bata son falasa Assirin Mijin ta barema gawasu bare Wanda bata san halinsu ba„


Mari ce tasake cewa“gaskiya sis ina son muyi kawance nikinga sunana Mari ,Nice Autama agidan mu, iya Asabe itace mahaifiyar mu, Mahaifin mu Malan Dauda yarasu shekaru goma sha biyu dasuka wuce mu tara ne a wurin Mahifin mu kuma wanan gidan na gado ne ,kuma dakuke zaune wanan gidan gidan Yaya Umar ne yace shibazai zauna damu ba sabida Matar shi muguwa ce batada kirki tana so tarabamu dashi mafarin da'aka raba shi yasamu gidan dakuke shine yasiyaar ku kuma kuka kama Haya ,Yayuna Biyar duk Maza zaune suke da matayen su da y'a yan su sai Yayuna biyu Mata dasukayi Aure sai Nida yaya ta daya wato Jamila ama ita tana gidan Kawunmu Souleymane kanan mahaifinmu dama tun tana yarinyar yakarbeta saidai tazo mana hutu”tafada dai dai lokacin dasuka shigo gidan„


Shiru kawai Nasreen tayi bata tankaba dan batasan mizatace ba ,kwankwasa wa Nasreen  tayi, Nana ce tabude tana fadun “Sannu da zuwa Anty Nana”da yawa Nasreen ta amsa „


Irfan kuwa baidago ba barre yasanan ba itakadai bace, Saijiyayi ana gaisheshi baidago ba yace “lafiya”daga haka yacigaba da aikin shi „

Mari kuwa bataji dadin yanda ya'ansa mata ba ama bata nunaba sai yar dariya datasaki tace to ni zantai islamiya idan kinada bukatar wani Abu kuma su iya Asabe magana zasu nunamiki ”godiya Nasreen tayi hade da cewa to insha Allah„


Murashun ta tahurama wuta tashiga yanka Albasa dan tana so tayi sandwich kasancewar Irfan yasiyo naman „


Naman ta wanke sosai sanan tasakashi acikin Léda hade dasa yazuzuka miya tayayanka poivrons kanana kanana,sai Albasaa da tayanka tahada da persil da céleri da Magi acikin ledar ta kule mikewa tayi hade dasaka hijab dinta „


Hafse tagani zaune tana aiki shine ta tanbayata inda ake nika ,dokar nikan Hafse tayi hade da amsar kudin taje akayimata nikanshi„


Nasreen kuwa ciki takoma Irfan ne yakeleta yace “ina nikan badai har kindawo ba”zama tayi bisa kujera mai tsuguni tace “a'a Hafse ce ta ansa taje tamin”bai kara magana ba itama bata tankaba „

Macroni tafada tadora Kenan Hafse takawo mata nikan godiya tamata itakuwa daga can bakin kofa take cema Nana “taso muje kigani”tafada tana kalon Nana da takali Baban ta shikuwa duk dayaji ama har yanzu ko baidago ba „


Ganin Nana taki tashi yasa Nasreen cewa “Tashi kije mana Babyn Uncle”kamar kuwa jira take tamike Hafse takama hanun ta suka fita „


Nasreen kuwa wani murhu tasake dorawa tasaka nikaken namanta tasaka yan Ruwa kadan cikin tukunyar dayaji kadan tabarshi kamar minti goma tarika motsawa sai dayafara tabshi ta saukeshi hade dasaka dan mai atukunya bayan yayi zafi tamaida nikaken namanta ciki tana dan jujuya wa hade d'à saka tumatur gongoni tarufe shi bayan minti biyu tasaka saka dan mai sosai kamshi yacika da gidan „


Irfan kuwa bakaramin hade miyau yaketayi ba ganin har yanzu abincin baikareba y'a aje computer shi yamatso kusan ta yace “Nifah ban karyaba kuma yunwa nakeji kinbi kincika makotanki da kamshi karkidaw alhakin su tunda babasu ba zakiyi”yafada yana sa cokali yadibi sandwich dun kasancewa tasauke kasa ido ya lumshe yana gyada kai alamar yayi dadi„


Nasreen kuwa dadi taji ganin y'a yaba mata macaroni tasaka akula sandwich dun ma tkai falon su Irfan kuwa dama yashiga kurya dakin tsp tasake gyara wuriin turare tasaka acikin daki ko ina sai kanshi yakeyi 


Kurya dakin tashiga tatar dashi zaisake wanka ya d'aura towel akugunshi kalon shi tayi ganin yaki shiga toilettes dun yatsaya kalon ta “miye ne wai bawanka zakayi ba kaje man”tafada tana gunguni kasa kasa „


Irfan kuwa baikara magana ba yashiga toilettes dun Nasreen kuwa kayanta ta cire sanin itadaya ce yasata cire komi kawai saiji tayi anyi sama da ita wustil wutsil tafara ama yaki kulata sai cikin bayin y'a ajeta hade kama Nonon ta daya yasaka abaki harde sauke ajiyar zuciya Nasreen kuwa wani zumm taji ,tsotsa yake ahankali yana matsa dayan itadai gashi yasakar musu Shower ruwa sai jikasu sukeyi„


Nasreen kuwa kanshi takama sosai tashiga shafawa ganin yadaga kafarta daya yasaka akafadar shi hanun shi daya kuma na suking dinta „


Sosai suka fita ahayacinsu sai nishi sukeyi „


Nana sukaji tana Kiran “Anty Nadawo”da Sauri Nasreen tasakeshi shikuwa kamar kara ingiza shi takeyi sai sunsunar ta yakeyi murya tadaga zata karbawa Nana jin sai kiranta take yashi saka bakinshi cikin nata yashiga kissing ,Nasreen kuwa bata so sam Nana ta Gansu ahaka ama shiyakasa fahimta „


Da kyar ta kwaci kanta ta tai Sauri tayi wanka ta fito daure da towel,Nana tagani zaune itada Fai'za sai fira sukeyi Nana na dariya agagauce tasaka kaya ta fita falon„

“Anty inata nemanki ina kikaje ne”ta tanbaya 

“Sannunku inakuka je ne haka”Nasreen tafada tana kauda maganar Nana dan batasan ansar dazata bata ba „


“Anty Hafse ce takaini yawo naga garinsu ”tafada tana dariya dariya sukayi suduka sai ga Irfan yashigo falon d'Aure  da towel Gashin kirjinshi yakwanta luf luf sai Ruwa ke diga „


Fitowar shi Kenan baidauka zaiga Hafse ba saikuwa yaganta kurri tamishi tana kalon gashin kirjinshi daya tafi da imanin ta bata taba Gabin Namiji mai kyawun halitta ba kamar shi ta sha'afa da kalonshi taji anbuga wani irin tsaki Wanda yadawo da ita hayacin ta„


Irfan kuwa ganin irin kalon datake masa ne yasashi buga tsakin yana Harar ta „


Nasreen kuwa duk ta lura hakan yasa ta mikewa tabishi dakin „.....✍️

            .........✍️Matar kuwa sai da Iya Asabe tazo taja hanun ta hade da bata hakuri har tafita tana jaraba„


Nasreen kuwa tsukaken wando ma tasaka da Riga tasaka katon Hijab Irfan takala tace “zanje nasiyo kayan Miya”baidago ba kamar yanda bata jira amsar shiba „

Tayigaba abun ta Tana fita dama matar batayi nisa ba tace“Hai madam kindauka tsoron ki akeji dazaki shigomin har gida ki zazageni Nida Mijina wallahi baki isaba Jaka kawai”tafada tana yi cikin matar hade da Rara mata tabarya da ta boye cikin Hijab dunta„


Pass kakeji ta kwdawa matar tabarya jini ne yabalewa matar Atake mutane sukayi kan ta itama tana kokarin yi cikin Nasreen dun ,Wace takeneman Wani dutsi takara raraka mata „


Mijin Matar ne yazo yashiga ririketa yana zagin Nasreen akan kowa yadaure mata gindi sai yakaita police station yashigar matar sa kamar yadaki Nasreen dasu Iya Asabe ke rike da ita har Hijabb dinta yafadi bata sani ba ido Rufe take fada itama hade da kokarin kwacewa tayi cikin Matar „


IRFAN kuwa dama kamar yasani Yabi bayanta dan yasan ta sarai batada rigima ama bata daukar wulakanci kuma murmushin datayi Wanda jinsa kawai yayi bai kaleta ba yasan akwai rigima shiyasaka shi bin bayan ta „


Mijin matar ne yayi kan Nasreen ganin Matar shi nazubda Jini yakai hannu zai wanketa da Mari yaji anrike hanun ankai masa Marin Tass Tass kakeji Irfan yadaukesa da bahagon Mari Wanda yasashi ganin wuta „


Irfan ne tsaye idon shi jajur alamar baccin Rai Hanun Nasreen ya kwace daga na Asabe ya turata gaban Mutumin yace “Daketa kamar yanda kayi niya bissimillah”yafada yana zarema mutumin ido „


Mijin kuwa wani irin tsoron Irfan da kwarjini shi ne yashigeshi kuma ga zafin Mari har yanzu baidawo dai dai ba „


Allah ya'isa Mutumin yashiga fadi hade dkama hanun Matar sa yana zagin Irfan Wanda yaja hanun Nasreen da karfi baiko tsaya Neman Hijab dinta ba fuskar shi jajur da idon shi dasuka canja kala„


Janta yakeyi har tana neman faduwa gida yashiga da ita ya kule kofar daki yasata hade da turata saman gadon Ranshi abace ,juyowa yayi yafito yabawa Nana Game tashiga murna hade da yin abunta „


Shikuwa Daki yashiga yakule kofar kalon ta yashiga yi tundaga kasa har sama wandon datasaka ya matseta sosai har ana Iya hango Chatin pant dinta ga rigar ma tasama ana ganin kirjin ta kanshi ya girgiza hade da zare belt dun wandonshi„


Nasreen kuwa tsoro ne yashigeta har tafara hawaye ganin yazare belt tadauka dukanta zaiyi hakan yasata rufe fuskar ta  tsorace take fadun “Please Yaya Irfan kayi hakuri bazan saké ba natuba dan Allah karkadakeni bazan karaba”tafada harda fara kuka wiwi „


Irfan kuwa kayanshi kaf yacire hade da hawrawa ssama gadon rigarta yacire yayi sama da ita hade da keta wandon ka karfi yayi rari dashi pant dunama yacire komi haka yabarta yashiga Kissing dinta tako ina babu kakayutatawa a zafafe cikin zafi yakeyin komi Wanda yasa Nasreen kara kuka duk da ba kwarai takeyi ba„

Sosai yashiga Kissing dinta Yajima yana wasa da ita sanan ya ware kafafunta yashiga da karfi yafara having sex da ita a zafafe babu wani tausayi a ayaw Nasreen ta bata masa Rai baisan wane hukunci yakamata yayimata ba ita bayan wanan yaw sai ya gurzata sai tagane Annabi ya faku,duka Bana Namiji bane haka idan yace zaiyimata fada zata Rainashi ne ama idan taji ajikin ta zatayi hankali„


Sosai yake bugamata gwatso itakuwa baiwar Allah sai ture shi takeyi sabida yaw tasan badan suji dadi yayi ba Wanda ita zafima takejin kasan ta namata sabida yada yake tura katuwar Abar sa cikin gindin ta babu tausayi„


Kuka takeyi sosai harda majina ganin idan yayi wanan style dun Yakima yayi wanan yanzu kusan awa biyu ana Abu daya cikin ta taji yana murda mata kuka tafashe dashi “Tace “Dan Allah kayi hakuri Baban Nana bazan saké ba wayo cikina wayo marata”tafada tana rike ciki bai saurara mata ba yaciga ba da bugunta yakusa relasing Kenan yasauka almakashi yadauko Yakoma samanta yaciga gaba da aiki almakashin yasaka yafara datse mata gashinta dashi yayanka mata gashin kadan yabarma mata Nasreen kuwa tariga da tasuma bata masan yanayiba Shikuwa sai dayatabatar da ya gurzata sanan yasauka akanta kanshi yadafe dayakejin yana Sara maasa „

Irfan kuwa bakaramin rudewa yayi ba jin abunda Matar tashi saliha take fada yasan yaw yatabo ta „

“badai dan Nayi fada akan Nana bane ,daga yanzu kanunamin yar kace batawa ba na tsame hanuna daga al'amarin ku”tafada tana mikewa hade da dafe cikin ta tayi toilettes sosai takejin cikin ta na murdawa adu'a tashigayi yi hade da tofah wa aikuwa taji dama dama „


Irfan kuwa naushi yakaiwa bangon dakin zuciyar shi ce take tafar fasa meyasa yayi haka meyasa yama salihar matar shi wanan abun tayi hakuri dashi akomi bai kama ta ba sam abunda yakemata yazama mai zalunci akanta ,shikaran kanshi bazai yafewa kanshi ba idan wani abun yasamu cikin yana son shi baya so yasake rasashi „


Nasreen kuwa tana fitowa bata ko kalli gefen dayake ba tazumbula hijab dinta hade da tada Sallah kasancewar Azahar tayi„

Shima mikewa yayi a raunace ya fito yatarar babu Nana ,yasan yanzu haka tana wajen su Iya Asabe „

Al'wala yayi cikin sanyi ya wuce masslaci ,Ana idawa yazauna yana kai kukan shi ga Allah yasan bakin Ammi ne kebibiyar shi ,sosai yake adu'a harda hawaye sanan yamike yayo gida „


Nasreen tana ganin yafita dama takare sallah al'makashin daya fara datse mata gashin ta tadauka zama tayi hade da aske kan gabadaya yazamana tayi kolo babu komi badai gashin ne bayason gani ba aishikenan zata karasa masa aikin sa„


Dakin ta gyara hade da saka turare taji murya Nana na fadun “Anty nadawo kikayita baccin ku kuka barni ko saida Anty Hafse taje dani unguwa”tafada tana dariya har hakwaran ta suka bayana wata irin tsawa tadaka mata Wace tasata yibaya tana ririke jiki“Bansan Sakarcin banza kinaji ko Ubanwa yace kije yawon”Nasreen tafada tana fido mata ido„


Irfan kuwa tun tsawar data dakama Nana har zagin datayi yaji ,sai akunanshi Murmushi kawai yasaki yasan shi take zagi bai ji ciwo ba kodaya yanzu kan tanuna mishi tabass ta tsame hanun ta daga al'amuran Nana „


Harara ta banka mata hade da shigewa falon „

Nana kuwa tana ganinshi tayi kanshi tana hawaye “Uncle me nayiwa Anty Nasreen ne takemin tsawa harda zagina bayan kuma ba kyau zagi ”kanta yashafa yace “wayafa fadamiki Antyn ki batajin Dadi ne kiyimata Uzuri kinji duk abunda zata miki kiyi hakuri kinji ,”cewar Irfan yana shiga falon„


Nasreen yatarar tasaka Kosan agaba tanaci hankali kwance ,Duk da kuwa shima yanajin yunwar kuma ko dumamen bazai iyaci ba gashi kuma yasan bazatyi Girki ba„


Zama yayi hade da duba wayar sa bai kara kalon gefen taba „


Itakuwa tana karewa tafito Nana tagani rakube abakin kofah wani irin kalo ta watsa mata tace “shi falon miyamiki maza shige ciki ”tafada cikin tsawa Wanda babu shiri Nana tashige ciki tana hawaye ,Irfan bai ji dadi ba sam Ama bazai nuna mata ba ko kashe Nana zatayi kuwa shidayayi hakan badan Nana na yar shibane sai ganin ta dayayi cikin matsatsun kaya mutane na ririketa wata kilama wani ya tatabata shine yabasa Haushi wasu kata sungane masa jikin Mata sanan ga cikinsa baya son yayi asarar sa„


Kai kawai yagyada yaci gaba da abunda yake Nasreen kuwa girki t  dora Wanda yadauka bazatayi ba ,murmushi yayi yace “ai shikenan nidai duk fushinki inda zaki saukemin hakkina aishikenan”


Tana karewa ta juyeshi akula anan ta aje dinar nata tayi tabarmusu nasu tacishige kurya „


Da kalo Yabi ta sai kuma yakali Nana Wace take a tsora ce da Nasreen saukowa yayi yazubama Nana shikuwa sam jiyayi bayamajin yunwar fita yayi yabar su „


Nana kuwa yana fita ta makaleshi tana hawaye “please Uncle karka tafi kabarni Tsoro nakeji”kanta yasha a hankali ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace “babu komi kinji kizauna d Antyn ki zanje nadawo bazatamiki komi ba kuma intamiki tsawa kiyi hakuri kinji tana sonki batada lafiya ne shiyasa OK?”kai kawai tagyadama sa takoma ta takure wuri daya Shikuwa tausayi tabashi ama yazaiyi ko kashe Nana Nasreen zatayi bazai tankaba yasan ita ce Wace tafa ra nunamata soyaya a famille shi bazai manta ba koyanzu yasan tana kaunar ta kuma yanada yakini zata sauko„


Nasreen kuwa sam batajin dadin abunda takewa y'ar tata saidai tanayine dama dan yaji haushi„


Fitowa tayi hade da gyara wurin Ruwa tasaka abokiti tadakama ta tsawa dagudu tazo tubeta tayi hade da yimata wanka ta taje mata kanta hade da samata sabuwar Riga da wando yan Pakistan „


Farfajiyar gidan tafito karo na farko Kenan anan ta zauna suka fara fira dasu Iya Asabe Wanda tayine dan tasa yakara jin haushi „


Irfan da sai bayan Sallah Isha'i yadawo yatarar da ita tsakansu Iya Asabe sai dariyar ta takeyi hankali kwance Ranshi ne yayi wani irin bacewa ama yadane yashige„


Itakuwa dayake kuruciya nadamunta sai kyaleshi da tayi Wanda yasa su Hafse fahimtar Fada sukayi „


Irfan nashiga yatarar da Nana zaune tana gyangyadi tana ganin shi ta yimasa oyoyo pink yabata kumatu hade da zaro Tablettes dun daya siyo mata dan tarika déba mata kewa yamika mata tsale tahaw yi hade da godiya murmushi kawai Yamata ganin tana farin ciki yasashi farin ciki shima kuma kobakomi yaji dadin yada tama Nana wanka yasan kuruciya kedamun Nasreen zaimata Uzuri„


Abincin yabude yaga dai tun na Rana ne gashi yunwa yakeji,huta yafara kokarin hurawa waje yafita yana neman ledodin dazai kamasa wuta „


Nasreen duk taganshi ama tashare dai Hafse ce tatashi cikin karai raya kugu kamar bayan muciya takarba hade da cewa “yalabai bari na kama ba ba girmanka bane ”tsawa yadakamata ba shiri tabar wurin„


Shikuwa tsaki yaja ya hura wutar sa dakyar ya dumama tuwan yasa a plat daya sukaci da Nana Wace kadan ma taci dan game yadauke mata hankali„

Wanka yayi yasaka kayan bacci sa hade  dasama Nana nata Adu'a yamata yatofah mata sanan shima ya kwanta yana tunanin anya Nasreen nada hankali kuwa kusan karfe goma ama har yanzu bata shigo ba„


Nasreen kuwa ita karan kanta tana son tashi sabida surutun Iya Asabe ya fara isar ta „


Sai shadaya tamusu saida safe yashiga part dinsu hade da kule kofar tashiga falon Nana tagani har bacci yadauke ta zama tayi hade dayi mata adu'a tashafa mata kuma duk Irfan na Lure kai kawai yagirgiza yajuyamata baya dama yadaiga shugowar tane „


Itakuma bayan shi tabi da Harare sanan tasaka kayan baccin ta ta kwanta abayan shi„

Duk yanajin ta yayi shiru yaw gashi ga Nasreen dinshi ama acewai ba'akirjin shi take bacci ba shi sam bazai Iya wanan fushin ba juyowa yayi karaf ta rufe idon ta„


Murmushi yayi bayan yakamata tana kalon shi harda Rufe ido ,fuskar ta yake kalo data dan fada kadan sabida kukan data sha jiya„


Kyaleta yayi bacci ne yayi awon gaba dashi ,itakuwa goganyar sam takasa bacci tasaba kulum a lalausan kirji take kwana sam bazata Iya d'aura kanta a saman filo ba„


Sai juyi take tana kalonsa hankali kwance yake baccin sa fuskar sa ta karema Kalo ,Gaskiya tasan datayi dacen Miji kyakyawa mai halaye na kwarai mai kwarjini dubu bacci yakeyi ama kwarjinin sa nanan kusan karfe biyu takasa bacci„


Ganin ba sarki sai Allah a hankali take matsawa kusan shi har tahaw saman kirjinshi a hankali harda ajiyar zuciya fuskar shi takala taga baccin sa yakeyi hankali kwance dan kiss tasakar masa a kirji Wanda yajishi har cikin Zuciyar shi duk abunda takeyi yanajin ta ,yiyayi kamar cikin bacci yake ya rungume ta aikuwa kara lafewa tayi da zumar zata rigashi tashi da asuba batare da ma yasan anyiba„


Shikan yarasa Nasreen dinshi Wace iri ce Anya zai Iya Rayuwa kuwa babuta Allah kadai masani baccinsu sukasha hankali kwance„


Da asuba yarigata tashi kanshi kawai ya girgiza jiyanda ta leme mishi ajiki kamar gawa taji jiki mai laushi,jin yamotsa yasata Motsawa itama tar tasauke idon ta akan nashi ,murmushi yasakar mata itakuma ta murguda masa baki tana Harar bango„


“to Malama inkin gama shan dadin naki kidagani zanyi sallah kinsamu jiki mai laushi sai murza kike kamar kinsamu matala”yafada yana Yin fuska shima„


“wanan jikin karfen danma kasamu ankwanta saman shi”tafada tana mikewa tashige toilettes dun bayan ta Yabi ,jitayi an rungume ta tabaya cikin ta yashiga shafawa a hankali itakuwa kokarin kwacewa takeyi „


“Haba yan Mata na miye wai fushi kike tayi da Mijin naki ,bayan bai kamata ba kin kwashi zunubi mijinki ya kwana yana fushi dake ga mala'iku da suke rubuta wa nidai bance zasu tsinemiki ba ama ai kingane abunda nake nufi ko”Cewar Doctor yana sakar musu shower „


Hawaye Nasreen tafara dan ta tsani ace mala'iku na tsine mata wanka tayi duk da yana cudata ama tasharesa tayi abunta hade da al'wala shima haka ganin sun makara yasa shi jamusu jam'i bayan tatado Nana ma „


Bayan sunkare ne Nana tafara gyangydi “Jeki kwanta dakika tsaya kinama Mutane gyangyadi ”cewar Nasreen tana dan Harar Nana Wace tun kafin takarasa tamike tahaye Matalar „


Sukuwa carbinsu sukaci gaba daja ,kusan minti talatin yaji tana shashekar Kuka Kalon ta yayi saikuma yakauda kai hakan yakara sata kara volume dun kukan ta murmushi yayi a ransa yasan yaw dai rigima takeji yatankata tace yamata laifi„„


Kukan ta takeyi hankali kwance ganin yaki kulata yasa tama tso kusan shi sosai tana kuka „

Cikin Kuka tace “Dama haka Amana tace ka raboni da kuwa da komi nawa sanan kazo kana fushi dani gakuma tsinuwar mala'iku ”tafada tana kwantawa jikin shi„


Doctor kuwa shikadai yasan yayakeji akan Hubbin nasa yasan da shine yakamata yabata hakuri ama dubi yanda ita ce mai ba shi Hakuri shikan Ubangiji yamasa komi da yabashi ita Matar aljanna ,idan har kafar shi ce zatasa Nasreen tashiga Aljnna zai daga mata ita sam sosai tawuce babu gargada jiyayi tana hayewa saman shi cikin kukan shgwaba da yake kara sashi jin sonta a zuciyar shi.....✍️

🖊️MAMAN FAUZAN CE🖊️

BOOK 2 
PAGE 31&32
            .........✍️“kinga nibabu Ruwana tunda haka kikafi so ”yafada yana kokarin tureta daga jikinshi „


“To ba kai bane kayi hakuri bazan saké ”

Kalon Juna suke ido ciki da kauna kusan minti uku ta jaye idon ta tana kwantawa saman kirjin shi Hannu yasa yarungume ta shima ya ce “shikenan na yafemiki kuma mala'iku baza su tsine mikiba kefa yar albarkace,yar Aljanna”yafada yana bata kiss a kolon ta kasancewar tacire dikon yaji ba dadi sosai akan miyasa zata karasa cirewa koshi yayi nadama ciresa sosai yaji ranshi bayama dadi har yakai ga yace “Meyasa kika idasa ciresa ”

“niga baka sone ”tafada ahakanli
“Ina son gashin nan naki mai kamshi da yalwa kiyafemun kinji Na-san ina cutar dake kiyi hakuri dani kinji ,Nana yarki ce babu Wanda zaishiga tsakaninku nayimiki hukunci ne sabida kishi Nasreen haka kikacire Hijab kowa yakalemin ke naji ciwo fiyeda yanda kaddara Nana tazomin ”yafada yana kwantawa saman kirjin ta kasancewar tasauka akanshi „


“Kayi Hakuri”tasake fada ta fahimci Kishin tane yasa yayi haka tana son shi “Ina sonka karka Auro Wace zata fini gashi kaji”tafada cikin shagwaba dariya ce takubu ce masa yace “Ni daga ku nakare ku biyu kun isheni Rayuwa ”

Nasreen sai yanzu ma ta tuna Da Mufida Take taji Mijin nata baya akan dai dai yakamata yasauke nauyin shi dayake kanta tunda bawai yasake ta bane “Baby Mufida kanaganin kayimata adalci kuwa ”tafada cikin rauni 

“Banyi ba Nasreen ama Na-san su Ammi zasu kula da ita karkidamu kinji”tsakulkulu yashiga yimata dan kawai yamantar da ita gida aikuwa tashiga baska dariya sukeyi abinsu har Nana ta tashi ta takure akan gado lura da haka yasa Nasreen takara kusan ta tana bata kiss a goshi tace “Babyn Uncle ba gaisuwa ko fushi kikeyi da Antynki”


“Anty Bakya so na yanzu har tsawa kikemin ”tafada tana hawaye 

“waye yafadamiki nikuwa nake sonki bari ma kigani yanzu haw nagoyaki”tafada tana dukawa alamar Nana ta haw,da Sauri Irfan yayi kansu yace “Haba ina bazaki Iya goyata ba keda bakida lafiya na fansheki”yafada yana dukawa gaban Nana alamar tahaw ,Nasreen ce tafara bubuga kafa cikin shagwaba tace “gaskiya nima sai angoyani bazan yardaba da wayan”dariya Nana tasaka tana fadun “Uncle Mu biyu zaka goya kaji kar Anty tayimana Kuka”shiama dariyar su yashiga yi Nana ce tahaw sanan Nasreen tadawka bazai Iya tashiba sai gashi yamike dasu yana yawata wa dasu sai dariya suke abunsu shidai nashi murmushi ne sai dasuka gaji sukace yasauke su „


Yana sauke su yafara nishi yace “to kunsan cin tuwan kishiya Ramko ne so kuzo kurama mun”

“Nidai dama Anty nace ta goyani ka fansheta dan haka ita zata goyaka dady”Nana yafada tana yi cikin daki tadauko tablettes dinta tashiga game

Nasreen kuwa tagane Nana wayo tayi to ita yanzu yazatayi “malama kiduka ke nake jira”

Kafa tashiga bubuga wa kasa tace “Gaskiya nidai a'a bazan yardava kai kato dakai haka zakace na goyaka dubi muscule inka bazan iyab Allah makuwa ”tafada tana kokarin arcewa


Karaf yariketa yace “haba Hajiya ta ina zaki saifah kin goyani no na fanshe kinsani kuma”yafama ta akunne „

Dariya tayi har dimple dinta na lobawa tace “to kasakeni zan goyaka ”sakinta yayi ,yitayi kamar zata duka sai kuma ta shige dakin tana dariya hade dayimasa gwalo dariya kawai yayi yana raya Nasreen ce farin cikin sa ita ce rayuwar sa gabaki daya „



A kwana a tashi ba wuya a Wurin Allah yau cikin Nasreen watan shi bakwai kuma har yanzu Basu koma gida ba rayuwar su sukeyi mai dama dama su ba'a talauceba sanan suba ba'a arzace ba ,Yanzu harma tadan gana tabarwa Allah komi ,cikin ta kuma yafito sosai tana samun kulawa daga Mijin nata ,Wanda yakoyi aikin kanikanci kasancewar kudin hanunshi sun kare kuma idan yaje bank da niyar cire kudi za'abibiye shi agano inda yake dan yasan duk yanda akayi ko kowa bai nemeshi ba yasan Samir zai nemeshi hakan yasa ya gwamaci yayi aikin gyaran motoci Wanda dakyar yadan saba sabida wahala shi hakadai suke rayuwa cikin Hukuncin Allah kuma cikin kudin yake samu suna sayo kayan baby Guda bibiyu yake sayowa sabida acewar shi yan biyu zata Haïfa masa ,itadai saidai tayi tayimasa Dariya akan zasu gani ai „„


Ahaka rayuwa ketafiya yanzu Doctor yana dan barin su suna Zuwa Wurinsu Iya Asabe kuma shima yana dan ansa musu„


Ranar wata laraba suka tashi Nana batajin dadin jikinta sai surutai takeyi akan ga maciji nan zai canyeta anyi sa'a alokacin Irfan na gida ba yan sallar azahar tana daki sai ihun ta sukaji dagudu suka shiga har Nasreen da cikin ta yayi mata Nauyi sosai „...

Suna shiga sukaga Nana na kuka tagame kai da jiki sai kira take maciji maciji ,Nasreen ce tayi kanta ta rungume tashiga tofah mata adu'a itakuwa sai kara shigewa jikin Nasreen dun take „


Irfan kuwa dakin yaduduba ama baiga komi ba ,shima zama yayi hade da tofah mata kusan minti talatun tafara atishawa so uku sanan tadago takali Nasreen tace “Anty kaina kemin ciwo”rike mata kan tayi sosai ta tofah mata tana karewa bacci yadauke ta gyara mata kwanciyar ta tayi hade da yin tagumi tana kalon ta„


Irfan kuwa ya fahimci wani Abu ba banza ba ga dukan Alamu tsoro akebawa Nana ,ama bai tabata ba zai daigani „

Mikewa yayi hade da dauro alwala yawuce massalaci „

Bai wani tsaya ba yadawo ya tarar da Nasreen na sallah tana kalon Nana dataketa baccin ta hankali kwance shafawa tayi hade da zama kusa dashi dan jinginawa tayi da jikin shi cikin Rauni tace “Inajin tsoro kar wani Abu ne zaisake samunmu ”

“Ba'abunda zai faru sai alkairi insha Allah”yafada shima yana Jimke hanun ta dake cikin nashi „


Shiru sukayi kowa da abunda yake sakawa a Ranshi ,cikin bacci kuwa Nana ta farka da mugun ihu tana kokarin guduwa dayake Irfan na kusan ta yayi Saurin Riketa sai kokarin kwacewa takeyi tana ihu Wanda yanzu kai yatabatar masa da Shafar Aljannu ce kedamun Nana to a ina tagansu riketa yayi game yana tofah mata ,“kadaina konamu kadaina konamu bamaso muba jikin ta aka aikomu ba ”Nana ce ke magana ama kuma Ajannun dake cikin tane akejin muryasu cikin zaro ido Irfan yace “Kufadamin Uban waye ya turoku kuma ajikin wa aka turoku har kukasamu damar shiga jikin yarinya karama Wace bataci al'hakin kowa ba”yafada yana fido ido „

“Bazamu fada ba bazamu fadama ba wacece ta'aikomu wadda muka so shiga jikin ta mutsete jinin ta tafi karfinmu kusan shekara Kenan muna son ganin bayan ta ama bata zama sai ibada da karatun al'kur'ani tafi karfinmu”suka fada suna kuka „


Karatu Irfan yacigaba dayi sukuwa sai Ihu sukeyi Wanda ga dukan alamu mace da namijine ajikin ta “wayo Boka Gulmanu kacecemu zai halaka mu ,zai kashemu ya konamu da Ranmu wai wai Boka burkatsutsu ”suketa fada Irfan kuwa daga karshema Karatu yasaka a wayar shi shima kuma yanayi “kufadamin wurin wa aka turoku”yakara daka musu tsawa„


“wurin Nasreen ne Matar ka tafi karfinmu munso kasheta munso shanye jini ta ama tafi karfinmu adu'ar Iyayen ta da wdda takeyi suke ceton ta”suka fada siko ga Nana na atishawa babu kyakyautawa„


Nasreen datake gefe tana tofah ma Nana adu'a Wace yanzu har hawaye ne kemata anbaliya jin wai ita za'a halaka ashanye mata jini ayau ta tabata adu'a kariyace daga kowane irin bala'i na duniya atake taji kaunar iyayen nata yakara shigarta ,sai tausayin Nana datakeji ya mamaye kaf ilahirin Jikin ta ,Sabida itane Nana take cikin wanan halin ganin tana atishawa ga majina sai zubowa takeyi yasata tara mata mayafinta taci gaba dayi kusan minti goma sha biyar sukaji tace “Alhmdllah”kalon iyayen nata tayi tace “Uncle yunwa nakeji”Nasreen ce takama hanun ta hade da kaita toilettes tayimata wanka hade sakama ta wasu kayan „


Falon suka fito suka sameshi zaune yabadana computer Nasreen ce ta zaunar da ita hade da sakamata abinci taci sosai fiyeda yanda takeci kulum abunda yadaure mata kai Kenan ba'ita kadai ba hatta Irfan yasan Nana bata son cin abinci wani lokacin sai yayi dagaske takeci„


Bayan takarene tace “Uncle zanje wurin su Anty Hafse ”kai kawai yadaga mata itakuwa tafice dagudu tana tsale „


Nasreen kamar jira take tafashe da kuka hade da Hugin dinsa tace “Meyasa sai ita dubi Mi Nana ta musu yarinya ce karama batasan komi ba sai farin ciki duk akaina ne suka jefata cikin wanan halin Allah ya isa bazamu yafeba koma wanene”tafada tana kuka sosai Bayan ta yashiga bubugawa yace “Komi zai wuce wanan ita ce kaddara Ahalin IRFAN ALI NABAZZAGA kiyi hakuri kinji ”lalashin ta yayi sosai har takoma dariya kasancewar gâta da ciki abun aisai yayimata yawa sam bazata Iya dauka ba tayi kan kanta da matsaloli abun ya'isa haka yana fatan wanan shine karshen jarabawar ta„

PAGE 33&34
            .........✍️wurin aikin dabai taiba Kenan yazauna yana debe mata kewa „

Haka dai suke rayuwa kuma kwana biyu Abun baikara tashimata ba saidai abun dayake daure musu kai yanda Take yasan cin abinci yanzu Suna dai Rokar mata Allah hade da tofe ta kulum kuma Tablettes dinta ma yasaka aka tura kur'ani kamil kira'ar sheik sudais dai dai matashin ta suke dorama ta shi sai safe shima kota na game ne kir'ar natashi har ita kanta ta saba idan basu saka ba takansa ma kanta kuma lokaci zuwa lokaci takan ce musu sukaita wurin Dadyn ta da Sa'diya saidai suyi mata wayo tun tana tanbaya har tadaina„

Yanzu cikin Nasreen ya shiga na haihuwa kuma Doctor dakanshi yake dubata lokaci zuwa lokaci yaga lafiyar babyn ahaka suke rayuwa cikin dadi da wuya sai dai sungodewa UBangijin su tunda sunada lafiya Irfan bashida wani buri yanzu ila yaga ta haihu lafiya„


Ranar wata juma'a Nasreen ta tashi da nakuda da dare wajen karfe daya na dare har sun kwanta tana kwance share daya dan yanzu sam batajin dadin kwanciya akirjinshi„


Taji anwani Harbeta marata ta karba batasan sanda ta tashi zaune ba ta shiga Rike ciki adu'ar da duk Tazo bakin ta yitakeyi sosai zufah ke karyomata ta ko ina sai nishi take wani irin karbawa da bayan ta yayi batasan sanda tayi karaba hade da yimasa wani irin dundu Wanda yasa shi tashi a firgice ya ma Rude yarasa yazaiyi”


Nasreen yagani sai nishi takeyi tana Durkushe a kasa kanta yayi yana ta nanata “inallilahi”dama yasan Nakuda ce Ledar dasuka sayo yadora ta akai sai nishi takeyi ga hawaye dake ta zubo mata sai jujuya kai takeyi tana damkar zanene matalar kasancewar Zani take saké tuni tayaye shi ta jefar jin kasan ta kamar zai zazago Irfan kuwa kayan aikin yadauko dama yatanade su dan wanan Tana bazai bari wani kato yazo yagane masa Sirrin saba koda kuwa macece yakanji kishi ,shiyasaka dama yaci al'washin shizai karbawa matar sa Haihuwa pant dun ya yaga Wanda dama yasha cemata tadaina sakawa sabida batasan yanakudar zata zo mata ba ama bataji jifa yayi dashi ya ware kafafunta dama colle dun yabude „

Cikin ta yakedan danawa hade dacemata tayi nishi ,sai tayi nishi kuma sai takoma ta kwanta sosai take shan wahala batamasan mitakeyi ba kusan minti goma kai yafara fitowa hanun ta yarike dayake cikin nashi yace “Yi nishi sosai yanzu zaifito ”aikuwa tanaganin tasamu courage tayi wani irin yunkurowa sai ga da yafado da Sauri Doctor ya rike shi dan kar yafadi kasa ,Nasreen kuwa ajiyar zuciya taketa saukewa„


Irfan Kuwa wani irin Farin ciki yaji Wanda baitaba jinsa ba Nasreen dinshi ce ta Haïfa masa wanan kyakyawar yar yanzu wanan jinin shice wanan yar tashi ce wasu irin hawaye yaji suna sauko mishi Na farin ciki da akasin haka ,Ina Ammi yau burin ta yacika nason ganin Jikokin ta gashi dai ya Haifi wada ba shegiyaba kamar yanda tace batason shegiya,yasan zata so wanan yana cikin haka yaji ta kara fasa kara tana nanata kalma shahada aje yarinyar yayi datke ta tsala kuka yayi kan Matar tashi„


Fahimtar wata sabuwar nakudar ce takama ta yasashi kara budeta yana fadun tayi ta nishi ,Nasreen kuwa karfin tane yakusa karewa sabida ta wahala duk da batayi diguwar nakuda ba jin yana bata courage yasa takara yin wani nishin sai ga Yaro yafado gabadaya ma yazo sai tsala kuka shima yakeyi na jarirai „


Cibiya ya yanke masa wanan karon namiji ne ba mace ba ,da jinin yaron da komi ya hada da mecen ya rungume su yana hawaye yau shine da kyautar y'a y'a biyu ,yanzu wanan y'a y'an nashine Allah sarki Shikuwa Miyayi wa Ubangiji ya Azurta shi da wadanan yaran kuka yafashe dashi sosai kamar karamin yaro suma yaran kamar sunsani suka kara cala kara „

Nasreen kuwa sai hamdllah takeyi na samun kanta datayi Ashe haka Haihuwa take ,gaskiya Allah yatsinewa yaron da baidauki iyayen shi abakin komi ba yake wulakantasu ya cimusu Zarafi jitayi wani irin girma da kaunar Mahaifiyar tata yashigeta Jin Irfan yafashe da kuka yasata kokarin gashi zaune itama wasu hawayen Farin ciki ne suka zubomata badai ita ce tahaifi wanan kosasun yaran ba ita ce da wanan kyautar Allah Mai girma tafada A Ranta„


Wani sanyi taji jin andora mata Yaran asaman ta gabadaya Irfan yagame ya rungume yana mai cigaba da kukan shi,mai hade da dariya “Dubi kiga kyautar da Allah yamana Nasreen mudin yan baiwa ne dubi wananan namune ,naso ace Ammi nakusa dabansan wane irin farin ciki zatayi ba,tazo ga y'ayan da basheguba sune take so to tazo gasu ”Irfan yafada yana rungume su kam ,Nana da kuka jariran yahanata bacci yasata shigowa dakin ta tarara Uncle dinta na kuka kalon jariran takeyi dasuketa kuka kuma ga Uncle dinta shima na kuka gwalalo ido tayi tace “Uncle waya bugeka kake kuka ,wanan jariran na wanene”tafada adan tsorace „


Hannu yamiko mata alamar tazo aikuwa bamusu ta zo kusan shi hade su yayi gabadaya yana fadun “Nana ga Kaninki nan ,Antyn kice ta haifesu yanzu”yafada Wanda har yanzu yakasa boye farin cikin sa „


Nana tsale tayi hade da murna tana fadun “Uncle wanan kana nufin namune tare zamu zauna dasu kulum ”

Kai kawai yadaga mata alamar Eh Nasreen kuwa tarasa wane irin farin ciki zata nuna„


Dakanshi yadora mata Ruwan zafishida kanshi yayiwa yaran wanka sanan yataimakwa Nasreen itama ta gyara jikin ta ,Da kanshi ya goge jinin daya bata yaje tarubude mahaifar turaren wuta yasaka yagyara ko ina harda Nana ma ta taikamai duk daba wani abun kirki ta tsinana ba„


Yaran yaska wa dan turare balai hawa kaiba ,Sanan yasaka musu Rigunan sanyi dama kamar yasani kayan Mace da na miji yake soyowa „


Nasreen kuwa tana Kare wanka tasaka kayan ta hade da shafe jikin ta da turare tsp suka fito abunsu kamar ba'ayi Haihuwa ba wajen karfe Hudu suka samu sukadai daita komi Nana kuwa tana makale da yan kanan nata „


Gaba Doctor yasasu yana kalo Namijin yadauka Wanda yake matukar kama da Nasreen ya sumbace shi a kunci sanan yamasa huduba da karatu saosai Tuwan dabino da zam zama yadan lasa mishi yako shiga tsostayar labansa dsukayi matukar jajur kamar na Irfan ,sai wasa takeyi da yan yatsun sa murmushi Irfan yayi yace “Allah yaraya Mana Imran ”yafada yana ajesa „

Macen yadauka Wace itama take matukar kama dashi kamar antsaga kara sak A'moctar tadauko komi nasu daya banbancin ita Macece shikuma namiji itama adu'a yamata hade da bata sauran dabinon da yatauna yabawa dan uwan nata „


Kiss yamata ako ina najikin ta yace “Allah yaraya mana Mama fannah Kenan zamu muyi musu lakabi da Mimi da Papa kinga ai dama sunan Uncle Imran nasaka ”yafada yana dorama ta Namijin alamar tabashi Nono „


Su Nasreen dabawa jariri Nono har zumudi takeyi Shikuwa dariya yakemata akasan Ranshi kuwa tausayinta ne fal acikin ransa kowace y'a idan ta haihu gidan su ake kaita ama ta wanka jego yada yakamata akula da ita sosai,ama ita kamar mara gata a gidan miji ga babu dangi kawar da tunanin yayi ganin ta ciro nonon Wanda suka kara dan girma kadan ama sunanan a tsayensu kamar yanda suke ,ganin tana kokarin Rufema yaron hanci sam batasan yanda zata rike shi ta shayar ba yasashi aje macen yagyrama ta da kanshi yakama Nono yasakawa yaron Abaki carf kuwa yakarba „

Wani irin kara Nasreen tasakai jin zafi ,hannu tashiga yarfewa Shikuwa kamar baigane mitake nufi ba yace “Lafiya kuwa kibashi yasha mana kusan awan nawa da zuwansu Duniya ama basu sha Nono uwar suba”yafada yana jijiga macen data fara kuka 

“Allah makuwa da zafi Dubi kaga Yada yake zukar min Nono wanan aisai yashanye Ruwan Nonon Allah kayimasa magana yadaina jamin da karfi kona fasa bashi”tafada tana Harar shi ,shikan Doctor sokoko yayi yana kalonta lalle Dama ance haihuwar farko kokayita baka hankali saidaga baya murmushi yayi yace “kema haka kikayiwa Mama ai dan haka duk abunda kayi dama sai anma oya kibashi yasha”yafada yana kara gyaramaYaro zama ganin irin rikon datamishi ,Hawaye tafara yi hade da kai mishi baki kamar jira yakeyi kuwa yafara Zuka kuka tafashe da shi tana yarfe hannu yasan da Zafi ama yazatayi kowace Uwa dahaka tasaba itama zata saba ........

BOOK 2 
PAGE 35&36
            .........✍️da kyar yasamu tabawa yaron daya bawan Allah yanasha yakoma bacci cikin sangenshi na yara yasaka shi ,yamika mata macen itama daya tabata sosai macen datekda shan Nono sha takeyi dakayar tasaki tana karewa kuma tafashe da kuka “Allah wallahi saidai kanemomusu Madara nibazan iyaba Yara sai dan karan shan Nono ni wallahi bazan yardaba su zubemin Nono daga karshe kaje ka'auro Wace Takeda Nonouwa tsaye tsaye ”tafada tana Harar yran hade da share hawayen ta

Shikuwa Irfan bakaramin' mamaki maganar ta tabashi ba wai yase musu madara bayan ga Nonon uwarsu sam Nasreen kuruciya bazata saketa ba ,sanan kuma Ninon ta zaizube ita yanzu har tasan kar Nonon ta yazube yaso yin dariya ama yadake yace “aikuwa dai Nonon Uwar su zaqusha idan bazaki Iya ba sai na nemo wata Na'aura ta shayar min dasu shikenan ke Kuma kirike tsayayun Nonoluwanki kiyita kalo bashikenan ba”yafada yana kokarin tashi, Hanun shi tarike tana hawaye tace “Kayi hakuri nidai kabarmin yara na ,wallahi banda kai sune nabiyo danike ji bazan taba Iya Rabuwa dasuba idan ma Nonuwan suna so suzube kamar silifass zanshayar dasu ko”Zama yayi hade da Sakata cikin kirjinshi yace “yawa Matar Aljanna cabulena baza su zubeba nikaran kaina bazan yarda ba dan kinsan su nafiso a ajikin ki ,ni kibani ma na tsotsa kadan kinji”cewar Doctor„


Yafada yana fidosu daga cikin Riga kyam suke suna kalonshi halshenshi yasa yana tsotsa a hankali yazuko Ruwan “shhh dan Allah dazafi nidai kadaina kai kasha yaran kama susha duk sai kunbi kun tsotseni tukuna ”tafada tana ture kanshi„

Kanshi yadora saman cinyar ta yahade hanayen shi akirji kafafunshi amike Nonowanta na sama kanshi nakasa kalonsu yake daga kwance yace “miye to ai dan  mû Allah ya haliceki kece gatanmu kibamu lusha dagani har yara na ko ”yafada yana kalon Macen wacce bacci take hankali kwance sai dan nishi datake sama sama alamar mura zata kama ta„


Nana dtayi bacci tundazu datake musu wasa kamar sunsan abunda take yagyarama kwanciyar ta Nasreen yasaka akirjinshi yana shafa dan gashinta da kusan Rabin nata yafito yace “Kinyimin komi ,kinbani Budurcinki,kinbani Farin ciki ,kin bani soyaya,kinbani kwanciyar hankali da nutsuwa sanan yanzu kinbani Y'a Y'a Bansan dawace kalma zan godemiki ba Nasreen kece Rayuwata idan har kyautawa ita ce kesawa mace tashiga aljanna kekuwa zakishige ta babu gargada Ina son ki Matata ,ina Kaunarki Allah yabarmu nake har karshen Rayuwar mu kinzame mana ginshikin Rayuwa”yafada yana mai ji shikan duk duniya a yanzu babu Wanda yakaishi farin ciki„


“Kifadamun mekike so Nayimiki ,Mekike so nasiya miki indai kudi zasu siya to zanso miki shi”Nasreen Wace hawayen farin ciki suka jika mata fuska Tarasa wane irin kauna Mijin ta yakemata ita wanan kadai ma y'a isheta„


“Ni kai Nake so kai kadai ka isarmin da komi ina tsananin Kaunar ka Abbun Nana ,Naba son komi ila kanunka soyayar dakakemin shikadai ya isheni Rayuwa”tana mai Shigewa jikin shi „

“wanan kinsamu indai shine Ummu Nana ”yafada shima yana kissing dinta Rungume abarsa yayi ga yan Yaransu daga gefe bacci ne yayi awon gaba dasu „„


Kukan Yarinyar ne yatashe su babu shiri suka farka dan harma sun makara daukar ta Irfan yayi yana jijiga wa ganin Nasreen tashiga toilettes bata jimaba ta fito dorama ta ita yayi ta fido Nono tabata karaf takarba tafara sha sosai dan sun fahimci tafi namijin tsotsa„


Shima kuwa Namijin kamar jira yake ya cala nashi kara Doramata su yayi duka biyu yataimaka mata take basu su biyu tsit sukayi saida suka koshi suka koma bacci Shikuwa a gagauce yayi wanka yatada sallah ganin koyajema Massalacin an kare„


Ajesu tayi cikin matalunsu Ta ta da Nana tayi sallah Ruwan zafi taje ta dora Shikuwa Irfan zama yayi yana lazumi hade da Tunanin inda zaisamu Wace zata kula masa da Nasreen doli tana bukatar Wanda zaimata wanka jego ,ga dai Iya Asabe ama bayajin zaiyarda tama Nasreen dun danshi har yanzu bai yarda da ita ba „


Salama sukaji hakan yasa Nasreen dake cikin dan hilin nasu ta'ansa Iya Asabe ce ta leko tana fadun “ga farar kudai ”gaisheta Nasreen tayi “nikuwa Jiya kamar naji kararar Jarirai a gidan nan”tafada tana dariya „

Murmushi kawai Nasreen tayi tace “Eh Iya na Sauka jiya ”

“kai masha Allah nayi Murna Alhmdlllh ina jikokin nawa”tafada tana kokarin shiga ciki ,Nasreen kuwa ciki tashiga tafadawa Irfan babu yanda ya i ya yafito yana gaisheta shiga tayi tana kalon Yaran ama Irfan ya tare bata samu damar daukan su ba ,sai daga nesa Allah yaraya tayimusu hade da céwa“Bari nazo na doramiki Ruwan zafi suma jariran ai suna bukatar gashi ”tafada yayin datake fita waje ganin Nasreen tama dora yasata aika Hafse ta ibo mata abunda akesaka wa aRuwan zafi ganyen Masu biki ,Ruwan suna tafasa ta saka saura cikin Roba takai ma Nasreen toilettes kasancewar Irfan ya fita Nunama ta yanda zata yi wanka tayi sanan ta fito ta dauki Roba ta juye sauran Ruwan Yaran ta yima wanka sosai tagasa su Wanda suma sunji dadin gashin dan anamusu suna kuka bayan ankare ansaka musu kaya sai bacci suke abunsu„


Nasreen kuwa koda take fitowa Yaran ankare musu wanka har sun fara bacci ,turare tashafa ta saka Riga da Zani da Hijab„


Iya Asabe Ruwan koko ta dora kasancewar dama tanada dan garin ta na tukidi tadama mata kunun ,Yan gidan duk yawancin su sunzo barka sai mazan dasukace sai Irfan yashigo zasu zo suga Jariran„


Kunun tasaka mata cikeda kwano tace tashanye shi itakuwa gata ba gwanar shan koko ba dakyar tayarda tasha Rabi shima dan taji Iya tace idan batashaba Yaran bazasu samu Ruwan Nono ba ,Nasreen dason Y'a Y'a shine ta kokarta tasha Rabi„


Irfan kuwa yanadawo wa wasu yara nabiye dauke da kaya yaki Buhun shinkafa biyar yasayo yasayo Buhun hatsi biyu sai Macaroni da couscous hade da robobin ma yara wanka duk da ya siyo saida yakara siyowa „


Dakin yashiga hanun shi dauke da Bakar Léda baba wani yaronma nabiye dashi ,Sannu da zuwa tamishi Nana ma tagaishe shi Wace take makale da kanan nata jin Iya Asabe tace zata daukesu ai nasu ne„

ansawa yayi yana leken Yaran yaga suna bacci hankalinsu kwance„


Nana yakala cikin wasa yace “To Nana wanan fah Aro akabamu naga kin tatare haka bakya son adauke su”yafada yana dariya kasa kasa ganin tabata Rai „

“A'a Uncle bakace namune ba dan Allah karkadokemin su Ina son su nizan dinga shigar musu fada bazan bari kowa yadakesu ba ”tafada har hawaye na sauko mata „

“Rabu da Uncle babu Wanda zai dauke miki so har abda”Nasreen tafada tana rungume Nana„


Dariya tayi hade da cewa“yawa Uncle kagani Anty tace baza'adauke minsu ba nizan dinga goyasu har wankama nizan musu”dariya ta kawai sukeyi itakuwa sai leken Yaran takeyi fita tayi wai zata gayawa kawayen ta zata yi eviting „


Ledodin daya zo dasu yanuna mata yace “ga Sunana turaruka ne da Kayan jarirai nasiyo musu hardake da Nana ma ba nason ayi taro kawai za'ayanka musu Raguna sanan araba naman ga makota a massalci za'ayi zanan suna”daga haka yamike yashige toiletté „

Nasreen kuwa wani haushin shi taji karo na farko Kenan dataji kamar ta gasa masa magana wanan ai wulkancine ne tadauka za'ayi musu biki ne kamar ko wane y'a y'a ama shine su zanene sunan kawai za'ayi abunda Irfan yakemata yafara kaita bango tagaji hawaye ta share ta fita dan taji Iya Asabe na salama „


Kudi tabawa Iya Asabe Wanda Irfan yabata kamar dubu biyar yace tabawa Hafse ta dorama Naman daya siyo dan yasan mai jego doli zata rika cin Abu mai Ruwa Ruwa da dalmi „


Naman daya siyo tanunama ta hade da céwa abawa Hafse tayi dorin ganin kamar kartasaka Iya Asabe aikin ,“zan d'aura dakaina Hafse batasan yanda ake hadin mai jego ba zanyi dakain”tafada tana fita waje dan sayo kayan miyan„


Irfan kuwa yandawo wa ya ganta kwance tabashi baya yasan dama za'arina yoshi wazai gayata babu zaimusu gata kamar yanda akeyiwa kowace Uwa da Yaran ta kyaleta yayi yaciga ba da abunda takeyi,Iya Asabe ke kulada Yaran harda wanka Nasreen Wace fatarta tayi sulbi sosai gwanin kyau dan Iya Asabe badai Iya kulada mai jego ba tasaba dama yawanci familles ita ake turawa tana musu wankan jego„


Kamar yanda yafada kuwa hakace tafaru Ranar suna anzana shi a massalaci Anrada Imran Irfan Ali da Fannah Irfan Ali anyitasa albarka Sa biyu yasiyo aka yanka hade da Raguna hudu Wanda mutane sunyita tsegumi akan Shida yake talaka ina yaga kudin sayan wa'inan Shikuwa bai masan sunayi ba anraraba Naman ga mutanan Unguwa Ranar kowa yaci nama gidan Bakanike ,dan Haka suke kiranshi„


Nasreen kuwa Naman aka dibamusu Iya Asabe tasaka aka soye musu shi sabida zai yimusu anfani Su Iya Asabe anci nama harda zawo„


Yara kuwa sunci gaba da samun kulawa daga bangaren Iyayen nasu bayan suna da kwana biyu Irfan yasa akayi musu Akwati daya daya ko wane Dana shi shake dakaya tsadadu da turaruka komi na jarirai ansaka ,Nasreen kuwa akwati biyu yamata Nana ma daya sosai tayi murna harda hawayen Farin ciki„


Kwana biyu yara nasamun kulawa sosai dan Irfan da kanshi yaduba yatabatar da komi normale sanan hankalin shi ya kwanta ,Irfan doli ya hakura Iya Asabe ke kulada Nasreen da Yaran ta.....✍️
