           .......✍️sosai suke samun kulawa har su Mari kan daukesu suje suyita musu wasa Har Nana Nasreen ke goyama su Wanda IRFAN yakan yimata fadan tadaina sabida kartaje ta kadosu„


Ama sam bata hanawa ganin Irin shakuwar da Yaran sukayi da ita ,Kamar yanda itama ta shaku da su dan ko kuka Suke zakaga itama tafara kuka Wanda Nasreen bakaramin mamaki hakan kebata „

IRFAN kuwa abun na Nana wataran kamar yadaketa sabida tsakani da Allah zata na kuka akan suna kuka barema in Nasreen na aiki ta kyalesu suna dan kuka ita ce zataje tayita jijiga su daga kwance kota dauki Mai sunan Mama wato Mimi dan Uncle Imran wato papa yafita nauyi nesa bakusa ba shiyasaka ma tafi daukan Mimi Wace itama bacci kawai ke Rabata dasu 

Nasreen kuwa duk data na biki bata zubar dakanta ba kuma Nonouwan ta nan yada suke kuma jarabar Mijin nata baifasa ba sukan danyi wasaninin su ama baya taba yarda ko pant ma tana cirewa bare yashiga ahakan take gamsar dashi ko takama bananar sa tayita wasa da ita tana tsotsa a haka har takawo ko kuma yasaka bananar sa tsakiyan Kirjinta yayi ta kaida komowa sosai yakejin dadin haka shikan kulum ba'abunda zaiyi sai yayi ta godewa Allah da Yabashi mata tagari„


Yaran su kuwa masha Allah gwanin burgewa duk Wanda zai Gansu to sai sun burgesu gasu dashiga Rai kamar me gasu kyawawa kamar iyayen su dan Mimi har papa har yafi ma Mimi da Nana kyau „


Nana kuwa kasancewar a gida yake koya mata karatu dama badai turanci ba itakan sosai take samun cigaba duk dabawai yasakata Makaran ta bane „

Nasreen kuwa masha Allah ta murmuje tayi bulbul abunta„


Yanzu satin ta Hudu kuma tun tana sati biyu jinin yadauke mata kawai dai bata yarda yasani ,Shikuwa Doctor yasan batayi Sabida yataba kamata tana sallah Shikuwa yashigo tajiya bata ganinshi yakoma da baya da baya sai bayan takamala yayi kamar yanzu ne yashigo sabida Iya Asabe tace takula idan tabiyema namiji zata zauna ne tayi ta zazaga masa y'a y'a itakuma ta tsofe ga Nasreen da son zama big girls shiyasaka ta dauki maganar Iya Asabe, Shikuwa dama yanada niyar yabarta ta huta ,dan haka yadan sarara mata Ranar dazata shigo hannu zata gane Kuran ta„


Iya Asabe takan bawa Nasreen kayan gyara itakuwa sai tayita karba tana sha sabida tana son Mijin nata yaji dadi zata yi komi danshi Bayan wata daya da dare bayan ta yima Su Mimi wanka har sun yi bacci hakama Nana tana rungume da Mimi adu'a tamusu  tashafamusu sosai„


Nasreen kuwa wanka tacaba tasaka wasu Arnan kayan bacci Wanda Anty tasoya mata bata taba sasu ba sai kamshi take zubawa „

IRFAN kuwa sai karfe goma yadawo yana shigowa a gajiya ya zauna a falon nasu Yaran yakala dasuketa shan sanyin su dan Eci yasiyo musu sabida zafi baccin su sukeyi hankali kwance ganin sha'awa ga Nana data wani makale musu ajikin matalun nasu Kiss yayi musu suduka ya mike ganin Massoyi yar tashi bata zo tarboshi bayan bahaka suka saba ba„


Yana shiga yaji tadaka tsala hade da Rungume tana dariya “Nidai kai zaka goyani shine kayi dare sai yanzu zaka zo ko”tafada tana kissing dinshi harshen ta ta zura
Cikin nashi tafara yimasa wani irin Salo mai Rikitashi ,Doctor ansamu abun da ake so shima taimaka mata yayi suka fara shiga wani hali “Kibari nayi wanka kinga duk nayi dati”yafada yana kokarin zare mata rigar ta wacce ta tsaya mata Iya cibiya murmushi yayi ganin tana juya kai yanda yara sukeyi alamar bata yarda ba“Nidai a'a kawai muyi yanzu Bana son wanka aibaka wani iska kuma bakayi datti ba”tafada tana sunsunar shi„

DOCTOR kuwa dama abunda yakeso Kenan kayanshi ta taimaka mishi yacire ishima yacire mata nata daukar ta yayi sai toilettes sai dayasake Sakata jika jikin ta suka saké yin wanin wankan„


Suna fitowa sukaci GABA da tsotsaye tsoyen su Kusan minti talatin suna romancy sanan yadauke hanyar shi„


Wani irin matsewa yaji tayi ga Ruwan Ni'ima sai sauka sukeyi ama shi abunda sam bai fahimta ba yaji test dinta ya canja sam yaji ta rege gardi da dadi ,d'à saké Dole akwai wani Abu sam matar shi yasan bahaka take ba ,Cigaba yayi da aiki bai yarda ya nuna mata komi ba sai da yatabatar da ta gamsu sosai sanan ya sauka Wanda shi sam bai gamsu ba har yanzu yakasa yin relassing Shiyasaka gara ya sauka Mara shi ce yaji takule hakan yasashi Bata Bananar ta tsotsa kozai samu yazubar tsotsa masa tayi yadan jima Kafin yayi relasing yakoma gefe yana sauke ajiyar zuciya abun yayi matukar d'Aure masa kai yasan matar shi da ni'ima ya akayi yanzu sam bayajin test dun wurin nan a matse kamar yanda yasan shi ama sam babu wani dadi doli zai gayamata sune mi mafita „


Kirjinshi yasakata kusan minti talatin suna A haka kowa yayi shiru ,“Baka gamsu ba ko”yaji ta jefo mishi tanbayar„


Baidauka zata fahimta ba ama sai gashi tagane kanta yake shafawa a hankali sai ji yayi hawaye sun jika mishi kirji dagota yayi hade da tashi zaune yana kalon ta ,kuka tafara yi harda shasheka rungumeta yayi yana bubuga bayan ta yace “bazan boye miki ba a gaskiya sam test dinki ya canjamin Baby na baka mar yanda nasanki ba fadamun kinsha kayan Matan nan ko”hawayen ta ta share tace “Ni bansha komi ba saï abubuwan da Iya Asabe kebani take fadamun wai na gyara ne zakaji dadi har kana Ihu”saura kadan dariya ta kubuce masa ama yadake wai har yana ihu “to shikenan daina kukan kinji komi zai dai daita karkisake shan abunda tabaki zanbaki wani sirri kiyi shi babu tantama zai maidomin Dadi na yada take ”yafada yana Murmushi ,itama dariya tayi mai hade da kuka Shikuwa dimple dinta daya lotsa yasaka yatsarshi daya yace “karkidamu zakisani kukan dadi inyita Ihu kinji ,ina wayo Nasreen dadi wayo dadi kasheni”yafada yana dariyar ta filon dake kusa dashi tadoka hade da dukan shi tako ina Shikuwa saï dariyar ta yakeyi gajiya tayi ta fara kukan shagwaba tana bubuga kafa ganin haka ya daina dariya ya rungume abarsa yana shafa bayan ta bacci yayi awan gaba dasu„


Da Asuba ma tashi sukayi sukayi sallah hade da tada Nana ma bayan sunkare ne su Mimi suka shiga tsala kuka doli tashiga aikin basu Nono duk da kuwa yanzu sundan yi wayo Nono kawai yake hadasu da Nasreen dan Nana zata wuni tana Raino ko su Mari ,shiyasaka dan sun fara kuka tabasu suka tsotsa shikenan kuma„

Haka suke rayuwar su cikin kwanciyar hankali dan yan Yaran nasu ,Irfan kuwa da kanshi yasiyo mata Ayaba da kankana kayan fruits da kanshi yake bata tanaci hadin kankana yakemata ysaka bayan yayi Mixer ta yakawo madara lait peak da kannunfari yahada mata tasha Safe da marecz tsawon kwana uku kuma bai kusancetaba ,A Ranar daya kusanceta kuwa sosai tabidi zautar dashi sabida yanzu kai yajita zam zam harma taso tafi farko zo kuga murna wajen Nasreen ganin yanzu kai tatabatar da komi dai dai ne Shikuwa bayan komi yakamala dariyar ta yashiga yi itakuwa tace ko oho tanada hujar yin haka „


Iya Asabe kuwa takawo mata wani sasake wai ta rafasa shi tasha sa Mai gida tsale karba Nasreen tayi hade dayin godiya kamar yanda tasaba bataki karba sabida kar taji ba dadi„


Haka rayuwa ke tafiya cikin kwanciyar hankali ga famille har ila yaw da su Mimi keda wata takwas yara sun fara wayo sosai sunsan Abbun su dan Abbu sukecema IRFAN kamar yanda Nana takoyamusu hakama Nasreen sukan ce mata Ummu sunsaba da Nana sosai zakaji suna kirn “Nana”sunan ta suke kiranta dashi Wanda babu yanda batayi dasu ba su rikace mata Anty ama sam sunki idan tana koyamu suna Nana dariyar ta kawai su IRFAN keyi itako sai tayi fushi kafin ake jima wa kuma tane musu dan har sunfi sabawa da ita fiyade da Nasreen ,IRFAN kuwa shima sunfi sabawa dashi dan jansu yakeyi ajiki baya son Yaran shi sutashi idan sunada damuwa sukasa fadamishi ma rashin h'shakwwa shike janyo Yara idan yana cikin matsala amai makon yagayawa iyayen sa saidai ya gwamaci yafadawa aminan sa kowani bare Shikuwa sam baya son haka shiyasaka inkaga yada yake dariya dasu yana musu wasa zaibaka mamaki „„


A wanan lokacin kuma Abu guda ke daurema Nasreen kai ganin yanda yanzu  Iya Asabe ke share ta idan tagaisheta sam bata ansawa hakama ko su papa sunje wurin su har su Hafse basa kulasu har Nana nafadawa Nasreen taga Idan taje wurin su Mari takama hararn ta Kenan bayan ita batasan abunda tayi ba ,Nasreen batada abun cewa sai cewa datayi sudena shiga sabgar su tazauna nan cikin part dinsu suyita wasan su.....✍️

           ......✍️Idan tafadawa IRFAN kuwa sai dai yace ta share su kowa yayi rayuwar shi itadai abun na daure mata kai ama tabar wa zuciyar ta „„


Wani Abu kuma dayake tsananin daure mata kai ganin yanda yanzu Irfan yake ansa gaisuwar Mari sanan kuma Iya jummai yakan duka yagaisheta harda murmushi ,sanan kuma IRFAN sai yakai karfe goma sha daya bai shigoba bayan bayayin haka cikin yajima ne yakai karfe tara ,kwanan nan duka haka yakemata har tafara tanbayar shi ko aikina yace mata Eh daga haka baisake cewa komi ba „„


Abunda ta fahimta kuma harda kudi ta taba ganin yabawa Hafse Wanda bata fahimci kona miye ba har cikin gidan kuwa tashigo sabida Nana ya'aika ta kirata a gaban ta yabawa Hafse kudi bata tanbaya ba sabida a tunanin ta bazai yi wani Abu ba Wanda badai sai ba „


Haka dai take ganin canje canje wanda har yakai Mari kan yi habaici tun bata fahimtar da'ita take har tafara fahimta ,ita bata fada batama iyashiba idan ba ranta bane yabace haka yasa bata maida musu habaicin bawai dan bata iyaba sai dan asamu zaman lafiya so tari Mari sai taga 
IRFAN yashigo sai tafara karai raya tana wani girgiza kugu kamar na akuya„„

Itakuwa Nasreen idan taga haka sai dai tayi dariya kawai dan abun yanzu yafara bata dariya „„


IRFAN tafawa yayi musu magana itafah tagaji shiru shiru ba tsoro bane gudun magana ne sai cemata yayi shibayason fitina tadaina shiga harkarsu abun yadaure mata kai ama tayi shiru abunta „„


Wata Rana tafito dan neman su Nana kasancewar yanzu tayi kawaye a unguwar duk dama Nasreen da Abbun su baya bari suna fita ama sukan fita wani lokacin„„


Tazo ta wucewa Iya Asabe tace “Oh ni Jikanyar Malan Mani su Jamila ankama Miji an kanai naye kamar danke kadai akayishi”tafada tana kalon Jamila yarta Wace tazo kwanan nan dama ai tana Wurin Yayan su ne yanzu kuwa tadawo dariya Wace aka kira da jamila tayi tace “aikuwa dai nimai miji ,Mazan gadai babu na Allah a cikinsu ”tafada tana tafawa itada uwar tata babu wani ladabi sai tsagwaron s'iskanci dan ta fahimci Jamila duk tafisu hutsara „


Nasreen data fahimci da ita suke murmushi kawai tayi hade da cewa “wutsiya Rakumi tayiwa yaro nisa ,Nan gani nan bari sadakin matsiyata ,Nagaba tuni yayi gaba haka Allah yatsara badai mutum ba saidai Allah”tafada tana fita daga gidan dan nemosu Nana a makota „„

Su Iya Asabe kuwa sosai sukaji maganar ta ta dake su harara suka bita da ita suna cizon yatsa dan sunji zafin maganar „


Itakuwa su Nana tanemo hade da yimasu Mimi wanka dan duk sunyi datti ,dama yanzu Nana itakewa kanta wanka dan tahanama amata tun tana fushi har takoma kuka hakan yasa Nasreen kyaleta tashiga yima kanta wanka sukayi suduka tafesa musu turaruka hade sai kamshi suke abunsu„„


Da dadare bayan yadawo yanzu Yaran sam basa ganin abbun su sai da safe dan wataran tun suna tanbayar sa har bacci yake daukesu „„„


Sannu da zuwa tamasa tazo zata rungume shiyadan kauce yana fadun “Nagaji kibari na huta”daga haka yashige daki tabar ta da baki saké ,wani irin zafi taji zuciyar namata ama tafara Kiran sunan Allah ,batayi zuciyaba tuno kalmar hakurin da Mama take nanata mata kulum ,daki ta tarar dashi yana kokarin cire kayansa taimkama masa tayi bai hanaba towel ya d'aura yana kokarin shiga toilettes batare da yakeleta ba bayan kotayi wanka shiyake bata mata doli suke shiga tare ama yaw ko kalo ,hawaye taji yana saukomata ta share hade da fido mishi kayan dazaya saka bata tashiba tazauna zaman jiranshi ,wayar shi taji yayi kara wacce ya canja layi bata kula ba dan ba dabi'ar ta bace daukar kiranshi saidai intaga sunan Wanda tasani ne wanan kuma sunan batasanshi ba ansaka Mr IM share wayar tayi ,seconde kadan tasake kira tsaki taja dan abun yafara bata haushi ,sai kira ke shigowa ganin mai Kiran yakidaina Kiran yasa ta dauka da niyar tacemasa yana wanka taji murya mace cikin shagwaba kamar ta sakarkaru take fadun “Dear nidai dan Allah ka kokarta akan Maganar mu kaji kuma kasan Yakamata ayi abun dagagawa”tafada tana kukan sakarci„


Wani irin faduwa gaban Nassreen yayi dan taso tadauki murya ama sam abun da take hasashe tasan ba gaskiya bane kashe wayar tayi tana tunanin badai IRFAN neman mata yakoma ba “innalilahi taketa nanatawa ”tasamu zuciyar ta yayi sanyi ,IRFAN kuwa yana fitowa daga wanka bai ko kaleta ba yashiga shafa mansa hade da saka kayan baccin sa dane zuciyar ta tayi tace “Ga Abincin nan ”dan kalon abincin yayi sanan ya yamutsa fuska kamar yaga kashi yace “banaci”daga haka yajuya mata baya hade da dana wayar sa ganin kira yasashi dubawa alamar har ma andauka anyi magana zaune yatashi yakaleta ganin itama ta haw matalar ta juya masa baya yace “waye yace kidauki kiran”bata kulashiba ta shekar dashi dan idan tace zata yi magana kuka ne zai biyo baya„„


Sam bai ji dadin daukar wayar datayiba yasan zatji badadi sosai Shikuwa baya son haka ,Adu'a yayi ya shafa hade da kwantawa ,itakuma tajima tana tunanin canjin yanyin mijin nata sam bazata juri wanan ba wane irin Salo IRFAN kemata ne zata bashi kwana biyu tagani idan ma laifi tamasa yagayamata tabashi hakuri bawai wanan canjin yanayin ba„


Dakyar bacci yadauketa da safe ma gaisheshi tayi hade da samishi ruwanka wanka su Nana ne da kanan nata suka rukun kune shi suna tayimasa fira Shikuwa sai biyemusu yakeyi kari tahadamusu na Dumamen tuwo „


Abinci sukaci shida y'a y'a sa itakuma nata tasaka taci daban kanshi kawai ya girgiza sukaci gaba da hirar su „


Ahaka suka kwashe sati biyu duk abunda yadace takan yimasa na Hakkin sa sai dai kusan sati biyu yanzu bai nemata ba abun mamaki da daurekai ga wani Abu daya tsiro mata yin waya da dadare duk tanajinsa duk dashi baicika magana ba daga uhm sai Uhmm ama takan boye tasha kukan ta Wanda har Nana tasha kamta tana kuka ta tanvayeta lafiya ama sai tacemata lafiya klw ,ahaka suke rayuwa yanzu babu wanan soyayar da IRFAN dinta kemata tana kai kukan ta ga Allah dan shine mai share hawayen bawansa „


Bayan kwana biyu da dadare yasameta zaune kusan shadaya na dare tana zaman jiran shi abinci tagabatar masa yadanci kdan sanan tahadamasa Ruwan wanka bayan yakare ne yasaka kayan baccin sa ya ransa ya sumbata hade dayimusu adu'aa„„


Kwance ya tarara da ita ta juya masa baya kwanan dasuke yanzu Kenan zama yayi hade dacewa “kitashi zamuyi magana”taso kintashi ama ta tashi zaune ba tare da takaleshi ba „Nasreen akwai abunda yake faruwa Wanda ninakasa fahimtar minene kitayani da adu'a kawai inajina kamar ba niba ,nikaran kaina inajin na sauya yazanyi zuciyata takanmin zafi da Radadi aduk lokacin danakali 
Fuskar ki naga ba walwala abun kan dameni kiyi hakuri dani kiyi hakuri dani karkigaji kicigaba damin adu'a Na-san komi zai dai daita,kiyi hakuri da abunda zakiji nafada yanzu Nasreen Aure zankara kwanan ”yafada yana kalonta„


Shiru ne yabiyo baya tsawan minti biyu yaji tasaki murmushi Wanda yasan ba banza ba idan tayishi “Aure ,Aure zakayi to meyasa kake fadamun bayan ni ba kowabace agunka face baiwa daka rabo daga ahalin ta kana tunanin kasayeta ne ai bakada Hujjar dazaka gayamin ni baiwarka banida wanan matsayin ,ama naji dadi dakafadamun Allah yabada zaman lafiya da Zuri'a dayaba”Tafada tana komawa kwanciyar ta „


IRFAN kuwa jinin shine yayi sanyi baiyi zaton zata fadamasa hakaba Shikuwa meyasa yazama butulu ne ,Anya kuwa yayi wa kansa adalci kanshi ya girgiza yakali bayan ta shima sai yajuya ya kwanta „


Nasreen kuwa maganar shi ce tadaketa Aure suna cikin wanan matsalar sam bazata hanamasa yayi Aureba ama meyasa sai yanzu dabatada Wanda zata kira danata bayan Y'a Y'a ta sukadai Takeda Wanda zasuna debe mata kewa „


Ahaka suka kwanta Wanda babu Wanda yayi bacci a tsakaninsu kowa da abunda yake sakawa da safe ma yada tasaba yimasa tayimasa sam bata nuna masa tasaka maganar aranta ba„„


Kwana biyu yayo akwati biyar shakeda kaya kayan radar kishiya ko kalon kayan batayi ba tamasa godiya a inda ya'ajesu anan yatarar dasu sai shine yadaukesu ya lanbade „


Kwana biyu sam Ma'auran ta sunrasa Miyake musu dadi Nasreen kuwa har yanzu takasa kuka sai dai abun nacinta tarasa Wanda zata fadawa taji sanyi „


Nana tasamu suna wasan su da kanan nata zama tayi tana kalon su kawai sai ji sukayi tace “Nana Abbunku Aure zaiyi”tafada tana kalon gefe„


Mikewa Nana tayi tadawo kusan ta hade da fadun “Shiyasaka naga bakwa magana da Abbu ko sabida zai kara Aure nima Bana so yayi Auren Ummu ina tsoron Wace zai Auro muguwace kamar Antyn Hanifa kulum kashi takebata kuma Baban ta nagani hakama Maman ta kijemasa kar ya Auro mana wata bamasan ta please ummu”Nana tafada tana fashewa da kuka ,Rungumesu Tayi ganinin su Mimi ma nakuka dan Antyn Nasu nayi .......✍️

🖊️MAMDOCTOR IRFAN🥼

_Marubuta Masu Aiki Da Fasaha Cike Da Zallan Tsagwaron Amana,Faɗakarwa Game Da Nishaɗantarwa Tare Da Wa'azantarwa,Alƙalaminmu 'Yancinmu_

BOOK 1
PAGE 41&42
           ......“Babu Wanda zai tabaku matukar ina raye bazakuyi kuka ba Nana kunada gatanku babu Wace zata zo ta har gitsamun ku”

Tafada tana lalashinsu sukayi shiru suka koma wasansu itakuwa aikin gidan ta shiga yi takamala tayimu wanka sosai sai kamshi suke aubawa abunsu kamar y'a y'an turawa dan dukansu farare ne Irfan sukabiyo Mimin da papa „„


Tablettes dinsu suka dauka suka shiga game dan Yaran akwai wayo da saurin fahimta da wuri suke hardace abunda ake koyamu dan yanzu har Alhmdlllh zuwa iklas sun Iya ta duk da bakinsu bawai yabude bane ama insuna yi zaka fahimci abunda suke fada„


Bayan sati daya taga baki nayawan sauka gidan ,har sukanzo sutanbayeta Aron kaya itadai takan basu tsakaninta dasu Iya Asabe kuwa habaici ne Wanda tarasa dalili„


Yau Asabar tun waJen karfe shida taga yashirya cikin wata Shada Kalamar madara da takami bakake harda saka agogo da hula yafito angonshi sak„„„



Nasreen kuwa banza tayi dashi tun gaiswar datayimasa Kalon ta yayi asanyaye da zaifita yace “Yau ne daurin Auren ”yafada a hankali „


Wani irin tsoron ne da fargaba suka shigeta gabanta ne yafadai ama tadake ta shiga Nanata kalamar “la'ilaha illah lah”cikin ranta „

Juwowa tayi tadan kalesa hakika yafito angonsa sak tace “Allah yabada zaman lafiya”daga haka takame bakinta taci gaba da Jan carbi ,zama yayi kusa da ita yace “Allah yahuci zuciyarki ,Amaria ba'anan zata zauna ba tana nan bayan mu acan zata tare ,dan Allah kiyi hakuri duk abunda zakigani ki daure kiyi adu'aa Allah yana tare dake ,komi zai wuce komi zaiyi dai dai insha Allah ”yafada yana mikewa yafita falon ya ishe Nana tana kuka tana sallah adu'a yaji tanayi wada tabasa mamaki sosai “Allah ya Allah na rokeka Allah kazabawa iyaye na abunda yafi zama alkairi ,Allah kasa Abbu bazai Auro Wace zata rika dukanmuba ”adu'a take sosai tana kuka,wani irin kaunar yar tasa da tausayin tane yashige sa ,hakika dazai Iya barin Auren daya bari shima baisan dalili ba haka kawai yakejin son yin Auren ,Fita yayi yabarta nan Wace duk dataganshi bata fasa adu'a datakeba „„


Nasreen kuwa zuciyar tace takema  zafi jitakeyi kamar zata fashe ciwo takeji a zuciyar ta ,Nana ce tashigo ta rungume Nasreen dun “Nana zuciyata zafi takemun jinake kamar zata fashe”tafada tana dafe dai dai zuciyar ta ,hannun ta takai ta dora saman na Nasreen tace “Bari namiki à du'a Ummu zaki samu sauki insha Allah zakiji sauki Abbu ne ko daga yaw babu Ruwan mu dashi”tafada tanayima Nasreen adu'a kasancewar ta iya adu'o sosai„


Tanayimata kuwa taji zuciyarta namata sanyi kwanciya tayi bisa kafar Nana hade da dora kanta saman cinyar ta ahaka bacci yayi awon gaba da ita„


Cikin gidan kuwa hidima ake tayi ana habaice habaice dangin su Iya Asabe har daga murya sukeyi dan Nasreen taji ama sunyi abanza dan batamasan sunayi ba tana bacci sai kusan karfe goma ta tashi kuma har yanzu Nana bata tasheta ba „

Da adu'a tafarka taga Nana na kalonta ,Itama kalonta tayi da mamaki tace “Nana badai tun danakwanta ba baki tasheniba kafafunki ai sun sanyaye ”tafada tana kokarin dadana mata kafafun mikewa Nana tayi tana fadun “A'a Ummu ai naga bakya samun bacci gara nabarki kiyi tunda kinsamu yanzu”tafada tana fita waje dan yiwa su Mimi wanka dan yanzu itakemusu ,Nasreen kuwa wani kara jin kaunar Nana ne yashigeta har takeji tamafi son Nana akan Y'a y'an ta Nana tanada karancin shekaru ama tanda hankali da wayon da wani baba sam bashida shi ,idan ta tuna tanada Nana takanji sanyi a Ranta „„


Mikewa tayi jin hayaniya lekawa tayi taga mutane sai hada hada akeyi a gidan ,badai biki akeyiba ita bata sani ba sam bataji dadi bâ ama zata barsu tunda basa bukatar ta cikin sabgar ta ,abinci sukayi sukaci abunsa ama ita sam hankalin ta baya Jikin ta „„


Haka suka wuni Irfan baishigo ba, sai bayan isha'i tana zaune tana lazumi taji guda natashi abakin kofarsu Mamaki ne yakasheta ama tashare dan mizai kawo amaria dakinta bayan kuma bataga ansata cikin shirginsu ba„


Nana ce tabudemusu suka shigo Amaria ce lulube Wace baka ganin naciki ama naciki naganinka sai wasu tsahi biyu dake rike da ita sai yan mata da sauran yara anata guda „


Nasreen kuwa bayan tatabatar da da gidan tane tamike salama tsahin sukayi ta'masa tana gaishesu taburma tashinfida musu suka zauna “to uwar gida ga Amaria nan munkawo miki ga amana nan duk da ba gida daya kukeba yakamata kurika zumunci yanda Mijinku zaiji dadi ku hade kanku kuzauna lafiya ga Mari nan munkawo Allah yatabatar da alkairi ko kinada abun cewa zamutai da amaria”

Nasreennkai bakaramin choc tashigaba jin abunda suke fada Mari badai Mari diyar Iya Asabe ba itace kishiyar ta ,dake wa tayi jade da tataro dukan jarumtar ta tace “Allah yabamu zaman lafiya yasa zaman gidan tane”tafada tana murmushi Wanda kowa yasha mamaki dan suna tabacin batasan d'à maganar Auren bâ tamusu bazata mikewa sukayi hade da fita „


Jamila ce tace “ashe dai wutsiyar rakumin batayiwa yaro nisaba tunda gashi ya kamota cikin sauki”daga haka tabar part dun ,

Suna fita Nana ta fashe da kuka hade da rungume kanan nata tace “ummu ,Ummu badai Mari bace amaria Abbu sam batadace dashi batda kirki tasha dukan su Mimi agabana kuma tace idan nafada zata kashesu bana so dan Allah mukoma can gidan Dady Hafiz bana son nan”tafada tana kuka dan sosai nana yanzu tafara zama yan mata d'un da karacin shekarunta takan fahimci wasu abubuwa ,karasowa kusan su tayi ta hada ta rungume gabadaya tace “Kidaina fadar haka kiyitayiwa Abbun ki adu'a kinji ,dubi yanda kike kuka haka su papa keyi haba Nanan Baba jannatul fidaussi”tafada cikin karfin hali dama idan tayi fushi da ancemata jannatul fidaussi tana sauka sabida labarin ta da Nasreen tabata wanan karanma sai gata tana dariya „

“yawa kuzo muje muyi kalo”tafada tana mikewa dan tasan Nana da son kalon film takan samusu a computer shi koshima yakan samusu zatayi karfin halin dazata dauke damuwar tasabida gara adoramata dukan damuwar Nana „


Falon suka koma ta dauko computer shi tasakamusu har bacci yadaukesu takashe hade da gyaramusu kwanciyar su ta tofamusu „


Tamike Kenan taji salamar sa amsawa tayi cikin karfin hali hade dayimasa sannu da zuwa tashige kurya dakin ,kayansu data wanke tashiga lunkewa„


Irfan kuwa yadauka zai tarar da ita tana kuka ama saï yaga sabanin haka Anya kuwa lafiya take kardai abun yana nan yanacinta har yamata il'a yasan zafin hakan ,kartaje takamu da wata cuta abunda bazai yafewa kanshi ba „


Dakin yashiga ya tarar da ita tana Linke kayan zama yayi kusa da ita kalonta yakeyi babu kyakyautawa itakuwa ko aijikin ta kamar babu dan Adam a wurin.....


“Nasan zafin dakike ji Na-san irin radadin da zuciyar ki keyimiki kiyi Kuka Nasreen kozakiji sanyi ,kiyi kuka kada zuciyar ki ta ilata bazan yafewa kaina ba wallahi sam bansan miya nayi wanan Auren ba kimin adu'a please”yafada yana date zuciyar shi „


Ajekayan tayi hade da matsowa kusan shi sosai dai dai zuciyar shi tadafa tashiga yimsa adu'a tana tofawa kusan minti biyar taja baya tace “zan rufe kofar ”tafada tana komawa falon  Irfan kuwa yanzu lamarin Nasreen d'un nashi yadaina bashi mamaki sai dai tsoro mikewa yayi ko wankan baiyiba ya fito kalonta yayi yaga yanda ta rungume Nana su Mimi na gefe wani irin tausayin tane yaji har saida kwal'a tazubomasa da Sauri yafita yana mai adu'a cikin ransa ,yarasa miyasa yayi Auren nan shi dai sanin shi ada kwata kwata baya son su iya Asabe barema Mari dayafi tsana a cikin su yaw itace ya Aura batare da sanin Iyayen sa ba batare da sanin su Ammi ba yaza suji idan sukasamu wanan labarin baisan ykayi ba yabawa Mari number shi ,shi sam har yanzu baisan miyakeji akan taba ,tafiya yakeyi ama sam hankalin shi baya jikinshi wani lungu naga yashiga wani dan madaidai cin gidane gidan kai baza'akirashi da gidan talakawa ba sam ,duk da bashida girme ama abun burgewane yasha kyau Wanda ga dukan alamu sabon gidane daga farfajiyar gidan duk shuke shuken fulawa ne ciki yashiga masha Allah nafada ganin wani kayatacen falo komi anzuba aciki kalar Blue sosai dakin ke kamshi ,Mari kuwa cikin lulubun ta takemasa sannu da zuwa nesa da ita yazauna yana dafe kanshi „„

BOOK 2
PAGE 43&44
          ......✍️yi yayi kamar babu mutum a Wurin yamike yabar falon wani daki naga yashiga masha Allah komi ansaka harsasu Eci zama yayi hade da cire kayan toilettes yashiga Wanda yajima kafin yafito„„

Yana fitowa jalabiya yasaka ya koma falon tanan inda yabar ta ledar kazar da yasiyo yamiko mata karba tayi babu kunya ta Haye mayafin ta Budé ledar tayi taga yoghourt da jus sai kaza miyau tane yabiya shikuwa wayar shi yadauko yashiga dadanawa kamar ba mutum a wurin kazar tabude tanayi tana ganin ko yana kalonta yagar kazar tashiga yi hankali kwance„


Irfan kuwa sam abun bai burgeqhiba tsaki yaja Yakoma dakin kwanciyar shi yayi yana tunanin Rayuwa yanda tazomishi ko ya Nasreen dinshi take baisani ba yasan duk inda take tana cikin hali na taimakon Wanda zai lalasheta shikuwa gashi gayanda rayuwa tayidashi,Mari ce tashigo kayanta ta cire babu ko kunya agabansa haka ta fito tsirara haihuwar uwar ta ,tana zuwa takwanta saman kirjinshi hade da shafashi tace “Haba dear miyne wai fushi kakeyi ne”tafada tana sha shafashi sam abun bai bada ccita ba jiyakeyi kamar yamaketa ama wanan ba halinsa bane kuma bazai hanata hakkin taba tunda an Daura ogansu na kanikanci shi yayimasa waliyci aka d'aura Auren yanzu gashi ga wata matar wada Ba Mufida ba da Nasreen rayuwa Kenan takanzo ma mutum tayanda baya zato baya son yin sex cikin irin wanan yanayin sam wanan sam ba halinshibane baya so yajimata dan zai ita jimata awanan yanayin ,ganin ta takura yasashi biye mata yacire kayanshi Romance dinta yakeyi Wanda yayi mamaki yanda babu kunya take yimasa wani salon Wanda ko Nasreen dayake tunanin tajima aharkar bazata zake hakaba ama shi baidauki hakan wani Abu ba duba dayanda yara sukeson Aure komi zasu rikayi ,yana son mace wayaya kamar wanan yajima yana romancy dinta Wanda har shidewa takeyi hanyar yadauka hade da yin adu'ar saduwa da iyali a hankali yakeshiga dan karyajimata yana shiga a hankali yaji yashige sulub wani irin faduwa gabansa yayi kardai Mari ba budurwa bace hakika wurin shiga yajita adan matse ama yanzu kuwa gabadaya yashige sosai tabashi mamaki yakaranci lungu da sako ajikin mace shi likitan mata ne yasan kowane part najikin mace yasan budurwa yasan bazawara ,wanan dinki akamata kuma batasan bazata iya hada Virgin na Ubangiji ba da nawani ,wani takaici ne yaji yakasheshi kardai laifin zina ne kebinshi Matar shi mazinaciya “inallilahi yashiga maimaitaw”itakuwa duk taga yanayinshi yacanja kuka tashiga yi hade da nuna zafi itaga Vigina ganin yana son gano ta tafashe da kuka tana fadun “bakaji ni kamar budurwa bako wallahi yaya a walune nataba yimasa rashin kunya shine yayita ball dani damukaje akacemin ai budurcina yarage ”tafada tana kuka wiwi„


Doctor kuwa yasan abubuwan dake zubar da budurci ama sam wanan ba hujja bace taushe zuciyar shi yayi yadauka batare da yayi relasing ba towel ya d'aura yashiga toilettes ya tsarkake jikinshi sam babu wani test dayaji ko Mufida dayake ganin batada wani test tafi Mari so sab'in tsaki yaketa ja shikadai ahaka yayi wanka yadawo yatarar da'ita harta fara bacci d'un du yadana mata akafa hade da bugamata tsawa ta gyaramishi shinfida sanan taje ta tsarkake jikin ta a tsorace ta gyara bed dun ta shiga wanka shikuwa kayan baccin sa yasaka hade da yin adu'a yashiga tunanin duniya dakyar bacci yadaukesa „„...


Bangaren Nasreen kuwa al'wala tayi takai wa Allah kukan ta yada taga Rana haka taga dare har akafara Kiran Sallah ta tada Nana sukayi zama sukayi suna nafil d'fili ahaka suka zauna suna lazumi„


Da safe kuwa yan biyu suka aza rigima akan sai ankaisu wurin Abbun su lalashin su sukeyi ama kamar zugasu sukeyi wani irin takaici yaran suka fara bata hakan yasa tadauki charger tafara tsulamusu sai kuka sukeyi dakayar Nana ta kwacesu itama tana kuka dan sam batason abunda yatabasu „„


Wanka tamusu hade da sakamusu dumamen tuwo sukaci kusan karfe shadaya sukaji ana guda Iya Asabe na fadun “Masha Allah amariya takai budurcin ta harda irin wanan kyautar dubu hamsin kai masha Allah wasu sai dai su mutu”Nasreenn kuwa duk tana jinsu dariya ma abun yabata wai dubu hamsin ,dubu hamsin ai ko wani kesh da'aka sakamata cikin lefenta yafi dubu hamsin har akwai na dubu dari ,kanta kawai ta girgiza taciga ba gyaran gidan .....


Irfan kuwa baidawo ba sai wajen karfe shida na marece aiki yatafi duk da Hutin da ogansu yabasu ama sam yaki danbaiga abunda zai tsinta agidan ba idan ma yazauna hakan yasahi yawuni yana aiki Wanda Nasreen kuwa ko ajikin ta bata nuna damuwa ba sai dai itadai kadai tasan abunda takeji har Nana nace mata “Ummu kingani ko Abbu tunda safe baizo ganinmu ba ,Ummu anya Abbu zaima kara kulamu”wayon Nana nabata mamaki kanta ta girgiza hade dacemata Tayimasa Uzuri zaizo wata kila aikine yarikesa„


Shine baizoba sai yanzu kusan karfe shida da salama yashigo ta'amsa masa su Mimi ne suka ruga hade da rungume shi daukarsu yayi sama yana kissing sai hira sukemishi wata yagane wata sam baya fahimta ya fahimci kuma dasukace Ummin suce ta dakesu baice komi ba yamaida hankalinsa kan Nana Wace tunda tacemasa sannu da zuwa bata kara kalonsa ba sai ma Tablettes dinta data dauko tashiga yin game„


Al'amarin Nana tafara bashi mamaki badai kishi taketa ya Nasreen ba lalle da Jan aiki a gabansa „„


Zama yayi kusada Ita yace “Babyn Uncle bakigani ba?”yafada tana kalonta ,dan dagowa tayi kuma saita maida kanta ga game ita tace “naganka mana namaka sannu da zuwa Abbu wani abun ne kuma”tafada batare da ta kalli kowa'ba saima jawo Papa datayi take nunamasa game over datayi„


Irfan kuwa kalonshi yamaida kan Nasreen alamar tanbaya yaushe Nana tafara rashun kunya dazata gayamasa wanan ,shekaru takwas ace tana fadamishi wanan kalaman „


Nasreen karan kanta tasha mamakin kalaman Nana saikace tsaramata su akeyi,ganin yana kalonta yasa takauda kanta taciga ba da tankaden tuwan ta „


Mikewa yayi ya shiga dakin babu jimawa yafito su Mimi yama kiss hade da tafiyar shi sasam ranshi badadi Laifin Nasreen yake gani badai iatace ke zuga Nana namasa rashin kunya ba doli zai yima abun tubkar hanci„

Nasreen kuwa sam bataji dadi ba kuma doli zata takawa Nana birki ,Nana ce ke dariya tana nunawa Nasreen yanda Mimi tacinye game dun taga ta shareta ,fahimtar hakan yasa ta taso gabanta tace “ummu miye akayi nayimiki wani laifin ne ”tafada idanun ta nacicikowa da hawaye ,kauda kanta Nasreen tayi tace “Nana Ashe bkyajin Magana ta ko meyasa bakyaji Mahaifinki kike fadawa wanan kalamn fadamun naji Allah yana fushi da wa'inan mutanan kina so Allah yatsame hanunshi daga lamuran mune?”tafada tana kalonta,kanta Nana ta shiga girgizawa tace “a'a Ummu daga yanzu nadaina bazan sakeba namiki alkawari”tafada tana kuka ,rungume ta tayi tace “yawa good girls Allah yamuku albarka idan yadawo kibashi hakuri kinji ”kai tadaga mata alamar to sanan takoma wurin ta sukaci

🖊️MAMAN FAUZADOCTOR IRFAN🥼

BOOK 2
PAGE 45&46
          ......✍️ahaka suke rainon cikin ta kuma ta daya bangaren sunata neman yanda zasu nemi Irfan dun „

Dakyar dasa bakinsu Abbi hafiz yamaido matar sa wacce tayi nadama sosai har kuka taknyi akan amidomata da Nana domin yanda takejin sa'adiya haka takejin ta duk da anmaidota dakyar suka shirya „

Abbi ma dakyar suka saka Samir yin Aure danyace sai abikinshi yadawo dama akwai wata yarinya dasuka daidaita ba'ayi wani shagaliba akan d'aura Aure dama shima yagina asibitinsa ana hakan yasashi maido iyakansa suka komi nan Zinder dun har mahaifiyar shi kuma matar tashi yarinyace mai hankali dasanin yakamata dan itama bazata wuce shekaru ashirn ba yakan kaita can wurin Mufida ta wuni eayake gasu sharifa sosai suke debe mata kewa a haka Ammi bata taba Kiran taba tamata yajiki abun kan har takance kodai har itane Ammin bataso tun tandamuwa harta daina ahak suke rainon cikin har yashiga watan haihuwar sa Abbi yaso akawota nan ama Ammi tace bahakaba ,hakan yasa Momy tace aidama zata iya kulada yar ta dady ne daman yakafe akan ta zauna acan ,Mama dakanta tazo tadauki Mufida suka koma can sukuwa su sharifa suka koma gida saidai sukanje su wunin mata acan sati daya dakomawa tatashi da nakuda nan take aka kira Samir sosai tasha wahala Wanda sunso yimata opérations ama Allah yatakaita ta zubada jini sosai sai Samir ne yakara mata danashi tasamu yan biyu duka Mata sosai tasha kuka idan takali yaran taga sak kamar Ammi ce sosai suke kema da Irfan murna wurin Mama ba'a magana barema Abba shiyasanar da Abbi sosai yayi murna yafadawa Ammi dan kalonshi tayi saikuma ta kauda kai umarni yabata da suje asibitin kasancewar batamasa jayaya yasa tabishi ,koda sukaje Masha Allah anshiryasu tsap yaran cikin gadajensu ansaka musu kayan sanyi da hula sai baccinsu sukeyi„


Hakama Mufida baccin gajiya kawai takeyi gaisawa sukayi ama banda Ammi da suka gaisa kasa kasa dan sosai takejin nauyin su Abba Wanda har yanzu batawani iya hada ido dasu itakuwa Mama sam bahaka bane a wurin ta domin tafahimce ta„


Ahaka suka fita akabar Abbi da Ammi adu'a sosai yamusu hade da sakamusu albarka shima fita yayi ganin anakiransa awaya Ammi da batama kalon face dinsu juyowa tayi sosai tamatso take kalon yaran dasuke kama da ita sak kamar antsaga kara wasu hawaye masu zafi taji suna sauka akn kuncinta sharewa tayi hade da dokarsu daya bayan daya tayimusu adu'a albarka tasakamusu sanan tashafa kan Mufida hade da fadun “Allah yabaki lafiya ,ina tsananin kaunar ku ,ina kewarsu watakila yar uwarki itama ta haihuwa Allah y'a albarkaceku ,Allah yakareku ”tafada tana bawa Mufida kiss a goshi share hawayenta tayi tafita „


Tana fita Mufida tabude idonta hawayen ta tashare alokaci daya kuma tasaki murmushi ashedai Ammi nason su take boyewa yaran takala sanan kuma ta rufe idon ta„

Ahaka takoma gidan Mama Abbi baiyi musu huduba ba yace sai babansu yazo Samir  Mufida yatanbaya tace tana so asaka sunan Nasreen da Ammi sosai Samir yayi murna hakama Mama Wace Saïda taji kewar yar tata ama taboye ansakama yara sunn Ammi wato Zainab dakuma Nasreen inda ake musu lakabi da Shuraifa ita Zainab dun itakuma Nasreen zasuna kiranta dasunan ta ahaka akayi taro yara sunga gata sosai anci ansha andankarowa yara da uwarsu kaya Nagano na fada komi Samir ne yayi ama duk ahaka su Abba da Mama akwati daya daya suka hadama mai jego da yaran ta hakama Abbi millon biyar biyar yasakamusu a accunt dinsu Ammi kuwa akwati bakwai tayi na kayan yara su bibiyu uwarsu uku ankashe kudi sosai Wanda da'aka gwadawa momy da Dady saida sukajin jina al'amarin dady kan yayi fada akan kashe wanan dukiya danma ba'afadamishi kudin da Abbi yasakamusu a accunt dinsu bane anci ansha har abokan Nasreen munafukai sunzo sunyita tsegumi sungaji sunbari„


Mai jego nasamun kulawa sosai Wanda zaka dauka itadun kamar Mama ce tahaifeta yaran nasamun kulawa sosai dan kulum sai Samir yazo dubasu hakama su a'moctar har Ummi taje barka kuma ra bikima taje kuma tunda abun nan yafaru Mufida batasake tunkarata ba haka itama saita saita sukeyi ,Ammi ma bataske zuwa ba „


Wanda har fada Abbi yamata ama tayi biris wata rana sharifa tadokesu al'moctar dakanshi yakai su har gida sharifa na zuwa hardaki ta dorawa Ammi su saman gado alokacin kuwa tana bacciba sai tashi tayi taga yara nakuka kuma bataga kowba dukda ranan kawai tagansu ama tsan wadan jikokin tane daukarsu tayi tashiga jijigasu sosai sukayi shiru kamar suna hannun uwarsu kalonsu take cikin so da kaunar sosai tatuna lokacinda Irfan na jariri data jijigasa yakeyin shiru bayada hayaniya sam hakasuke kamar Mahaifinsu hawaye ta share hade dabasu kiss Sharifa ce wacce taboye à toilette tace “Ammi jikokin kine kidokesu kiji duminsu wadanan jinin kine ,jinin Yaya Irfan kidaina pretanding munsa kinason Yaya har yanzu dan Allah kiyafemesa bakisan wacce irin rayuwa yakeyiba a inda yake kiyafemasa kodan y'a y'an sa wata kilama baya cikin yanayi mai dadi ko yamutu”tafada tana kuka tass kakeji Ammi tadauketa da Mari tace “A'a yana Raye baimutu ba Na-san Irfan dina zaidawo gareni insha Allah nadade dayafemasa Allah ykereminsu aduk inda suke”tafada tana rungume Sharifa kuka sukacigaba dayi dakyar dai Ammi ta rarashi sharifa a ranar ana suka wuni sosai Ammi kebasu kulawa ko kuda bata bari yana tabasu„..


Abbi ma yayi murna ganinsu shima yayi wasa da yan jikokinsa gashi sam basuda kuka madara aka kadamusu sukasha Ammi dakanta tamusu wanka tamaidasu bacci sai kusan magrib Al'moctar yamaidasu inda sharifa tanunawa d'un kulawar da Ammi tabasu dan tadauka awayarta batareda Ammi tagani ba dariya kawai tayi hade dafadun “nasan dama ai tana sonsu nidai tunda tayafemana shikenan Allah yabayana mana su”da Ameen ta amsa „


Masha Allah yaran sunyi bulbul dasu sosai kuma sun saba da Mama da Ammi dnba ruwansu da uwarsu inba Nono zsushaba hakama m'Samir kan daukesu yakaisu gidanshi haka zasu wuni cikin gata dan matar Samir badai kirkiriba yaran sunsaba da Samir dan Papa sukecemishi dan harma sunfara iya tashi tsaye sunadan koyon tafiya duk randa ba'akaisu gidan Ammi bako akwai rikici dan ko minti uku ne sunfiso suganta suna Kiran Ammi itakuma zatayita biyemusu ne idan sun isheta da barna kuma zata kira Asidq tace “diyan nan naku sunfa isheni kuzo kukaima Mama can tayita fama”dariya Asidq keyi yace “haba Ammi kima gode Allah kina ganin anamiki Barna idan nayi Aure turai zamuje nadaukesu saiki daina ganinsu ma ai kinhyta sai kinkoma rokon amaido miki ”yafada cikin wasa dariya Abbi yayi yace “to Hajjiya nidai karkisa adaukemin su dan ni nafison inga suna barnar ina sayo wani”yafada yana fita waje ganin ana Kiran sallah magrub „




Dariya sukayi shima Yabi Abbi suka wuce massalaci la maman ce tadaukesu tamusu wanka dan yanzu itakadai suke yarda a gidan Ammi tana musu wanka dan kamar Sosa musu takeyi itakuma Ammi dakyau take darjesu ahaka dai yaran Masha Allah sukayi shekara biyu sunyi bulbul dasu kuma Mufida takoma gidan ta dai Wanda Abbi yaso tadawo ama taki doli suka kyaleta tazauna acan dun „


Samir doli shike kukada aikin asibitin dan dama yabada sako abwa Samir dun shine zairika kulawa da wurin kuma yake kulawa danashi kuma anasamun cigaba hakama yarinyar da Irfan ke kulada ita tasamu lafiya sosai domin al'moctar shike daukar d'un wani Abu nata Alhmdlllh hankalinta yadawo jikinta sosai kuma batamanta Irfan ba tunda Al'moctar yafadamata yayi tafiya bataji dadiba taso tamasa godiya ama ba hali duk dahaka Al'moctar yamaida duk hankalinshi gareta har su Abbi da su Rumasa iyayen margayiya Iklas kanje dubota ta warke garass saidai tace babu inda zataje hakan yasa Rumais taroki alfarma abata ita tarike Ammi taso daukarta Ama ganin Rumaisa na kuka yasa sukabata kasancewar batadakowa anan batasan kowa ba Masha Allah yanzu komai normal dan tayi bulbul abunta bazakata cewa anmata fyade ba Al'moctar yafada tarkon kaunar ta sosai yakeson ta haka itama batada magana sai tashi har Rumaisa taganosu tayi murna sosai tanadai jira taga lokaci dukda bata son rabuwa da ita dan tasaba da ita sosai hakama su hajiya iyayensu takan debemusu kewar Iklas„


Ahaka komi ketafiya normal sai dai damuwar Irfan dake damun Mufida wacce takansha kukan ta aboye tana rokon gafarar ubangiji„



Bari mu waiwayi su Nasreen „


Nasreen kuwa ta falawa Allah lamuransa tasan shine mai komi mai komai zaimata sakamako mai kyau„„

Da misalin karfe biyu na dare kasancewar lokacin Hadari ne yasaka Nasreen ta tatre komi takai daki garin ga zafi hakan yasa suka fito waje dan shan iska ana haka Ruwa yabarke dokarsu tayi takai daki bacci sukeyi sosai hankalinsu kwance banda Nasreen d'Atagama lazumi yanzu tadan karo kyuran kadan ama bata rufeba can cikin bacci taji ana diranyowa kamar ana jefo wani Abu.....✍️

Masha Allah yaran sunyi bulbul dasu sosai kuma sun saba da Mama da Ammi dnba ruwansu da uwarsu inba Nono zsushaba hakama m'Samir kan daukesu yakaisu gidanshi haka zasu wuni cikin gata dan matar Samir badai kirkiriba yaran sunsaba da Samir dan Papa sukecemishi dan harma sunfara iya tashi tsaye sunadan koyon tafiya duk randa ba'akaisu gidan Ammi bako akwai rikici dan ko minti uku ne sunfiso suganta suna Kiran Ammi itakuma zatayita biyemusu ne idan sun isheta da barna kuma zata kira Asidq tace “diyan nan naku sunfa isheni kuzo kukaima Mama can tayita fama”dariya Asidq keyi yace “haba Ammi kima gode Allah kina ganin anamiki Barna idan nayi Aure turai zamuje nadaukesu saiki daina ganinsu ma ai kinhyta sai kinkoma rokon amaido miki ”yafada cikin wasa dariya Abbi yayi yace “to Hajjiya nidai karkisa adaukemin su dan ni nafison inga suna barnar ina sayo wani”yafada yana fita waje ganin ana Kiran sallah magrub „




Dariya sukayi shima Yabi Abbi suka wuce massalaci la maman ce tadaukesu tamusu wanka dan yanzu itakadai suke yarda a gidan Ammi tana musu wanka dan kamar Sosa musu takeyi itakuma Ammi dakyau take darjesu ahaka dai yaran Masha Allah sukayi shekara biyu sunyi bulbul dasu kuma Mufida takoma gidan ta dai Wanda Abbi yaso tadawo ama taki doli suka kyaleta tazauna acan dun „


Samir doli shike kukada aikin asibitin dan dama yabada sako abwa Samir dun shine zairika kulawa da wurin kuma yake kulawa danashi kuma anasamun cigaba hakama yarinyar da Irfan ke kulada ita tasamu lafiya sosai domin al'moctar shike daukar d'un wani Abu nata Alhmdlllh hankalinta yadawo jikinta sosai kuma batamanta Irfan ba tunda Al'moctar yafadamata yayi tafiya bataji dadiba taso tamasa godiya ama ba hali duk dahaka Al'moctar yamaida duk hankalinshi gareta har su Abbi da su Rumasa iyayen margayiya Iklas kanje dubota ta warke garass saidai tace babu inda zataje hakan yasa Rumais taroki alfarma abata ita tarike Ammi taso daukarta Ama ganin Rumaisa na kuka yasa sukabata kasancewar batadakowa anan batasan kowa ba Masha Allah yanzu komai normal dan tayi bulbul abunta bazakata cewa anmata fyade ba Al'moctar yafada tarkon kaunar ta sosai yakeson ta haka itama batada magana sai tashi har Rumaisa taganosu tayi murna sosai tanadai jira taga lokaci dukda bata son rabuwa da ita dan tasaba da ita sosai hakama su hajiya iyayensu takan debemusu kewar Iklas„


Ahaka komi ketafiya normal sai dai damuwar Irfan dake damun Mufida wacce takansha kukan ta aboye tana rokon gafarar ubangiji„



Bari mu waiwayi su Nasreen „


Nasreen kuwa ta falawa Allah lamuransa tasan shine mai komi mai komai zaimata sakamako mai kyau„„

Da misalin karfe biyu na dare kasancewar lokacin Hadari ne yasaka Nasreen ta tatre komi takai daki garin ga zafi hakan yasa suka fito waje dan shan iska ana haka Ruwa yabarke dokarsu tayi takai daki bacci sukeyi sosai hankalinsu kwance banda Nasreen d'Atagama lazumi yanzu tadan karo kyuran kadan ama bata rufeba can cikin bacci taji ana diranyowa kamar ana jefo wani Abu.....✍️

PAGE 49&50
          .......✍️jitayi anturo kyauran ahankali wuta taga ankuna haskene ya hasakasu wasu zaratan maza tagani tsaye tsaye saman kanta „„

Su hudu ne sanye suke da bakaken kaya sun rufe fuskokinsu da tafukan hanayensu kowane rike yake dabindigarsa sai idanunsu da'ake gani Nasreen kuwa “innalillilahi tashiga nanatawa ”hankalin tane yayi mugun tashi bindiga suka doramata akai „

Daya daga cikinsu ne Wanda ga dukan alamu shine ogansu yace “ke Ina kudi ”yafada cikin dan tsawa hade da dora dan yatsarsa abaki alamar kartamusu ihu „


Bakinta takama wasu hawayene taji suna zubomata ahankali tace “Babu ”kasancewar ana tsala Ruwa babu Wanda zaiji ama tsawar dayamata daba ruwa akeba da anji„


“ina kudi nace kona fasa kan nan naki”yafada yana kara saita kanta kanta takara girgizawa alamar babu,daya daga cikine yace “haba oga kafasa kanta kawai”umarni yabasu akan su binciki dakin sugani babu bata lokaci suka shiga bincike ama basuga komi bâ haskawa yayi yaga su Nana kwance kaf yakaremusu kalo kasancewar da zafi yasaka Nana darma gaba Wanda Nasreen tasha yimata fada akan haka ama sam bataji saitace zafi yaw ma darma gaban takeda tana rungume da Mimi sai papa dayake bayanta sunsata atsakiyar su„

Mask inshi yacire yabushe da dariya yace “tunda bakida kudi zanfanshe akan yarki”yafada yana nunamata Nana wani irin tashin hankali tashiga hade da fashewa da kuka tace “dan Allah kuyi hankuri karku lalatama y'a ta rayuwarta dan Allah karku keta mata hadin ta kuyi hakuri”tafada tana hada hanayenta biyu alamar roko hade da fashewa da gunjin kuka Wanda yatada Nana„

“ummu lafiya suwaye wadanan?”

Tafada tana karasawa kusada Nasreen ta rungume ta ,Nasreen dunma rungume ta tayi kam ajikin ta tace “babu komi Nana kiyi adu'a aranki”tsawa mutumin yadaka musu yace “ke kumin shiru ko nafasa kwakwalwarku”yafada yana saita daidai kwakwalwar su kuka nana tafara tana adu'a aranta hade da rungume Nana sosai “nace uwarki tabani kudi tace babu shine nace zanfanshe aknki kingane me nake nufi?”yafada yana zaroma Ta ido kanta tashiga girgizawa alamar a'a murmushi yasaki yace “ina nufin zan kwanta dake duk da yanzu kifara kirgan dangin ma bakida wani Abu na cikakiyar y'a mace”yafada yana kokarin fisgota ,Nana yatinyace ama tanada fahimata kuma tagane Miyake nifi hakan yasata kara rungume Nasreen Tana kuka tace “ummu dan Allah karsumin komi ,kayi hakuri karkamin komi dan Allah ,wayo Abbu kaceceni”tafada tana shigewa jikin Nasreen Wace itama tariketa gam dakarfi yafisgota yadora akass yadaneta ,Nasreen kuwa zatamuke tayi kan ta yaranshi suka saita ta kuka tafashe dashi tace “dan Allah karku mata komi ku kyaleta ga wata y'a ta nan ku fanshe akanta”tafada tana doko Mimi „


Jijigata tashiga yi Wanda yasa ta tashi Allah sarki ganin uwar tata nakuka yasata itama fashewa danata kukan tace “ummu na waye yatabaki na bashi kashi”duk da shekarun su biyu ne da yan watani ama dawayonsu sosai rungume ta tayi tana rarashi hade da fadun “babu komi Mimi uncle dinkine zaibani chocolat shine fah yace saikinje wurinsa idan bahakaba bazai bani ba kuma ga Anty nana ma yace saikinje zai saketa”tafada cikin karfin hali„


Oga kuwa bakaramin mamaki wanan al'amarin yakebashiba itakuwa wanan uwar wane irin son m takeyiwa wanan Babar yar tata har tazabi yaketa hadin yar ta yar shekaru biyu bayan tasan idan yahayake mata kasheta zaiyi„


Yasha kissa ama baitaba fyade ba yanzuma baisan abunda yasa uakeson yin abunba abunda baitaba yiba fyade saidai kissa ama banda wanan jiyayi yarinyar nafadun “Uncle gani to dan Allah kabawa su Ummu chocolat dun ni gani”tafada tana hawa jikinshi sabida zaune Yakima yana mamakin wanan al'amarin „


Kyakyawane sosai ajin farko farine ama ba sosaiba kamar bafulatani yake bashida jiki saurayine dabazai wuci shekaru ashirirn da biyu ba „


Nana ce tayi Saurin doketa daga jikin shi tana fadun “a'a ka kyalemun kanwa karka kasheta nikamin kaji ka kyale Ummu batada lafiya ne”tafada tana dorawa Nasreen Mimi „


“ummu kiyi hakuri kibari yayi kasheta zaiyi idan yamata kinji kimin adu'a ”tafada tana kalon Nasreen hade da rungume ta „

Mikewa tayi hade da doramasa Mimi tace “gatanan kayimata abunda kakeso ”daga haka taja hannun Nana dake kuka wiwi tana turjewa sukayi Fallon akabar su kam ta rungume Nana dake kokarin kwacewa tatafi wurinsu „


Shikuwa gogan haka kawai yakeji idan baiyi fyaden nan ba bazaiji dadi ba Mimi ce ta rungume shi tace “Uncle ganito kabawa Anty na chocolat dun tadaina kuka kaji”tafada tana taba sajenshi wandonshi yazage hade da fido sandar girmanshi dake Mike yace “kikama wanan kishamin zanbasu chocolat”Allah sarki Mimi batasan wanan abun ba haka takama tana dansha dukda bata shiga bakinta yaran nashi kuma dama nawaje sun azawa su Nasreen bindiga„


Kusan minti ashirin tafara kuka alamar bata so dokarta yayi yadora akan abunsa aha'kali yake shafamata bisa Virgin dunta ahankali yake tirawa Wanda yasata fashewa da kuka a hankali yakeyi Wanda tuni yashigeta da karfi wani irin kuka ta tsala Wanda yasa Nasreen rufe kunan ta Nana kuwa da karfi ta kwace tayi cikin dakin kanshi tayi tana dukansa ama shi baimasan tanayiba kusan minti ashirun ya tireta ganin batama ko nunfashi „

Da Sauri yazari wandinshi sukayi waje Nana ce tadoki Mimi ganin tayi kaca kaca dajini bata ko motsi „


Daukota tayi tadorawa Nasreen cikin kuka tace “ummu Mimi ,Mimi bata motsi Ummu mukaita likita”tafada tana jijiga Nasreen dun Wace tayi suman zaune tun likacinda taji kukan Mimi na karshe „

Papa ma yatashi sai kuka yakeyi ganin owa na kuka kama Mimi kuwa ganin Nasreen bata kulata ba yasata dokarta tayi waje da'ita kasancewar yanzu andaina Ruwan sosai sai yayafi da'ake ahaka take tafiya duk da duhun farine gashi anyi ruwa bakajin motsin komi sai itatuwa dake kadawa „


Bata KO ganingabanta kasancewar tasan gidan Irfan dun dan kawarta dake ynguwar ta taba Nunamata tace Ashe nan ne gidan Abbun ku da Eh kawai ta'amasa bawai dan tasan nan dun bane„


Tafe take tana faduwa duk tabace da laka da jini „


Kwankawasa tayi ama shiru batayi kasa guiba tacigabda da bubigawa iya karfinta „

Irfan dake bacci cikin falon yana mugayen mafarkai yaji bugun kofar dakyar yasamu yakuce daga mafarkin zaune yatashi yana adu'a can yakoma jin bugun kofar rigarshi yasaka kasancewar dagashi sai dogon wando fitowa yayi yakusa kawo wa yaji bugun da karfi„


Jiyayi Nana na kwalamasa kira batadamu da dare bane tace “Abbu Abbu wayo Abbu kana inane Mimi zata mutu tabarmu”


Aida gudunshi yakarasa yana budewa Yaga Nana rungume da Mimi duk jikin ta yabace kamar wada aka kwato daga bakin kura, karbata yayi itama Nana saigata tazube ajikinshi daya hannun yasa yatarbeta bata fadun kasaba dayan kuma yana rungume da Mimi „

Wani irin tsorone da tashin hankali suka shigesa miye yake faruwa badai wani Abu bane yasamsu .....✍️

BOOK 2
PAGE 51&52
           .......✍️inallilahi yashiga nanatawa hankalinsa yayi mugun tashi ganin jini na zuba takasan Mimi gashi ko motsi batayi„

Dubawa yayi yaga Mike faruwa abunda yagani ne yasashi shiga tashin hankali Wanda tunda yake baitaba shigaba mihakan kenufi Mimi ,MiMin sa fyade adu'a yashiga yi cikin ransa idonshine suka kada sukayi jajur baya ko ganin gabansa yakoma da Mimi ahanunsa kasancewar ga jiyan dama yasiye babu wani tshoho akan zai rage wani Abu kuma yana son saka su papa makaranta gashi makarantar bakusa bane abunda yasa yasiya kenan„

Da saurinshi yadauko makula yatada Bâbur dun dikon Mari yadauko ya daure Nana abayansan dan karta fado yasaka Mimi agaba sai hospital yana zuwa yasauko su dokar Mimi yayi yasuke Nana kasa kamar gawa yayi ciki da ita karbata Nurse dun sukayi cikin gagawa Doctor sukazo dan anadamusu caiss dun fyade ne„


Céwa sukayi bazasu tabata ba matukar babu yan sanda wata irin shaka Irfan yamasa idonshi sunyi jajur kamar wuta yace “idan ta mutu na rantse maka da Wanda raina yake hannunsa kaf danginku sai na kulesu sainasa anma kisan wulakanci”yafada yana kara shakesa likitocin ne suka kwacesa yana nishi suka karbe ta kayan aiki suka dauko suka shiga bata taimakon gagawa „


Wani kyakyawan saurayi ne baki ne ama ba sosaiba yasha gayu cikin kananan kaya kan nan nashi yasaha gyara sai kamshi yake zubawa sanye yake cikin farin glass Wanda sukayi matukar kamashi sosai yafito kamar bakin ba fulatani yazo shiga yaga kamar mutum kwance da Sauri yakarasa yarinya yagani sharkaf kamar wada aka kwato daga bakin kura subhannallahi yafada kalon fuskar ta yayi sosai tamasa kamada wani Wanda yasani ama yamanta waya yakira saïga wasu nurse Uku umarni yabasu suka dauketa hade da dorata saman gadon„

Binsu yayi abaya yashiga wani dan daki madai daci inda anan suka dorata taimakon gagawa suka shiga bata sam batama nunfashi dakyar suka samu nunfashin ta yadai daita ajiyar zuciya yasauke yazauna bisa madai daicin d'gadon daya karemata kalo yashiga yi shikan fuskar nan kamar yataba ganinta yadai manta ne kawar da tunanin yayi ya fito zaije duba wasu marasa lafiya yaga Irfan saikai dakawo yakeyi kamar wani zaki fuskar nan tashi jajur „...

Kalonshi yayi hade da saké murza idonshi kodai gizo abun kemasa ba Yaya Irfan bane kara kalinshi yayi eh tabass shine baiyi wata wata ba yadauki wayarsa office dunsa yakoma yakira kusan minti uku yakashe yashiga nanata wanan al'amarin badai duk bidan nan da'akema Yaya Irfan yana garin nan ba „


Kassum ne wanan tunanin dan kan shi baisan shiba sosai so daya yataba ganinshi danshima lokacin Auren su yazo shima can yake wurin Imran yana karatu dan uncle Rabi'ou ne „


Yaji anbada cigiyar sa suma sunsha nema ama shiru tabass shine saidai yayi baki„


Irfan kuwa yarasa awace duniya yake sam yama manta da Nana da Nasreen jin Kiran sallah asuba yasashi barin wurin sam hankalinshi baya jikin shi adafe yayi sallah sam baimasan Miyake fadaba Saïda yazo wucewa kusan Bâbur dun nashi yafara tunanin Nana dububata yashiga yi ama sam baigantaba tanbaya yakeyi ama babu Wanda yacemasa yaganta hankalinshi ne yakara tashi„


Kaf asibitin yaduba ama babu ta adu'a yakeyi sosai aranshi yaw tazame masa bakar Rana shikan wacce irin jarabawace Allah yajarabesa da ita yazaiyi da rayuwarsa duniyar kunci takemasa waye zai rarashesa babu yabawa kan shi ansa „

Bakin kofar dakin da akashigar da Mimi yake tsaye wasu likitocine suka fito kansu yayi ganin sunyi shiru rigar mutunan guda yakama ya daukesa da mari yace “Mimi na bata mutu ba ,duk Wanda yacemin ta mutu sai na kashesa ”yafada yana nuna yan da zai kashe sa cikin dakin yashiga yatarara ansa mata wata na'ura a kasanta ga oxygène da taimakonshi take nunfasawa„

Sosai jikin shi kerawa komai nashi yasaki bashida wata madogara sai ta Allah daba ya daba ya yakeja har yafito dataimakon dan acaban yazo gida Wanda shima yadaiyi niyar taimakasa ne ganin kamar baya hayacinsa „


Su iya asbe ne keta sabgar abincinsu na saidai saidai Baisan ina Nasreen dinshi takeba yasan ko inatake bata hankalinta yana son ganinta kozata rarasheshi domin yasan tafishi jarumta itadun tamkar uwace a wurinsu itace madogarasu baiko kali su Iya asbe ba yayi cikin dakin ahankali yashiga dakin „


Nasreen yagani zaune takankame jikin ta wuri daya tana kalon gefe idonta sun bushe ko kyaftawa batayi dukawa yayi hade da dora Kansa saman cinyarta yace “Nasreen komi yabace komi nawa kara rugujewa yakeyi zunubi nane kuke karba kulum nine bakincikik ki nikan wane irin mutun ne mai saka butulci da alkairi dama zina bashice Na-san wanan ranar zatazo ama meyasa sai yanzu meyasa bata bari sai muna kusada Wanda zasu rarashemu ba Nasreen meyasa bakibada Nana ba sai Mimi kekan wacce irin zuciya Allah yabaki ,Meyasa sai Mimi meyasa sai Mimi Nasreen ”yafada hawaye na sauko mai daga gefen idonsa papa ne yatashi dama yagaji dakuka yakoma baccin wahala ganin bamai rarashinsa„


“Abbu Mimi ”papa yafada yana kuka hade da fadawa kan Irfan yashiga rera kukansa „

Nasreen kuwa duk tanajinsa tasan dayanzu kan tarasa yar tata bamakawa Mimi tabarsu„

“Nasreen kifamun ni Azzalimi ne ko maci Amana Mara tausayi kaicona Ammi na kiyafemun kozanga dai dai Na-san fushinkine ke bibiyaya kiyafemun Ammi na ina zanganki kirarasheni”yafada yana kara rungume su Nasreen da papa dakejikinshi jiyayi ance “Ammin ka tadade da yafema jarabawarkace hakan yarona ,Ammin ka zata kasance atare dakai aduk lokacinda kakeda bukatarta”Ammi ce mai wanan maganar wacce hawaye na tsiyaya daga saman fuskarta wasu nabin wasu „


Nasreen da Irfan gabadayansu suka Mike tsaye Wanda yasa papa kara fashewa da kuka ganin sun yardashi kasa kuma yaji ciwo„

Ahankali Ammi ta tako zuwa garesu tass kakeji tadauke Irfan da mari har sau biyar kan Nasreen takoma itama tabata Mari uku hawaye na zubomata ta fashe da kuka kamar yan da daga Irfan dun har Nasreen ke kuka kamar yara “ashe zaka iya fushi da Mahaifiyarka datayi wata tara tana rainoka cikin cikinta ,tayi shekaru tana baka kariya ,ta kwana tanama adu'a bata bacci matukar bakda lafiya tashiga damuwa matukar bakada lafiya ,tayi kuka idan baka kusa da ita meyasa meyasa yarona kabarni da tunanin kana raye ko kamutu ,meyasa y'ata zata biyewa Tunanin Mijin ta bata tunanin hlinda iyayen ta zasu shiga akanta”Ammi tafada tana kara rungume su ajikin ta suduka kuka suke babu mai rarashin wani kusan Minti goma sukaji ance “idan kingama naki hukuncin zanyi nawa”Abbi ne mai wanan maganar yana kalon yaron nashi da surukar tashi ta gari da sasarfa suka karasa kusan Abbi kasa suka kai kansu saiji sukayi Abbin ma yarungumesu yana fadun “kulum Ammin ku saitayi kuka akanku takansha tanbayr kanta wane hali kuke ciki unci abinci kunyi wanka kuna inane mikukeyi duka tana tanbaya kuntafi kunbar iyayenku cikin kunci yarona Ashe zaka iya fushi da iyayenka”yafada goge musu hawayen „


La Maman ce tadauki papa tana fadun “taho yarona waye yatabamin kai fadamun na dakesa ”tafada tana jijigashi luf yayi yana ajiyar zuciya itakuwa ko kalonsu Nasreen batayiba ba tace “Abbi muje mubarmusu gidan nasu mana meyasa mukazo ma nidama yarana nazo doka danhaka muje kar Yaya Samir yaki maida mu”tafada tana yi waje da papa duka hankalinsu suka maida gareta masha Allah tazama budurwa kyakyawa ajin farko kan shi Abbi ya girgiza yace “kowa fushi yakedaku Samir nawaje baima shigoba”Abbi yafada yanajan hannun Nasreen da Irfan dun sukayi waje inda sukaga su iya Asabe cirko cirko suna kalonsu

Irfan kuwa Ammi yakala yace “wanan iyayen Mata tace ta uku Ammi kimin duk hukuncin dakike so ama karkisake fushi dani”yafada yana kara damkar hannun ta hannunsau takara damkewa tajasu har cikin Mota  Musadiq ne ke dama shiyafadawa Abbi shine sukayi samko sukazo kuma saidai yabiyo bayan shi saiko gasu Ammi cikin jirgi nan take yabawa abokinshi umarni yakai musu motar kuma yasamu wani yazo dasu cikin wata motar mafarin sukazo cikin motocinsu  shike  driving sai La maman dake gaba doke da papa ,sai Abbi da Ammi Dayar motar kuma Samir ne ke driving sai Abokin Musadiq sai su Irfan da Nasreen abaya „


Irfan kuwa Samir yagani yakara jiki yayi bulbul abunshi saidan fuskarshi data fada alamar damuwa atatare dashi kuma duk akan Abokin nashine wato Irfan„

Ko kalonsu baiyiba suka tada mota mamaki sosai Irfan keyi Samir ne yaganshi kuma yake kauda kansa bayason ganinshi ,Zaijuri komi ama banda fushin Shakikin shi kuma Aminin nasa massoyin nasa shiru bamai wani magana har suka karasa su Abbi ne agaba dan Musadiq yabibiui abunda yakawoshi asibitin kuma anfadamasa shine yama Abbi da Ammi bayani Ammi harda kukanta domun ta tausayawa yarunyar suna shiga sukaji Nasreen na fadun Ammi Nana bara nakoma nanemota bansan inda sukajeba tafada hawaye na wanke mata fuska rungume ta Ammi tayi tace “babu komi kinji ainan aka kawosu suzamu duba”Irfan ne zaiyumusu bayani sukaji Murya Nana na kwalama Nasreen kira Wanda ga dukan alamu ta farkane kuma nurse dun nabiye da'ita sabida dama yace akula da'ita dagudu kamar yan da ita ma Nasreen tabudemata hanaye suka rungume juna sosai Nana ke kuka tana fadun “ummu Mimi ta mutu dan Allah kikauni wurin ta idan naga wanan mitumin da hannuna zankasheshi sai nakashesu saina konesu ”tafada tana gwada yan da zata yayanka su sosai take kuka ,Nasreen ce ta rungume ta tace “Mimin ki bazata mutu ba kidaina kuka haka Nana karjima kanki ciwo idanma ta mutu adu'a zakimata”jin abunda Nasreen kefada na in ma ta mutu adu'a kadai zata mata yasata jadabaya zata ruga Samir yariketa tana ganin shine ta kara fashewa da kuka tana kokarin kwacewa cikin kuka tace “kaikuma wanene sakeni duk bakuzoba lokacinda ake ketama kanwata mutunci tana kuka ama ku kuma kunacan kuna bacci sai yanzu zakuzo”tafada kamar wata mara hankali sosai Samir ke kokarin ganin yatsaidata ama sai kokarin kwacewa takeyi „


Jitayi Ammi tariketa kalon kekuma wacce ce ta mata sanan tace “Malama sakeni kekuma wacce ,kedun nan cefah wacce kika zagi Abbu na kika kirani da yar Zina ban mantaba bakida wata alaka dani ”ji Kake Tass Tass Irfan yadauketa da Mari Wanda yasa ta yin shiru harsaida ta hankada hade da dafe kunci kusan minti daya kowa shiru kakeji can sukaji Nana takama Hannun Nasreen tana fadun “Ummu na muje muje yaw kinga harda Abbu na kedukana akan wacce takirani da yar Zina tadaki Mahaifina ta zubar Miki da babyn ki kizo muje mubarsu bamason su ”tafada tanajan hannun Nasreen „


Gani tayi Nasreen na girgiza mata kai alamar a'a bazata jeba hakan yasa ta saki hannun Nasreen dun tafara ja daba ya daba ya tana fadun “shikenan zanje na nemi Mimi mukoma gida nizan kukada da'ita ku kuma kubisu dama dan ina yar zina ce ba shiyasaka kuka raboni da Mahaifiyata Na-san da'itace bazata ki bina ba ”tafada tana kokarin guduwa da Saurin Nasreen ta damkota tasakata ajikin ta tana kuka tace “ashe Nana zaki iya tunawa da akwai wata mahaifiyar ki datake son farin cikin ki bayan ni ,Nice fah ummun ki mai son farin cikin ki haba lelena”fadar Nasreen 

Kusan minti goma suna kuka sanan Nana tashiga sharema Nasreen hawayenta

BOOK 2
PAGE 53&54
         .........✍️“kiyi hakuri Ummu ba banida kamarki kiyafemun dan Allah bazan sakeba ummi”yafada tana kama kunan ta murmushi Nasreen tayi tace “yawa yan matana kibawa Abbunki hakuri hakama kibawa Iyayena hakuri sune suka haifemu idan kinmusu badai daiba doli zamuji ciwo mudauki mataki koma wane yace zaitaba su bare kuma yar mu, sanan kedun ba yar Zina bace ga Abbunki ga umminki mune Luka haifeki bakida wasu bayan mu idan nasakejin kince haka ko kinma wani rashin kunya ba ruwana dake ”Nasreen ta fada ta na nunamata su Ammi alamar tabasu hakuri„

Ammi kuwa datayi suman tsaye jin abunda Nana ke fada wato Kenan lokacinda da tayi ta dukan su Nasreen nada ciki kuma ya bare sosai jikin ta yakara yin santi tayi nadama sosai yanzu gashi yarinyar tarike abun aranta shekaru biyu ama bata manta ba meyasa tayi hakan jitayi ance “kiyi hakuri kakus bazan sakeba ,umma tabani tarbiya Raina ne yabace kiyafemun ko kuma nakima zuwa gidanki”tafada tana kama kunan ta ta shagwabe fuska murmushin karfin hali Ammi tayi tace “aikuwa idan ma baki zoba zansaka adaukomin ke dakarfi dama nafiki nauyi duk da na tsufah”tafada tana saka Nana cikin jikin ta haka taroki Abbi ma gafara Samir kuwa ko kalonta baiyi ba sai ma juya mata baya dayayi ganin haka yasata hawaye tana kalon iyayen nata alama Nasreen ta mata dataje gabansa taduka aikuwa da Saurin ta ta dawo gabansa yakara juyawa itama tajuyo kalon ta yayi sanan ya stuke fuska hawaye tashiga yi ta fashe da kuka tace “Dady kayi hakuri bazan saké ba dan Allah ,kasan Allah nason masu yafiya”tafada tana kara rungume shi„

Shima rungume ta yayi yace “shikenan Angel dina nayafemiki ama jarkisake”yafada yana bata kiss agoshi jisukayi an kwala ihu dagudu Irfan yashiga dakin„


Mimi ce keta kuka tana fadun “wayo Anty cikina Abbu”tafada tana kuka idon ta rufe Samir ne yashiga bata taimakon gagawa Irfan kuwa Abbi ya rungume yana kuka yana dora duka laifin kansa ana haka su Uncle Imran sukazo dasu hajjo harda Jauro yana ganinsu kuwa yayi kansu yana rungumesu dai bayan daya duka sun yafemishi zaune sukaji jugum jugum suna jiran fitowar su Samir dun Nana kuwa na rungume ajikin Abbi sai shasheka takeyi kusan awa guda suka fito gabadaya kowa yayi kansu yana tanbaya zufah Samir ya shafe sanan ya kalli Abbi yace “Abbi jibi zamu wuce America”daga haka yasakai gaba yabarsu „

Anan suka wuni cikin jimami Nana ce kawai adakin da Mimi take dan kuka yasha sosai akan saidai abarta wurin ta kamar yan da ta ce bazata yi kuka ba sukuwa sunadaga waje banda Irfan sam ranshi bayamasa dadi ganin Samir baya kulasa hakama la mama duk fushi suke dashi sai wajen Magrib yatuna yabar Mari agida kobakomi tana hakki akansa tunowa da ita yasa Mufida tafado masa a Rai gabansa ne yafadi ganin ga kowa banda ita yana son ganinta yana son jin muryata yasan Mufida zata zauna tajirashi bazata yi wani Auren tabarshi ba duk da kuwa bayamata son dayakema Nasreen ama itadun wani jigone a rayuwar sa yana sonta Dube Dube yafara ama sam bai hangotaba yana son tanbayar inatayi ama wazai tanbaya banda la maman saikuma Samir gashi suna fushi dashi„

Daga massalaci ya wuce wurin Mari tundaga falon yaga alamar anyi rawa sabida falon a hargitse yake tsaki yaja yazauna yana tunanin rayuwa wani bangare yakanji farin ciki zaikoma cikin ahalinsa wani bangare kuma sam baya masa dadi yarasa miyasa „


Mari ce dokeda abinci sai karu yakeyi tsakin dayaja ne yasata juyowa ba zato kwanan yafadi harara yamaka mata yace “mtsss shasha Ashe bakida hankali bakisan idan Mijin ki bai wuni lafiya ba kizo kinemi shi ko ,narasa minagank ajikin ki na Aure ki ,kishirya anjuma zamuje wani wuri”yafada yana barin wurin „


Soshe soshe tafa ra tana gunguni bai kulataba yayi dakin shi wanka yayi yashirya dakinta yaleka yaga tana kokarin saka mayafi yace “minti uku nabaki”daga haka yabar wurin harara tabishi dashi tana fadun “yo cemaka akayi inason tafiya ne mtsss Sainaga dama zanzo”tafada tanamai cigaba da mitarta sai kusan minti shida takamala baicemata kalaba yaja motar dakarfi sai hospitalier dun shine gaba tana biye dasu abaya har sukashigo dakin da famillyn suke „

Da salama yashiga gaishesu yashiga yi suna amsawa banda Nasreen data kawar da fuskarta kamar bataganshi ba „

Mari kuwa tunda tashigo tayi salama bata kara gaisheda kowaba saima tsaye datayi akan su harara yazabgamata hakan yasata dukawa tace “sannunku”Abbi ne kawai ya'ansa sai Jauro da tsohouwa„


Kanshi yasosa yace “Mai ran karfe ga Matarka nan daya”yafada yana dukat dakai„

Dariya Jauro yayi sanan yace “Anyadai wanan zata iya zama dani kuwa dan naga batada kai”yafada cikin wasa ama magana ce yayi„


Ahaka dai bawanda yasake cewa komi kowa yacigaba da Firar sa Nasreen kuwa mikewa tayi dan taleka su Nana bayan minti uku Irfan yasulali Jiki Yabi ta bayan kowa yasan inda zaije„


Mari kuwa dama tasan wurin Matar sa zashi wani Irun kishine ya turnike ta ga Shi taga alamun yan uwan nan nasa basa sonta„


Nasreen kuwa tsaye take ta waje tana kalansu ta madubi cikin tausayawa tana tsananin tausayin yar tata wasu hawaye ne taji sun sulalo mata itakuma tata kaddarar kenan „


Jitayi anrungume ta tabaya Wanda koba'afadamata ba tasan wanene “Komi zaiyi dai dai Komi zai wuce zamudawo fiyeda da Mimi mu zatasamu lafiya„yafada yana kara rungumeta ajikinshi„


A hankali ta banbare hannun ta daga jikin shi tace “Nima nasani komi zaiyi daidai tunda nadawo cikin ahalina komi zaiwuce Na'auri Wanda zai kuladani Wanda zaiyi adalci atsakanin matan sa bamai son kansa ba zanyi wani Auren insha Allah”tafada tana kalonshi cikin ido....✍️

BOOK 2
PAGE 55&56
          .......✍️wani irin tafarfasa yaji zuciyarshi nayi ranshi ne yabace yaushe Nasreen tazama haka agabanshi take fadamasa zata rayu da wani bayan shi bazata rayu dawani ba karyane „


Tass yadauke ta da Mari cikin tsananin kishin dake cinsa yace “karya kike wallahi bazaki rayu dawani ba matukar ina raye idan nafara noma agona babu Wanda ya'isa yashigeta yayi noma kobayan bana raye kuwa jarumi daya akeyi akarni daya kuma wanan jarumin nine”yafada yana fido ido waje kamar zaidaketa„


“dama kai mikasani banda son kanka kulum abunda kakeso shi kakeyi Ni Nasreen doli kasakeni na Auri wani”tafada cikin kuka tana barin wurin „


Naushi yakai wa iska hade da hura lumfashi mai zafin gaske Yajuyo Kenan yaga Nana tsaye idon ta na zubar da hawaye dan ta juyo Kenan tagansu tsaye kuma kamar Ummun ta na kuka mafarin tafito kenan„


“Abbu mai yasa kulum kai kesa Ummu kuka Abbu kaji tace zata rabu dai idan ta Auri wani tatafi damu gidan ta bazai somuba kamar yanda kake son mu ba haka kawata take shan wahala ahanunun Mijin Babar ta ,Dan Allah Abbu kabawa Ummu hakuri karta tafi tabarmu itakadai keson mu”tafada tana kuka sosai dukawa yayi dai dai tsawon ta yace “Hakika ni kulum mai zalunci ne agareta na Rabota dakowa nata na kaita wata rayuwa wacce bata saba ba nayi Aure bata hanamin ba ,na yimata hukunci bayan tana kan gaskiyar ta kulum nine silar Kukan ta Nana ina son ummun ku yazanyi tayafemun ”yafada hawaye na zubo masa ,hawayen tashiga share masa ta rungume shi kusan minti biyu sai can tace “Ummu na sonka Abbu bazata taba rayuwa dawani bakai ba ,alokacin dakayi Aure kabarmu kulum takan tasheni tace Nana ina son Abbunku duk da abun nan dayakemin zansadaukar dakomu nawa danshi yayi farin ciki duniyar dababashi tamkar wuta nake kalon ta ,zata sauko Abbu ka gyara kuskuren ka Abbu kaji”tafada tana kalon cikin idonsa„


Shikan yanzu yatabatar da Nana tafara wayo fiyeda tunanin shi Allah yayi zata zo tawanan hanyar Nana batada laifi a abunda ya aikata baya fatan Allah yahukunta ta akan Abun„

Murmushi yayi yace “Babyn Uncle tafara wayo Ashe sai Aure”yafada yana taba kuncin ta ,fuskarta ta rufe tana dariya tace “a'a Abbu nibazanyi Aureba Abbu zan zauna nayita kulada Mimi ne har tasamu lafiya”tafada hawaye na gangaro mata 

“Abbu idan naga mutumin nan wallahi Abbu saina ilata shi saina sashi yaji kunya”tafada tana barin wurin„

Kanshi yajinjina yana tunanin Nana wani likacin sai yaga kamar fadamata kalamai nan akeyi ya Mike Kenan yaga Mari saman kanshi„

Harara tazabga mishi hade dayin tsaki tabar wurin mamaki ne yakasheshi shi Mari ke zabgawa harara haka kamar tasamu warin ta lalle bata gurzuba zatagane shayi Ruwane „


Ganin kowa ya wuce massalaci yasaka shibinsu shima sai kusan karfe tara da rabi sukashigigo Nasreen ,Hajjo ,Ammi,sune zasu zauna da'ita sai Musadiq da Samir acikin Maza kowa aka maidashi gida Irfan kuwa yanata neman yanda zasu kebance da abokin nashi ama sam Samir yaki bashi damar hakan badan yasoba Ammi tace yaje gidansa waya baroma iyalansa badan yasoba ya je dan yanason zantawa da Ammin sa akebance„

Ama taki bashi dama gida yatarar da Mari kwance saman kafet tanaganinshi ta mike tarike kugu“Kaga Malan nagaji da karuwanci da kuke yi ko kunya kawani rungume ta agaban mutane mtsss sai kace wani sakarai”tafada tana jijiga jiki„


Murmushi yasaki sanan yama tso yadamko gashinta yace“karuwanci ma iya wane tunda da Mijin ta tayi karuwanci in fitsari banza ne kazama tayi ,sanan nine sakaran ko yaw zantabatar muki bakisan wanene Irfan Ali Nabazzaga ba ko banaa duka ko zagi saidai Mutum yaji ajikinshi”yafada yana daukarta da karfin tsiya dakinta ya wuce directe dama daga ita sai wata fifigiyar riga sai dan wando yaga rigar yayi yashiga aikin cinta yana so yamata kwatan kxacin cinda yakewa Mufida dan yasan inyamata Wanda yakema Nasreen saidai wata ba'ita ba Mari duk sonda takema sex yaw taji tatsane shi domin bugu'ta sosai Irfan keyi babu kyakawtawa sosai yabata kashi da sandar girmanshi sai kusan karfe daya yasarara mata hade dajan tsaki yayi dakinshi dan sam baiji wani armashiba„


Wanka yayi hade da yin nafilfili sai kusan karfe biyu yakwanta Mufida ce tafado mishi a Rai yasan harda hakkin ta kebibiyar shi lalle yazama doli yasan halibda take ciki domin yashaida itadun mai kaunar shine tanamishi son so yanamata so 30daga cikin Dari„


Saikuma daga daya bangaren Nasreen dunshi idankomi yadai daita yasan zasudawo fiyeda dama„

Ahaka bacci yayi awan gaba dashi„

Ta bangaren Nasreen kuwa taci aniyar bazata koma ba gidansa tagama zama dashi Auren da bakomi acikinsa sai tarin kalubale zata zauna dashine idan harshi bai juya mata bayaba ama yanzu yajuya babu wani sauran zama a tsakanin su„


Da safe wajen karfe takwas ankamala shirye shiryen tafiya Samir Musadiq Nana Ammi sai hajjo ne zasuje amarica dun „

Irfan tun karfe shida yazo ama duk da haka yatarar da mutante motoci shida ne sukaje rakiyarsu har aéroport dun bayan kowa yafitone „

Mimi ce akafito da ita bisa gadon asibiti KO motsi batayi sosai hankali duk wani mai rauni yatashi„

Nasreen kuwa Labé take bayan Ammi wasu zafafan hawaye na zubomata na tausayin yar tata „

Jitayi ankamo hannun ta bakowa bane face Nana janta takeyi har gadon da Mimi take tace “Ummu kimata adu'a kidaina kuka namiki alkawari zan kulada ita fiyeda yanda zankula dakaina,Mimi zata warke ama idan kunamata adu'a fah zata rayu insha Allah kisakamuna albarka”tafada tana hawaye hade dakama hannun Nasreen tadora akan ta„


Sosai Nasreen ke kuka Wanda yasa duk wani mai rauni a wurin zubda hawaye har Abbi saida hawaye suka zubomai ama yayi Saurin gogewa “Allah ya albarkaceku Nana keda yan uwanki nasani kobana raye zaki kula dakanan ki Allah yabata lafiya”tafada tana bawa Nana kiss a goshi da gudu tabar wajen takoma cikin mota taci gaba da kukan ta,kuka take sosai kuka take sabida tausayin yar ta,tana kuka sabida watakila wanan shine karshen haduwar su,tana kuka sabida tsoron halin da Nana zata shiga idan tarasa yar uwar ta sosai take kuka “na yafemiki Mimi ne idan haduwarmu alkairi ce aduniya Allah yahadamu idankuma sai mungamu a can Allah yasadamu cikin jannatul fidausi na yafemiki Allah yabaki lafiya”tafada tana kwantawa jikin kujerar taci gaba da kukan ta„

A wurin jirgin kuwa Nana ce takoma gurin Abbunsu ta kamo hannayen shi tagama da nata Dana Mimi tace ta'anso papa dake hannun la maman tace “kayimuna adu'a kai Mahaifinmu ne adu'ar ka itace zata zame mana garkuwa Abbu ka kulada Ummu karoketa tayafema mudai mun yafema ga kanina nan Amana ka kulamin dasu karkabari Ummuna tasake zubda hawaye Abbu kasakamuna albarka”tafada tana hadasu su uku„


Hawaye ne ke saukowa akan ido Doctor shikan yarasa dawane baki zaiyi magana ba mizaicema Allah idan yatanvayesa hakkin y'a y'an sa cikin karfin hali yashafa kansu yace “Allah yabawa Mimi lafiya Allah ya'albarkace ku ,ki kulada kanku Allah yasa abunda kukaje nema kusamu” sosai yamusu adu'a sanan yaja dabaya shima yakoma mota„

Nana ce tazo gaban su Ammi hade da rike kunan ta ta shagwabe fuska cikin yarinta tace “kuyafemin kakani na natuba nayi furcin dabai daceba akanku Ummu na tajan fadamin yiwa baba rashin kunya rashin tarbiya ne nikuma Na-san Ummu na mai tarbiya ce kuma tabani kuyafewa iyayena please ”tafada hannun ta Ammi takama hade da shafa fuskar ta tace “Manyan yara basa kuka insha Allah Mimin ki zata samu lafiya” Abbk ma adu'a yamusu sosai sanan suka shiga jirgin papa harda kukan shi akan sai yabisu„


Nana ce da Musadiq karshen hawa har tashiga ta fito bakin matata kala cikin kuka da daga murya tace “Abbu kayi kar kamanta ka kulamin da Ummu na karkasake barin tayi kuka”daga haka takoma cikin Jirgin„

Su Ammi kuwa jinjina kauna dake tsakanin Nana da Nasreen sukeyi mota suka shishiga suka koma gida„


Irfan kuwa duk sakon da Nana kebashi yaji sosai yaji har yafara kewar yaran nasa ahaka suka koma kowa jiki ba kwari„


Ammi kuwa rarashin Nasreen tashiga yi tana bata baki ahaka suka karaso gida da zumar jibi zasu koma can Zinder dun.....✍️




 MAMAN FAUZAN CE🖊️DOCTOR IRFAN🥼
