BOOK 2
PAGE 57&58
       ........✍️Nasreen kuwa yanzu batada wani buri daya wuce taganta jikin Iyayen ta tana kewarsu tana son jin muryarsu tana kewar kanan ta anan bangaren hajjo takwana „


Papa kuwa yana wurin la maman dan yasaba da'ita tsakanin jiya da yau 


Nasreen ce kwance wajen karfe tara ta rufe idon ta tana lazumi ama sam ba kwana takeba la maman ce tashigo da papa da salama ganin kamar yana bukatar Mahaifiyar shi duk da bawai yana rikicibane dacewar hakan tagani„

Papa kuwa yanaganin ta yayi gunta da Sauri sabida sun iya tafiya yanzu ,la maman kuwa ko kalon Nasreen batayiba tayi baya harzata Budé kofar taji Nasreen tace “haba Autar Ammi hardake fushi kike damu, haba lelen Abbi kiyafewa yayun ki mana hakika munyi kuskure muna neman afuwa kimuna duk hukunci dakike so ama bazamu juri fushin kiba”tafada tana kama kununwan ta alamar neman yafiya kamardai yanda yara keyi„


Hawaye ne ke saukowa akan idon la maman a hankali ta juyo takaraso kusa da Nasreen tace “hakika munsha kuka ,kuma kulum cikin shi muke sabida rashinku kusa damu kusan shekaru biyu da watani zucuyoyinmu cikin alhini suke sabida bamuda tabass kuna raye ko kun mutu,Anty Nasreen kuntaba tunanin halinda zamu shiga bakutaba yi ba ama mu kodayaushe cikin naku muke shin kun kyuatata ma zuciyoyin mune?”tafada tana kalon ta kai Nasreen tashiga girgizawa hawaye na zubomata rungume juna sukayi suna hawaye “na yafemuku Na yafemuku kunemi gafara iyeyen ku shine mai mahimanci”fadar La Maman 

Kai kawai Nasreen kedaga wa alamar insha Allah „

Ta bangaren Irfan kuwa yaso yadawonan dazama ama Abu yagagara sabida Ammi tace sam saidai yakoma gidan shi bashida wani zabi dayawuce hakan „

Yana komawa yatarar babu Mari baice komiba yayi wankansa dama can yaci abinci kwanciyar shi yayi sai kusan karfe takwas tashigo bata aza zata tarar dashiba saiji tayi yace “karo na farko kuma na karshen dazaki fita batare da Izini naba ”
“koda gidanmu ne?”ta tanbaya
“koda gidankune”shima yabata anda kai tsaye „

“hmm wanana ne kuma mutum bai isaba”tafada tana barin gurun dan tsoronshi take yanzu sosai tsaki yaja yacigaba da duba computer shi„


Da asuba kuwa yafadamata da tashirya da zasu koma can inda iyayensa suke sanan ta shirya duk wani Abu datake bukata „


Tunani Mari tashigayi karfa taje can inda babu danginta amata wulakanci sosai tashiga tunani harta doki waya takira Iya asabe kusan minti shabuyar suna magana sanan takashe„

Da safe kuwa wajen karfe goma Ammi tasa akai Nasreen tadoko duk wani abun bukata ta itada la maman sukaje„„

Suna shiga suka tarar da mutanan gidan kamar kulum gaishesu Nasreen tayi hakama la maman duk da mutanen basu kwanta mata a Rai ba ama haka tagaishesu Nasreen saka yaro tayi yadauki kayansu nasakawa da duk abunda zasu bukata kayan kitchen dunta kuma bata dauki komi ba haka ma matala da sauran kayan aiki kayansu kadai tadauka suma sababin tabar tsahin sunzo taga wucewa Jamila tafara yabamusu habaici tana zagi akan adaiyi adalci duk wada taci amanar yar uwasu basu yafeba „


Nasreen dai bata tankaba sai la maman ce wacce sam bata daukar wulakanci tace“ aikin banza inda ba'asan asalin kuti ba datace da madina tafito aikin banza kawai harmu za'agwadawa hutsara wacce iri ce bamu iyaba”tafada tana harara jamila 
“ke karamar yar iska wallahi zanyi kasa kasa dake nan wurin waye sa'anki”tafada tana kule kugu„

Nasreen ce taja hannun la mama tana fadun “kuyi hakuri yarinta kedamun ta”sai kokarin kwacewa da la maman keyi ,Jamila kuwa taci damara sai zage zage sukeyi itada yan uwanta har iya asabe ma na dan habaice habaice “Mtsss ke tsohuwar banza miyakai shiga maganar yara aikin banza ahaka za'akare” la maman ce mai wanan maganar ,karaf sai a kunan Irfan jikake Tass yadauketa da Mari ranshi abace yace “Yaushe kikayi girman dazakina fadun irin wanan magan ganun Ashe Al'moctar basu kuladakeba abunda kika gadama kike ko”yafada cikin hasala„


Nasreen kuwa batace mishi kalaba taja hannun la maman dake kuka sai cikin mota rarashin ta tashiga yi ama kamar kara ingizata akeyi suna shiga gida tayi kan  Ammi tana kuka„


Hankalin Ammi ne yatashi dan rabon dataga kukan Autar tata ta manta cikin rashin hankali tace “fadamun Auta na waya tabaminke ko bakida lafiya ne”tafada tana tataba jikin ta„

Bawani rashin lafiya sai iskanci nan take yazayenema Ammi abunda yagani dan duk abunda yafaru tundaga farko har karshen akan kunan sa „

Ammi kuwa ranta ne yabaci tasan Autar ta bata fada akan karya yanzuma tasan itace mai gaskiyar hannun ta takama suka shige daki dama Nasreen part dun Hajjo ta wuce danbatasan mizataceba „


Irfan kuwa yagane fushi Ammi tayi ama hukuncin dayayi yasan akan dai dai Yale „

Momy kuwa da marece tasaka dreban ta yadauko mata Nasreen da papa harda la maman Wanda adadare take ama ganin yanda Momy kejanta jiki yasata sakewa sosai tayi mamakin gatan da tanuna mata itama kawai sai tasake sukayi girki tare papa kuwa kamar yasan ta yamakale mata shopping sukaje tayimusu sayaya wada dakyar Nasreen tasa tabari har su Nana da babusu saida tayimusu nasu shopping taso takwana ama Jauro yace maza tadawo sabida karfe bakwai jirginsu zaitashi badan Momy taso ba ta yafi Dady ma kyutar dubu dari biyar yabawa papa yace yasiye chocolat godiya sosai Nasreen tayi Momy kuwa harda runguemta daga karshe sukayi bankwana Nasreen nayaba irin halacin Momy duk da dama can baya bawai tamata wani abun bane„

Tanadawo wa taje part dunsu Abbi tagaishesu anan tabar papa shida la maman wacce taketa fushi da yayan nata „


Wajen karfe goma kuwa Ammi tasa aka kiramata Nasreen babu bata lokaci tazo Ammi na bakin gado zaune itakuwa na kasa babu yanda Ammi batayi da'ita tazauna bakin gadon Ama taki„

Shuru ne yabiyo baya sai can Ammi tace “Hakika ba'abunda zance miki Saïda namiki adu'a y'a ta a kulum idan nayi sallah nakan rokarwa y'a y'a na samun Mata na gari burin kowace Uwa kuma Alhmdlllh nayi dace ,Irfan yasamu mace ta gari wacce tasan kima da darjar Iyayensa samun kamarki sai antona,kinzauna dashi adadi kuma cikin wahala ,nayimi asarar babyn ki Wanda bacikin sani bane ama kika yafe kike zaune dashi ,shine yakawomiki yar sa ama kikarike ta baki kyamace taba sabida hanyar datazo Allah yamiki albarka”Kanta ta dukar kasa cikin jin dan nauyi tace “Ammi Nana y'a tace koba komi atsakanina da Abbun su ,Ammi bakimin komi ba ke uwace ,uwace wacce samun kamar ki sai antona kiyafemuna mune zamurokeku gafara amaimakon mubaku lokaci ku huce saimuma mukayi fushi Wanda hardashi duk matsalar damuke shiga dan Allah kuyafemana Ammi”tafada hawaye na zubomata saukowa Ammi tayi daga kan gadon tarungume Nasreen dun tana shafa bayan ta kusan minti uku suna ahaka sanan Ammi takoma kan gadon tace “Nadade da yafemuku Allah y'a albarkaceku”tafada tana murmushi godiya sosai Nasreen tayi sanan taskoma sashin su Hajjo acan takonta cikeda mafarkin gobe zataga Famillyn ta„


Asuba nayi Irfan yakai Mari har gida bayani yamusu hade dacewa duk kayan da'akabari ayi sadka dasu ,sanan zaitafi da Matar shi,Dubu dari biyar yabawa Iya asabe yafita yabar ta tayi bankwana da yan uwanta„


“to kingadai zakije inda babu iya bare baba kikulada wadanan jajayan mutanan danaga basuda alamar sonki kikama kanki kegadai kekadaice ga wanan ”Iya Asabe tafada tana mikawa mari Wata Léda kusan minti talatun suna magana sanan suka rakota har bakin motar Wanda saura kadan takaici yakashe Irfan „

Su Ammi kuwa dama duk sun shirya sukadai suke Jira  ,a aéroport suka hade dan takwas saura Wanda bakaramin haushi Ammi tajiba kodama sukazo gaisuwar su kadai ta'anasa Nasreen kuwa dagashi har Mari basu isheta kaloba Allahn Allah take taje taga Ahalin ta sosai take zumudi karfe bakwai Jirginsu yadaga zuwa baban Birnin Zinder....✍️




🖊️MAMAN FAUZAN CE🖊️DOCTOR IRFAN🥼

BOOK 2
PAGE 59&60
           .......✍️karfe shadaya Jirginsu ya sauka Ababan Birnin Damagaram sosai farin ciki yacika zuciyar Nasreen harda hawaye sukazobo mata kasar databari batare da tana cikin hayacin taba yaw gashi tazo cikin hankalin ta tareda dukan farin ciki„

Dama Abbi yaturo adaukesu motoci uku ne suka zo daukarsu tafiyar minti shabiyar rakaisu har gida„

Sunayi parking suka fito mai gadine yazo yana kwasar gaisuwa hakama ma'aikatan dukawa sukayi suna gaisheda Irfan duk da ancanja wasu ama akwai wasu haka gidan ancanja mishi peinture ga fulawowinan ako ina har girma Ankara mishi„

Ciki su Abbi sukashiga hakama Nasreen da la maman dake daukeda Papa Wanda sai rikici yake akan asaukeshi yaga tsunstaye abunda baisaba ganiba ama taki sauke shi„

Irfan ma abaya yabiyosu Asidiq suka tarar yanan kalon kwalo yana kurbar Jus jin salamar Abbi yasashi mikewa gabanshi ne yafadi ganin Wanda baiyi zatoba dan dama kuma koda sukatashi shida Al'moctar basu gansuba Saïda gabaya ne suka shaidamusu tafiya ce takamasu Ummi kuwa cewa tayi batajin dadun Jikin ta duk dakuwa basu fadamata inda zasuba hankalin ta yatashi ama dagabaya tabasar„


Asidiq kuwa dukawa yayi har kasa yana gaishedasu Abbi cikin sakin Fuska yake ansamusu ,yi yayi kamar baigansuba yakamo hannun la Maman yanafadun “Autar Ammi a'ina kika samo maikama da Yaya Al'moctar harma yafi shi kyau”yafada yana karba Papa Wanda sai kalon Asidiq yake na rashin sani duk dayaga kamarsa da Abbun sa luf yakwanta akafadar Shi kamar yasansa„


“wanan ai yaro nane bakaga munakama ba”tafada tana shafa bayan papa dayake kan kafardar Asidiq „


Asidiq kuwa yafahimci tabass wanan dan na Yaya sune Irfan tunda gakama nan sanan gasuma Nana yamikawa shi ama yaki tafiya kamar yasanshi“a'a lalle wanan yaron darigima kake to aiki yasameku tunda Allah yabaku da makale mata”Ammi tafada tana hawa sama shi Abbi dama yadade da hawa „

Irfan kuwa mamaki ne yakashesa yanda yaga Asidiq yazama saurayi mazan takar tasa ta fito sosai harda aje wani gemu ,yazama saurayi sai Aure kuma hakan na nufin baigansu bane yasharesu„

Nasreen kuwa cikin dari dari tace “Yaya Asidiq ina kwana ”tafada tana goge hawayen ta ganin yashareta kamar baisan da'ita ba bayan ada yafikowa bata girman ta yakanyi faran faran da'ita fiyeda Al'moctar „


“lafiya”kawai ya'ansa yana kokarin hawa saman benen yaji anfisgoshi da karfi„


Tass Irfan yadaukeshi da Mari idanun shi JaJur yace “Asidiq nikake cewa baka ganeba nine nazama kamar shara a wurinka haba kanena kaine kafi kowa Jin maganata kaine kafikowa sona acikin Yan uwana kaine nafi shakuwa dashi haba Kanena Ashe zaka iya fushi da Irfan dinka ,Ashe akwai Ranar dazata zo kamaidani shara ninefah Irfan dinka”yafada hawaye na zubomishi„

Jiyayi Asidiq yafada jikin shi yanamai fashewa da kuka sosai yake kuka kamar ancemasa Irfan dun yarasu ,Irfan dunma hawaye ne keciga dayimasa anmbaliya kusan minti uku Asidiq yace “bazan manta dawanan fuskarba ,bazan manta Irfan dinmu ba, meyasa katafi kabarmu bayan kulun sai munyi kuka akan rashinka haba Yayan mu ”yafada yana mai goge hawayensa „

Bubuga bayanshi Irfan yayi yace “kuyi hakuri kanaina Ina sonku nima daku nake kwana nake tashi a cikin Rai na Ina sonku kuyafema Yayanku”yafada yana gogema Asidiq hawayen shi„

La maman ma kuka takeyi sosai ,jin kukan nata ne yayi yawa yasa Irfan kalon Asidiq yace “kaga Auta ma fushi take dani ,taki tayafemin ”yafada yana tabe labansa kamar zaiyi kuka ,Dariya ce takubucemusu suduka lamaman mikewa tayi tana dagudu tafada Jikinshi tana maicigaba da kukanta rarashin ta yayi hade da rokon gafarar ta kuma tayafemasa Asidiq ma Yaroki gafarar Nasreen dukda bawani abun yamata „

Ana Haka Sharifa da Al'moctar suka shigo suna dariya sabida bakaramin dariya da shirmen su Nasreen karama da mai sunan Ammi yabasu ba„


Cak suka tsayarda dariyarsu sunamai karema mutanan falon kalo„

Sharifa kuwa kalotake karema mutana falon sosai tafahimci wadanan mutanan Wanda ko a Mafarki tagansu zata ganesu ,kawar da kalonsu tayi tashigo falon tana dukawa kadan da gaida Asidiq tanamai Rungume La Maman cikin dariya tace “gidan bayamun dadi idan bakyanan Yar uwata kinbarni da Yaya al'moctar sai nikadai yake duka hakama Yaya Asidiq baya barina ina zuwa wurin Anty”tafada tana kokarin Jan hannun La maman subar wajen„

Al'moctar ma tsab yaganesu ama sai yabasar yana mai fadun “a'a baki mukayi sannunku da zuwa”yafada yana gaishesu hade da dokar papa dake kamadashi sosai dan koshaka bayayi wanan dan Yayan shine„

“Kai wanan dai yana kama da Yaya Irfan badan baya rayeba da nace wanan dansa ne,koba hakaba ne kanwata”yafada yana dakatar da sharifa dake kokarin hawa saman benen gaisheda su Ammi„

Juyowa tayi da murmushi a fuskar ta tace “hakane kan Yaya Al'moctar Allah yajikansu”tafada tana sakarma Papa dariya dake kokarin zuwa wurin ta„

Karbashi tayi hade da fadun “masha Allah kyakyawa dakai ”

Irfan kuwa yasan duk abunda sukeyi shakiyancine yagane sarai bawai danbasu ganeshiba ne „

Nasreen ma haka daurewa tayi tace “inayini Yaya Al'moctar ”tafada tana dukar dakanta kasa„

Amsawa yayi yana kokarin barin wajen ,“Allah sarki nikukewa kamar bakusani ba Ashe lokacin zaizo dazuk shafeni ,Ashe kalon matace kukemin ,lokaci zaizo dazu manta ni,ninefah Irfan dinku haba kanaina kuyi afuwa ga yayan ku kutafemasa”yafada yana matsowa kusan su Hannu yabudemusu suduka sukafada jikinshi suna kuka „

“bazamu mantaku ba da tunanin ku muke kwana dashi muke tashi meyasa katafi kabarmu batareda kasan halinda muke ciba ”Al'moctar ne mai wanan maganar„

“bazan saké ba kutafewa Yayanku”duka yahadasu yana rarashi Kan Nasreen Sharifa takoma tana fadun “nayi kewarki Anty na kulum nakance Na-san yanzu kin haifamuna Babys masu kama dake Ashe hakane saidai kashe Yaya Irfan yayo Allah yasa kada yayi zuciyar shi”tafada tana kara daukar papa tabashi kiss„

Irfan kuwa shida kanan nashi maza duka zaune suke kusadashi sunsaka shi atsakiya suna Hirar yo sharbe „

Sharifa ce tace “Anty banga Nana ba a'ina kukabarta”hawaye ne suka fara zubowa a'idon ta nan take kuma tafashe da kuka Wanda yaja hankalinsu Irfan„

La Maman ce tashiga rarashin ta dakyar tayi shiru labarin abunda yafaru tun barinsu gidan har abun dayafaru ama bata fadamus sauyawar da Irfan yamata ba„

Kuka sharifa da la maman sukasha ,hakama su Asidiq sunyi hawaye kuma sun Jijinawa hakurin ta sanan kuma sunji itadun Alkairi ce a rayuwar Yayan nasu dasuma ahalinshi sosai suka tausaya mata hakama Yayan nasu ,nan take suka shiga neman Mari dudubawa sukayi basuganta waje sukaje Ashe tanacan wurin dawisu abunda bata taba ganiba koda akashigama dama ita bata bisu ba„

Kiranta Irfan yayi tana tai tana dariya tana kalon dawinsu gaisheta sharifa tayi sannu kawai tace daga haka ,su Al'moctar ma gaisheta sukayi ,shikuwa Irfan harara yayita zabga mata„

Falon suka koma sukuma part dinsu sukaje da Irfan dakuna Uku ne a part dun dakinshi suka nunamasa Wanda yanana yanda Yale sai dai ancanja ma kayan zaman wurinsu kuma ancanja komi nadakin tsap sai kanshi turaren wuta yakeyi koba afadamasa ba yasan aikin Ammin shine„

Bisa gadon yakwanta yana tunanin rayuwa yanzu yaw shine cikin ahalinsa,bisa gadon gidansu Alhmdlllh yafurta„„„


Nasreen kuwa dakin la Maman takwana kasancewar basu shiri da Mari dukda ba'ita bace tamata „

Mari kuwa dakin sharifa takwana kasancewar yanzu kowa dakinsa kuma la Maman ce tace arabamusu sanadin sharifa nakawo kawayen ta suyima dakin kacakaca mafarin Ammi tasa akayima sharifa nata dakin „„

Da asuba kuwa massalaci suka wuce basudawo ba suduka sai wajen karfe bakwai ,Abbi sama yawuce dakinshi,hakama su Asidiq kasancewar Lahadi yasa basa zuwa aiki„

Nasreen kuwa suma sunjima da tashi suna lazumi har karfe shida suka shiga kitchen itada la maman su sharifa makara sukayi dama ita Mari sai Safiya takeyin sallah ko ayi wata ,wata ba'ayi„

Kan karfe takwas sun kamala komi sun jera dama sai tara ake haduwa daninig „

La Maman kuwa papa tayiman wanka ganin yatashi shiryashi tayi tasaka masa kayansa sosai yayi kyau abunshi sai kamshi suke zubawa suka sauko yana sai ta goyashi itakuwa taki sai dariyarshi takeyi suna saukowa Asidiq na saukowa shima sai kamshi yake zubawa gaisheshi tayi ya'amsa yana daukar Sa ganin yana fushi yace “My boy waye yatabamin kai Baba na fadamun yanzu na ramama”yafada yana bashi kiss a goshi

PAGE 61&62
               ........✍️“anty ce baja ta goyani ba”yafada yana murza hannun sa akan cikin idon da jaye hannun Asidiq yayi yace “shikenan my boy kyaleta karkadamu zansiya ma chocolat anjuma kuma bazaka bata bako”yafada yana wucewa dashi daning dun zama sukayi yadora papa akan cinyar shi ,su Abbi ne suka sako harda Ummi wacce batasan dazuwansu ba ta tafi yawon bikin nata kamar yanda takefada sai cikin dare tazo Wanda kowa yayi bacci har Abbi ma dayaketa jiranta„„

La Maman ce tagaishesu hakama Asidiq „

Ummi kuwa gaban tane yafadi ganin yaron yana kama da fuskar datafi tsana aduk duniya sosai tarasa nutsuwar ta cikin in ina tace “kai kuma a a ina ...ka samo...wanan yaron”Dariya Asidiq yayi yana kara rungumo Papa jikinshi yace “kicanka ummi dawayake kama” cikin dan dariya Wace daga gani kasan ta muna furci ce tace “Irfan ”tafada cikin dan daga murya Wanda yaja hankalin kowa hata Irfan da Nasreen dasuke shigowa yanzu„„

Da murmushi akan fuskarsa yakarasa yana zuwa ya rungume Ummi yace “Haba Ummi na Ashe kinaja haryanzu kintaka dai dai yafada yana dan jadabaya yaduka kadan yagaisheta hade da fadun “nayi missing dinki ummi na nayi missing d'un game dinmu ”yafada yana kara murmushi„

Ammi kuwa murmushi tayi domin tafahimci komi ,Nasreen kuwa dukawa tayi tana gaishedasu Abbi ,hakama tazo gaban ummi tana gaida ita wacce tayi suman wucin gadi „

Asidiq ne yace “ummi lafiya kuwa”firgit tadawo hayacinta tana kame kame hade da fadun “sa...sannunku da zuwa ”tafada cikin in ina„„„

Sosai hankalin ummi yatashi kuma Irfan yafahimci haka sai satar kalon Irfan takeyi da papa kasa zama tayi sai dibar nata datayi tayi part dinta„

Kowa a wurin cikin nutsuwa yakecin abinci „

Kofa akabude akashigo Al'moctar ne dauke da mai sunan Nasreen ga mai sunan Ammi wato shuraifa Wace ita aganinta ta girma tabarwa Nasreen karama karamta taki adauketa sai dariyar shirmensu yake „


Shuraifa ce tasaki hannun shi tayi gun Abbi tafada jikinshi dama shiyakoma falon tana fadun “Abbi nikan nagirma yanzu kagama yaw Uncle baidaukeni ba”dariya Abbi yayi yace “yawa Matata aidama nasanki da daukar girma sannu”tafada yana zaunar da'ita kusan Shi jikinshi ta rakume tana kalon su Al'moctar dake shigowa„

Da salama yashigo gaisheda Abbi yayi yana sauke Nasreen karama itakuwa dagudu tayi gun Ammi tana fadun “Ammi kinga nace Moma takaini gidan Mama taki wai saidai takawoni wurin Anty sharifa bayan karatu takesamu”tafada tana leken sharifa dan tasan sarai idan sunzo karatun alkur'ani takedan koyamusu gashi sunada fahimta shiyasaka suke rikewa sosai dukda basu shiga scool ba„

Dariya Ammi tayi tace “to kwantar da hankalinki kishiya yaw ba ina gidaba nidakaina zankoyamuku”itakuwa Nasreen karama dariya tayi har dimple dinta yaloba kamar na Nasreen baba karaf kuwa idonta yasauka akan papa dashima yaketa kalonta kamar tasan shi„

Leken shi taketa yi dariya yasakar mata haka itama kokarin sauka yake ganin haka yasa Asidiq saukeshi wurin su yakarasa yana leken su karasowa yayi yamikama Shuraifa hannu labewa tayi jikin Abbi tace “o'l
O”dariya yayi mata yakoma wurin Nasreen karama ma yabata saikuwa tamika mishi nashi karba tayi sukayima juna dariya„

Irfan kuwa tun dayasauke idonshi akan Yaran yaji gabanshi yafadi wanan yaran koba tantama y'a y'an sa ne Jikin shine yafara rawa hankalin shi yatashi hakan na nufin Mufida ce tahaifamasa wadanan yaran kanshi yashiga girgizawa„

Nasreen ma sosai taga kamaninsu dasu Mimi bashaka  wanan y'a y'an Irfan ne to yaushe aka haifesu„

Hakan na nufin Mufida ce tahaifesu„ bata karasa tunanin taba taji ana fadun “ Ammi wallahi nakusa maidomuku yaran nan sama yara sai barna nikan narasa wasukayi wurin kirita ”tafada tana shigowa tana mai lalabur wani Abu cikin Jakarta „

Gaban Irfan ne yasake faduwa ganin Mufida ,tabass bazai manta wanan fuskar ba ko a mafarkine kuwa Mufidan shine tazama haka itace takara jiki tazama wata big girle kamar wata hajiya itace tayi wanan kyan haka Wanda gashi yanuna ajikin ta dandama tanada kyanta rama ce kawai da'ita yanzu kuwa Mama ta gyarata ta ciko tako ina„

Sosai yake karemata kalo sanye take cikin wata bakar shada kasancewar ta fara sai color dun yakama ta„


Tasaka sarkarta da yan kunan ta na zinariya mayafi baki tasaka da Jakarta sai madai datan takalmanta Wanda basuda tsayi kasancewarta mai tsayi karawa kawai zatayi jiyayi zuciyar shina bugawa„

Wani kamshin turaren yasake ji Wanda yasashi kalon bayan ta wani kyakyawan mutum ne Wanda bazai wuci shekaru talatun da Biyar ba dakadan Irfan zaifshi„

Farine ama ba Tass ba dan Irfan yafishi fari dogone ama basosaiba shima sanye yake cikin bakar shada sai kamshi yake zubawa da salama yashigo jerawa sukayi suna dariya abunsu kamar wasu ma'aurata dukawa sukayi suna gaishedasu Abbi amasawa sukeyi cikin kulawa jade da tanbayarsu lafiyar gida„

Irfan kuwa wani irin tafarfasa zuciyar shi keyi jiyayi kirjinshi namishi zaf„

Fuskarshi Jajur yake kalon Su sukuxa basuma san yanayiba cikin izza yamiki yayo falon dan dama banisa da daning dun duk anaganin kowa kuma anajin kowa „

Karasawo yayi ya fuskanci Mufida sai huci yakeyi baisan yana son taba sai yaw Ashe  haka haka so yake „

Mufida kuwa kalonshi tayi da murmushi akan fuskarta tace “Ammi baki kukayine sannu” tafada tana yabamasa wani murmushin „

“Eh Yayankune Irfan ai jiya mukadawo”Ammi ce mai wanan maganar „


Murmushi tayi sanan ta kali Wanda naga sunzo dashi tace “Habibi ga Yaya Irfan Wanda nake fadamaka yayi tafiya Allah yayi yadawo”tafada tana sakarma mutumin murmushi Wanda ke kara bayarnar da hutun datasamu„


“Allah sarki habibaty sannu yayan mu”yafada yanamikamasa hannu „

Irfan kuwa dayacika saura kiris yakejira yafashe tsaki yaja yana fadun “banson haukar banza wanan wayene karkicemin Aure kikayi cikin Aure,Wallahi baki isaba mikeke takama dashi inanan a matsayin Irfan dina bancanjaba”yafada cikin tsawa idanunshi sun firfito„


Abbi ne yace “lafiyarka kuwa ,kaga zauna nama bayani”cikin fushi yakara juyowa yace “Abbi dubi fah gabana Mufida kefadawa wani Habibi ,Habibi fah Abbi Mata ta dakanta ,Abbi bansaketa ba wallahi ,kuma Aure sai yayi shekara hudu ake rabashi idan mijin yabatane,nukuwa gani shekarun basu cikaba,Abbi wadanan yaran nawane kobaku fadaba gashi sunakama dani ,wallahi Abbi yarana ne wadanan ”yafada yana daukar Shuraifah da Nasreen karama yasaka ajikinshi„

Luf sukayi kuwa kamar sunsanshi,hawayene ke zubomishi tabasa komi zai faru bazai rasa matar saba duk dabawai yana son ta bane ama wanan y'a y'an sane yanaji ajikinshi”


Hawaye keyi sosai ashedai yanada rabo awani gun Allah sarki shidinkan yasan yatabata mai sa'a 

Ammi ce tayi gyaran murya hakan yasahi gyara zamansa “da farkodai ina so kasani Mufida a yanzu ba matar kabace katafi kabarta baka wai waitaba tana bukatar Aure shiyasaka bayan tahaifi wadan yaran Wanda sukaci sunanana dana Nasreen ,mai sunana munamata lakabi da shurafah,itakuwa Nasreen karama mukan kirata da sunan ta ,ga Mijin tanan wanan shine abunda yake faruwa”Ammi tafada tana kalon dan nata„

Irfan kuwa a zabure yamike cikin fushi yake fadun “A'a Ammi nifah bansaki matata ba wallahi Ammi niban saké taba karkurabi da uwar y'a y'a na karkuyimin haka ba'a Aure akan Aure”yafada yana ja dabaya fita yayi daga falon„„


Nasreen kuwa gaban Mufida ta duka tana fadun “Anty dan Allah meyasa kikayi haka please karkimishi haka Abbun su baitaba mantawa dakeba yana sonki dan Allah kakirabu dashi”tafada tana gama hanayen ta alamar roko„„

Mufida kuwa rungume Nasreen tayi tana fadun “kiyi hakuri yar uwata kedun tadabance kidaina damuwa dashi ni yanzu nayi Aure na kinga Mijina ma”tafada tana nunamata mutumin datake ikrarin Mijin tane„

BOOK 2
PAGE 63&64
           .......Papa Mufida tadauka tanamishi kiss a goshi hade da kalon Wanda takira da Mijin nata tace “Habiby tashi muje karmu makara”tafada tana mikewa hade da dokar Jakarta sunamasu yiwa Abbi Sai ajunma„

Nasreen kuwa sokoko ta tsaya tana kalon ta saida suka bacemata sanan tamaida kalon ta ga yaran Wanda sukaci sunansu hakika taji dadi kuma koba afadamata tayarda da tuban Mufida jitayi yaran sunshiga ranta sosai taji tana kaunarsu dokarsu tayi ta dora akan cinyarta ,su Abbi kuwa da kalonso da kauna sukabita Ammi ce tace “to Asidiq ka kare kakaisu Gida mana”dato ya'ansa„


Nasreen kuwa dadi taji sosai yaw zataje taga Ahalinta suna nan zaune dai har kusan karfe goma shadaya Asidiq yace suje waya tadauka tayi ta Kiran Irfan tafadamasa sabida tasan har yanzu yana da iko da'ita ganin baya dauka yasa tabarwa la Maman wasika akan idan yadawo tamikamishi karba tayi itakuwa sukawuce itada papa da Asidiq tafesuke yana nunamata canje canjen da'akasamu ,sosai suka shiga gari dakyar tagane hanyar dankomi yacanja„„

Parking taga yayi hakan yasata fahimta inda suke gidan takarewa kalo Wanda sosai aka maidashi baba harda get baba da karama kasancewar bazai shigaba dan zashi wani meeting na gagawa yasashi parking bakin kofar fitowa yayi tana biyedashi Papa na rikeda hannunsa ya kwanwasa wani bakin mutum ne sirara ne yaleko dariya yayi yanafadun “a'a Malan Asidiq kaine lalle sannu da zuwa kushigo”yafada yana basu hanya hannu yabashi sukayi musabaha shima cikin dariya yace “lafiya klw sannu Malan nuhu ,a'a nidai wucewa zanyi Abba yanaciki ko”yafada yana kalonshi„

Cikin sakin fuska yace “eh suna ciki dukda dai naga yaso fita ama naga yakoma wata kila yafasa”wabda aka kira da Malan Nuhu yabashi ansa„

“yawa wadanan bakasansu bako,wanan yar Abba ce wacce nake baka labari itada Yaya na”yafada cikin dariya “yawa kacemusu sunyi bakki”karaf kuwa Nasreen wacce har hawaye sunfara zubomata tace “a'a Yaya Asidiq kabari naje nayimusu surprise”tafada tanayi cikin gidan da gudun ta„

Kanshi kawai Asidiq yagirgiza yana fadun “ka kaimata papa”karbashi yayi yanamasa baye baye da zumar zaiso masa chocolata„

Nasreen kuwa tana shiga gidan farin ciki yakara mamayeta ganin irin daular dasuke ciki kofar datake ganin itace zaisadata da su tashiga wani kayatacen falon tagani sosai yayi kyau komi anzuba kamar gidan wani baban ma'aikaci„„

Saï kamshi turare ketashi Su Abba tagani gabadaya zazaune suna kalon TV tashar sunna TV ,Rufaida kuwa tana duba wani littafi hakama Imran dake game„


Hawaye takeyi sosai mai gameda dadariya ihu tayi sosai Wanda yasasu Abba maido hankalinsu gareta“Abba ”tafada tana ruguwa da gudunta tafada jikin shi kuka tafashe dashi mai karfin gaske,Kuka take na farin ciki yaw itace agidansu a Jikin Abban ta ,komi zai sauya tasani kuma lokaci yayi ,Abba ma shafa bayanta yake ama kamar tunzurata yakeyi sakinshi tayi tafada Jikin Mama tana kuka Mama ma kuka tafashe dashi ,“Mama na nayi kewarku ,Mama kuyafemin laifi nane danatafi nabarku ,Dan Allah kuyafemin bazan sakeba natuba Abba”sosai take kuka tana sumbatu kusan minti biyar suna ahaka tajuyo gasu Rufaida Wanda suke kuka suma wiwi budemusu hanayenta tayi suka fada sosai suke kuka awanan ahalin Abba ma duk jaraumtar sa saida yayi hawaye„

Suna ahaka Abba yabasu umarni zama Malan Nuhu ne yayi salama amsa mishi Abba yayi gaishesu yayi hade da tura Papa yashiga ,Wanda yayi wurin Mahaifiyar shi„

Rufaida ce tadaukeshi da hawaye a fuskarta tace “Yaya Nasreen kina nufin wanan yaron dankine”tafada tana dorashi akan cinyarta kai kawai Nasreen tadaga mata aikuwa tasakashi ajikinta„

“Alhmdllah ,Alhmdlllh munayiwa Allah godiya dayasaka kukadawo cikin ahalinku ,Rayuwa dama Allah kan jarabi bayinsa matukar imaninsu ba ragage bane ,Jarabawa tanda mahimanci arayuwar musulmi tahakane zaigane yayane mu'amalarsa Takeda Ubangijinsa ,to Alhmdlllh naji dadi dawowarku gida Allah yamuku albarka ”Abba ne mai wanan maganar „

Imaran ne yace “Yaya Nasreen fadamuna miye yasameku bayan kunbarnan aina kukaje kukabarmu cikin zukumi”kalon kanin nata tayi Wanda yazama saurayi kyakyawa son kowa kin Wace tarasa„

Kuka tafashe dashi tashiga basu labarin abunda yafaru kuka sosai kanan nata keyi harda Abba saida yatausayawa yar tasa sosai ya jinjinawa hakurin ta dan har tasoma tafi mahaifiyar ta hakuri ,Mama kuwa abun yabatamata rai kuma taci aniyar yar ta bazata koma ba wahalar taisa hakan ,kozata koma kuwa sai yaji ajikinshi„


Dokar papa Abba yayi yana fadun“zomana Abokina sauran matan nawa sukuma sudawo nagansu dansai nzaba”Mama ce tadan saki fuska tana fadun “lalle makuwa Alhaji tokuwa zmugani karkarasa wacce zaka zaba fah”tafada tana dariya ,Nasreen kuwa jikin Mama ta lafe har Rufaida na tsokanar ta kodai zata fanshene itadai bata kulata ba ,ganin Rufaida tashiga Kitchen yasata shiga itama takama mata aiki„

Ta bangaren Irfan kuwa sai wajen karfe uku Yakima part dun su Abbi sabida damuwar datamasa yawa yanashiga yaga Mari na kalon TV tadora kafa daya saman daya harara yazabgamata baiko kaletaba yahaye saman dan autaninshi Nasreen na dakinsu la Maman da salama yashiga gaisheshi La Mama tayi ya'amsa yana Dube Dube tanbayar ta yayi ina papa nan ne take bashi lbarin tafiyar su hade da Mika mishi lettre yakaranta„

“aslm Abbun su dan infadama ne natafi gida nayita kiranka bansameka ba”abunda ta rubuta Kenan acikin takarda ranshune yabace yaushe Nasreen tayi idon dazata fita batare da izinin shiba baice kalaba uanunke takarda yabugo kofar Wanda yasa la maman bin kofar da kalo tana mai girgiza kanta„

Irfan kuwa baiso hakaba yaso yamaida ta dakanshi kamar yanda yaje da'ita tareda yardar iyeyen ta„

Ama bakomi zaigwadamata shi namijine tsayaye akan iyalanshi,bâ sakaran Namiji bane„

Nasreen kuwa cike take da farin ciki ganinta cikin a halinta makota duk sunzoyima sannu da zuwa dan cewa akeyi tabi namiji tabar iayeyn ta bayan namiji badan goyo bane dan diyawa kance sabida kudinqhine tabishi sabida sunsamu labarin abunda yafaru nacewa Mijin ta mazinaci ne kuma gashaida nan tunda ga y'a nan yasamu abarikancinsa Mama dakanta Idan taje wuri' biki takanji wanan kirin kirin saidai ta toshe kunan ta tabarwa Allah lamarinsa„

Ahaka har yaw da mutane kezuwa gaisheta Wanda wasu gulma ce kawai kekaisu itakuwa tagane takan tarbesu cikin Mutunci„

Yau kwanan ta biyu kuma sai yaw Irfan yazo gaisheda su Abba Wanda sam Abba baisnunamsa komi bâ saima nasiha dayamasa akan mahimanci hakuri da jarabawa godiya yayi sosai hade da rokonsu gafara Mama ma dadan sakin fuska suka gaisa kuma ta yafe ganin dare ne yasashi zaitafi suka FITA m da papa Abba ne yace tabishi„


Irfan kuwa sam bazaidauki wanan ba ganin gidan ya'isa hakan doli zata koma gidan shi dukda haryanzu baije can gidan bâ m yagani„

Nasreen ce tabude mota tashiga hade da gaidashi shuru Yamaha m yanamai cigaba dayiwa papa wasa „„


Wani bakin cikine taji yataso mata tana gaidashi yanamata shiru kusan minti goma sai biyewa papa yake daketa shirmen su da su Rufaida„

Ganin rainin hankalin yayi yawa yasaya kokarin Budé kofa zata fita jitayi anfiszogota dakarfi har saida Hijab dinta taji tana kokarin shaketa ,har idonta sunfito ta kaleshi hawaye ne zubomata„

Ganin haka Yasa Irfan sauke papa hade da Mika mishi chocolat dinshi yayi cikin gida da gudu kofar motar yarufo da karfi dab da'ita yazo hade da karema fuskar ta kalo wacce yanzu takara kwanta sabida Hutun datafara samu fatarta tafara dawowa dai dai dukdka bawai tawani yiwani Abu bane ama akwai canjin yanayi bakinta datasakawa Janbaki Wanda sai sheki uakeyi yakala ganin duk ta tsorata yasashi dan sakin murmushi harshen shi yasa ya laso lebenta kamar wani tsohon maye ,kara lasawa yayi daga karshe yahade bakinshi danata yashiga bata hot kiss Wanda yasata fara mustu mutsun kwacewa ,hawaye taji suna zubomata watodai bazai shafamata lafiya ba itakan komi zaiyi bazata komaba kusan minti uku tana takokarin kwacewa ama takasa gatsarama cizo tayi Wanda yasashi sakin ta baishirya ba „


Cikin kuka tace “kaidama ba'abunda kasani banda zalunci Allah shizaimin sakaya bazan yafe duk zalunci dakamin nasoka so mai tsanani ama daga karshe Nice nzama abun wulakantawarka Allah yasakamin kaine silar da y'a ta takeciki zaluncinkane ,kai azalumine ga iyalensa kasakeni ,kasakeni Irfan saika sakeni bazan zauna dakai ba kokai kadaine Autan maza kuwa”tafada tana zazabgamishi Mari a fuska sosai taci kwalarsa tana dukanshi a kirji„


Irfan kuwa baitaba shiga tashin hankaliba Irin wanan Nasreen dinshi ce kecewa zata rabudashi,ita take furta yasaketa dukan datakemishi sam bayajin zafinsa duk da fuskarshi mai darja da kwarjini take duka kalaman ta sunfi zafafa mishi zuciya huci mai zafi yake fitarwa kukan ta nadaga mishi hankali“Haba Nasreen dina ninefah Irfan dinki KO kinmata haba Matar Aljanna kiyi hhakuri dani nayiwa Nana alkawarin bazan saké saki kukaba kada nakasa cikawanan alkawarin kidaina kuka komi zai wuce”yafada yana kokarin rungume ta hannunshi tabuge tace “nima nasan komi zai wuce ama badakai ba kai azzalumine shiyasaka Anty Mufida takasa zama dakai duk sindatakema kuwa saika sakeni na Auri wani Wanda zaibani farin ciki ”tafada tana kara volume dun kukan ta ,Irfan kuwa yanzu yakai wani limite dabazai iya juran jin wanan zafafan kalaman nata ba hakan yasashi fita daga motar yazagayo yabude fidota yayi da karfin gaske Wanda yasata faduwa kasa cikin tafarfasa zuciya yace “wallahi dani dake mutu karaba takamin kaza kabewar kankaba bakincikin mai taushe ,Aure nidake sai dai mutuwq”yafada yana koma kujerar dreba yajata dakarfi yana barin gidan ........✍️
